func _ready() -> void:
	_init_unit_circle()
	_load_sprite_manifest()

func _init_unit_circle() -> void:
	_unit_circle_pts.resize(24)
	for i in range(24):
		var ang := float(i) * TAU / 24.0
		_unit_circle_pts[i] = Vector2(cos(ang), sin(ang))

func _load_sprite_manifest() -> void:
	var path := "res://src/data/sprite_manifest.json"
	if FileAccess.file_exists(path):
		var txt := FileAccess.get_file_as_string(path)
		var parsed = JSON.parse_string(txt)
		if parsed is Dictionary:
			sprite_manifest = parsed

## GEMINI.md Law 5.2: Native Typography Helper (`make_pixel_label_settings`) with Resource Caching
func make_pixel_label_settings(size: int = 14, color: Color = Color("#FFF4D6")) -> LabelSettings:
	var cache_key := "%d_%s" % [size, color.to_html(true)]
	if _label_settings_cache.has(cache_key):
		return _label_settings_cache[cache_key]
	var ls := LabelSettings.new()
	ls.font_size = size
	ls.font_color = color
	ls.outline_size = 2
	ls.outline_color = Color("#1A120B")
	ls.shadow_size = 1
	ls.shadow_color = Color(0.0, 0.0, 0.0, 0.6)
	ls.shadow_offset = Vector2(1.0, 1.0)
	_label_settings_cache[cache_key] = ls
	return ls

func create_pixel_label(text_str: String = "", size: int = 14, color: Color = Color("#FFF4D6"), center_align: bool = false) -> Label:
	var lbl := Label.new()
	lbl.text = text_str
	lbl.label_settings = make_pixel_label_settings(size, color)
	lbl.mouse_filter = Control.MOUSE_FILTER_IGNORE
	if center_align:
		lbl.horizontal_alignment = HORIZONTAL_ALIGNMENT_CENTER
	return lbl

## GEMINI.md Law 3.3: 5:1 Ultra-Flat Polygon Drop Shadow (`shadow_width = w * 0.55`, `shadow_height = shadow_width * 0.18`, `Color(0, 0, 0, 0.20)`)
## Optimized with polygon caching to eliminate per-frame trigonometry and array allocations.
func draw_drop_shadow(canvas: CanvasItem, entity_visual_width: float, center: Vector2 = Vector2.ZERO) -> void:
	var w_key := int(roundf(entity_visual_width * 10.0))
	var c_key := Vector2i(int(roundf(center.x)), int(roundf(center.y)))
	var cache_key := Vector3i(w_key, c_key.x, c_key.y)
	var pts: PackedVector2Array
	if _shadow_polygon_cache.has(cache_key):
		pts = _shadow_polygon_cache[cache_key]
	else:
		if _unit_circle_pts.size() != 24:
			_init_unit_circle()
		var shadow_width := entity_visual_width * 0.55
		var shadow_height := shadow_width * 0.18
		var rx := shadow_width * 0.5
		var ry := shadow_height * 0.5
		pts = PackedVector2Array()
		pts.resize(24)
		for i in range(24):
			var u := _unit_circle_pts[i]
			pts[i] = center + Vector2(u.x * rx, u.y * ry)
		_shadow_polygon_cache[cache_key] = pts
	canvas.draw_colored_polygon(pts, Color(0.0, 0.0, 0.0, 0.20))

func get_manifest_entry(sheet_key: String) -> Dictionary:
	if sprite_manifest.has(sheet_key):
		return sprite_manifest[sheet_key]
	return {"cell_width": 60, "cell_height": 60, "hframes": 4, "vframes": 1, "total_frames": 4, "regions": []}

func get_frame_region(sheet_key: String, frame_idx: int) -> Rect2:
	var entry := get_manifest_entry(sheet_key)
	var regions: Array = entry.get("regions", [])
	if regions.size() > 0:
		var clamped_idx := clampi(frame_idx, 0, regions.size() - 1)
		var r: Dictionary = regions[clamped_idx]
		return Rect2(float(r.get("x", 0)), float(r.get("y", 0)), float(r.get("w", 60)), float(r.get("h", 60)))
	var cw := float(entry.get("cell_width", 60))
	var ch := float(entry.get("cell_height", 60))
	var hf := maxi(1, int(entry.get("hframes", 4)))
	var col := clampi(frame_idx, 0, hf - 1) % hf
	var row := clampi(frame_idx, 0, hf - 1) / hf
	return Rect2(col * cw, row * ch, cw, ch)

func get_evolved_crop_id(base_island: int, building_level: int) -> String:
	var base_idx := 0
	if base_island == 2:
		base_idx = 2
	elif base_island >= 3:
		base_idx = 3
	var evo_steps := int(floor(float(maxi(1, building_level)) / 25.0))
	var final_idx := clampi(base_idx + evo_steps, 0, 3)
	return "crop_" + str(final_idx)

func get_evolved_delicacy_id(workshop_id: String, workshop_level: int) -> String:
	var base_idx := 0
	if workshop_id == "cauldron":
		base_idx = 2
	elif workshop_id == "orbis_linked":
		base_idx = 3
	var evo_steps := int(floor(float(maxi(1, workshop_level)) / 25.0))
	var final_idx := clampi(base_idx + evo_steps, 0, 3)
	return "delicacy_" + str(final_idx)

func get_crop_stage_region(crop_id: String, stage_idx: int) -> Rect2:
	var c_data: Dictionary = CROPS.get(crop_id, CROPS["crop_0"])
	var row := int(c_data.get("crop_row", 0))
	var st := clampi(stage_idx, 0, 3)
	if sprite_manifest.has("crops/crops_sheet.png"):
		var items: Array = sprite_manifest["crops/crops_sheet.png"].get("items", [])
		if row < items.size():
			var stages: Array = items[row].get("stages", [])
			if st < stages.size():
				var sr: Dictionary = stages[st]
				return Rect2(float(sr["x"]), float(sr["y"]), float(sr["w"]), float(sr["h"]))
	return Rect2(st * 40.0, row * 40.0, 40.0, 40.0)

func get_delicacy_region(delicacy_id: String) -> Rect2:
	var d_data: Dictionary = DELICACIES.get(delicacy_id, DELICACIES["delicacy_0"])
	var idx := int(d_data.get("idx", 0))
	if sprite_manifest.has("buildings/delicacies_sheet.png"):
		var items: Array = sprite_manifest["buildings/delicacies_sheet.png"].get("items", [])
		if idx < items.size():
			var reg: Dictionary = items[idx].get("region", {})
			if reg.has("w"):
				return Rect2(float(reg["x"]), float(reg["y"]), float(reg["w"]), float(reg["h"]))
	return Rect2(idx * 36.0, 0.0, 36.0, 36.0)

func calc_upgrade_cost(base_cost: int, level: int) -> int:
	var lv := maxi(1, level)
	return int(round(float(base_cost) * pow(1.15, float(lv - 1))))

func calc_milestone_value(base_value: float, level: int) -> float:
	var lv := maxi(1, level)
	var milestone_pow := int(floor(float(lv) / 25.0))
	return base_value * float(lv) * pow(2.0, float(milestone_pow))

func get_animal_trait_tier_count(level: int) -> int:
	var cnt := 0
	if level >= 10:
		cnt += 1
	if level >= 25:
		cnt += 1
	if level >= 50:
		cnt += 1
	return cnt

func get_animal_trait_tag(animal_key: String, level: int) -> String:
	var t_cfg: Dictionary = ANIMAL_TRAITS.get(animal_key, ANIMAL_TRAITS["bunny"])
	var t_name := String(t_cfg.get("name", "专精"))
	var tiers := get_animal_trait_tier_count(level)
	if tiers <= 0:
		return "Lv.10解锁[" + t_name + "]"
	var roman: String = String(["I", "II", "III"][clampi(tiers - 1, 0, 2)])
	return "★" + t_name + roman

func calc_bunny_harvest_trait_bonus(level: int) -> int:
	return get_animal_trait_tier_count(level)

func calc_logistics_speed_trait_mult(level: int) -> float:
	return 1.0 + 0.20 * float(get_animal_trait_tier_count(level))

func calc_chef_duration_trait_mult(level: int) -> float:
	var tiers := get_animal_trait_tier_count(level)
	if tiers <= 0:
		return 1.0
	return pow(0.85, float(tiers))

func calc_shiba_tip_trait_bonus(level: int) -> float:
	return 0.15 * float(get_animal_trait_tier_count(level))

func calc_work_duration(animal_key: String, level: int) -> float:
	var a_cfg: Dictionary = ANIMALS.get(animal_key, ANIMALS["bunny"])
	var base_t := float(a_cfg.get("base_time", 2.6))
	var lv := maxi(1, level)
	var raw_dur := base_t * pow(0.96, float(lv - 1))
	if animal_key == "fox" or animal_key == "penguin":
		raw_dur *= calc_chef_duration_trait_mult(lv)
	return maxf(0.45, raw_dur)

func calc_carry_capacity(animal_key: String, level: int) -> int:
	var a_cfg: Dictionary = ANIMALS.get(animal_key, ANIMALS["otter"])
	var base_cap := int(a_cfg.get("base_cap", 1))
	var lv := maxi(1, level)
	return base_cap + int(floor(float(lv) / 5.0))

func calc_move_speed(animal_key: String, level: int) -> float:
	var a_cfg: Dictionary = ANIMALS.get(animal_key, ANIMALS["otter"])
	var base_spd := float(a_cfg.get("base_speed", 72.0))
	var lv := maxi(1, level)
	var spd := base_spd * (1.0 + 0.03 * float(lv - 1))
	if animal_key == "otter" or animal_key == "toucan":
		spd *= calc_logistics_speed_trait_mult(lv)
	return spd

func calc_shiba_price_bonus(shiba_level: int) -> float:
	if shiba_level <= 0:
		return 1.0
	return 1.0 + 0.1 * float(shiba_level)

func calc_buffer_capacity(level: int) -> int:
	return DEFAULT_BUFFER_CAP + maxi(0, level - 1) * 5

func calc_item_unit_price(item_id: String, building_lv: int, stall_lv: int, shiba_lv: int) -> int:
	var shiba_mult := calc_shiba_price_bonus(shiba_lv)
	var stall_mult := 1.0 + 0.08 * float(maxi(1, stall_lv) - 1)
	if DELICACIES.has(item_id):
		var d: Dictionary = DELICACIES[item_id]
		var base_c := float(d["base_crop_val"])
		var w_mult := float(d["mult"])
		var raw_v := calc_milestone_value(base_c, maxi(1, building_lv))
		return maxi(1, int(round(raw_v * w_mult * stall_mult * shiba_mult)))
	elif CROPS.has(item_id):
		var c: Dictionary = CROPS[item_id]
		var raw_v := calc_milestone_value(float(c["base_value"]), maxi(1, building_lv))
		return maxi(1, int(round(raw_v * MULTIPLIER_RAW * stall_mult * shiba_mult)))
	return 15

## PRD v2.2 Section 3: Recruited Headcount per Island (Island 1 starts with 1 free Bunny; Max caps defined in EconomyConfig.ISLAND_RECRUIT_CAPS)
var island_recruited: Dictionary = {
	1: {"bunny": 1, "otter": 0, "fox": 0, "shiba": 0, "penguin": 0, "toucan": 0},
	2: {"bunny": 0, "otter": 0, "fox": 0, "shiba": 0, "penguin": 0, "toucan": 0},
	3: {"bunny": 0, "otter": 0, "fox": 0, "shiba": 0, "penguin": 0, "toucan": 0}
}

var save_timer: float = 0.0
var pending_offline_seconds: int = 0
var pending_offline_gold: int = 0

## v2.3 Direction 1 & 2: Dynamic Weather/Time-of-Day Cycle & Carnival Frenzy Buff
enum WeatherState { DAY = 0, DUSK = 1, NIGHT = 2, RAIN = 3 }
var current_weather: int = WeatherState.DAY
var weather_timer: float = 0.0
var frenzy_timer: float = 0.0

## v2.4-4: Pomodoro Focus Timer Companion (25min / 1500s) & Gift Box Reward
const POMODORO_DURATION: float = 1500.0
const POMODORO_REWARD_GOLD: int = 500
const POMODORO_FRENZY_DURATION: float = 30.0

var pomodoro_active: bool = false
var pomodoro_time_remaining: float = 0.0
var pomodoro_completed_count: int = 0

## v2.3 Direction 4: Mountain & Sea Collection Codex (4 Crops + 4 Delicacies -> +10% global sale price per unlocked entry)

	"crop_2": false,

	"delicacy_0": false,

	"delicacy_3": false

var amber: int = 0
var total_amber_earned: int = 0
var active_contract: Dictionary = {}

## v2.5-2: Island Landscape Decorations & Passive Buffs
var unlocked_landscapes: Dictionary = {
	"bee_hive": false,
	"wind_chime": false,

func _ready() -> void:
	var all_args := OS.get_cmdline_args()
	all_args.append_array(OS.get_cmdline_user_args())
	for arg in all_args:
		var s := String(arg)
		if s == "--self-test" or s == "--playtest":
			is_automated_test_mode = true
			active_save_path = SELFTEST_SAVE_PATH
			break
	if not is_automated_test_mode:
		load_game()
	if active_contract.is_empty():
		generate_next_contract()
	_sync_codex_from_progression()

func reset_for_playtest_state() -> void:
	gold = 120
	total_gold_earned = 120
	desktop_companion_mode = true
	amber = 0
	total_amber_earned = 0
	active_contract.clear()
	unlocked_landscapes = {
		"bee_hive": false,
		"wind_chime": false,
		"tiki_torch": false
	}
	generate_next_contract()
	unlocked_islands = {1: true, 2: false, 3: false}
	building_levels = {"soil_1": 1, "mortar": 1, "stall": 1, "soil_2": 1, "cauldron": 1, "soil_3": 1}
	animal_levels = {"bunny": 1, "otter": 0, "fox": 0, "shiba": 0, "penguin": 0, "toucan": 0}
	island_recruited = {
		1: {"bunny": 1, "otter": 0, "fox": 0, "shiba": 0, "penguin": 0, "toucan": 0},
		2: {"bunny": 0, "otter": 0, "fox": 0, "shiba": 0, "penguin": 0, "toucan": 0},
		3: {"bunny": 0, "otter": 0, "fox": 0, "shiba": 0, "penguin": 0, "toucan": 0}
	}
	raw_baskets = {
		1: {"crop_0": 0, "crop_1": 0, "crop_2": 0, "crop_3": 0},
		2: {"crop_0": 0, "crop_1": 0, "crop_2": 0, "crop_3": 0},
		3: {"crop_0": 0, "crop_1": 0, "crop_2": 0, "crop_3": 0}
	}
	workshop_inputs = {
		"mortar": {"crop_0": 0, "crop_1": 0, "crop_2": 0, "crop_3": 0},
		"cauldron": {"crop_0": 0, "crop_1": 0, "crop_2": 0, "crop_3": 0},
		"orbis_linked": {"crop_0": 0, "crop_1": 0, "crop_2": 0, "crop_3": 0}
	}
	workshop_outputs = {
		"mortar": {"delicacy_0": 0, "delicacy_1": 0, "delicacy_2": 0, "delicacy_3": 0},
		"cauldron": {"delicacy_0": 0, "delicacy_1": 0, "delicacy_2": 0, "delicacy_3": 0},
		"orbis_linked": {"delicacy_0": 0, "delicacy_1": 0, "delicacy_2": 0, "delicacy_3": 0}
	}
	stall_inventory = {
		"crop_0": 3, "crop_1": 0, "crop_2": 0, "crop_3": 0,
		"delicacy_0": 0, "delicacy_1": 0, "delicacy_2": 0, "delicacy_3": 0
	}
	unlocked_codex = {
		"crop_0": true, "crop_1": false, "crop_2": false, "crop_3": false,
		"delicacy_0": false, "delicacy_1": false, "delicacy_2": false, "delicacy_3": false
	}
	pending_offline_seconds = 0
	pending_offline_gold = 0
	last_offline_report = {}
	pomodoro_active = false
	pomodoro_time_remaining = 0.0
	pomodoro_completed_count = 0
	_sync_codex_from_progression()

func _process(delta: float) -> void:
	save_timer += delta
	if save_timer >= 15.0:
		save_timer = 0.0
		save_game()

	if frenzy_timer > 0.0:
		frenzy_timer = maxf(0.0, frenzy_timer - delta)

	if pomodoro_active:
		pomodoro_time_remaining -= delta
		if pomodoro_time_remaining <= 0.0:
			complete_pomodoro()

	weather_timer += delta
	if weather_timer >= 42.0:
		weather_timer = 0.0
		set_weather((current_weather + 1) % 4)

func set_weather(next_weather: int) -> void:
	current_weather = clampi(next_weather, 0, 3)
	if SoundManager != null and SoundManager.has_method("crossfade_to_ambient"):
		SoundManager.crossfade_to_ambient(current_weather)
	EventBus.weather_changed.emit(current_weather)

func get_weather_crop_growth_multiplier() -> float:
	var mult := 1.35 if current_weather == WeatherState.RAIN else 1.0
	if frenzy_timer > 0.0:
		mult *= 1.6
	return mult

func get_weather_vip_chance() -> float:
	var base_c := 0.36 if (current_weather == WeatherState.DUSK or current_weather == WeatherState.NIGHT) else 0.18
	if frenzy_timer > 0.0:
		base_c = maxf(base_c, 0.50)
	return base_c

func get_weather_status_text() -> String:
	if frenzy_timer > 0.0:
		return "★嘉年华加速(" + str(int(ceili(frenzy_timer))) + "s)"
	match current_weather:
		WeatherState.DAY:
			return "晴昼·常速"
		WeatherState.DUSK:
			return "黄昏·富豪2x"
		WeatherState.NIGHT:
			return "夜市·富豪2x"
		WeatherState.RAIN:
			return "微雨·农田+35%"
	return "晴昼·常速"

func trigger_carnival_frenzy(duration: float = 20.0) -> void:
	frenzy_timer = maxf(frenzy_timer, duration)
	EventBus.carnival_frenzy_started.emit(frenzy_timer)

func start_pomodoro(duration: float = POMODORO_DURATION) -> void:
	pomodoro_active = true
	pomodoro_time_remaining = maxf(1.0, duration)
	EventBus.pomodoro_state_changed.emit(true, pomodoro_time_remaining)

func stop_pomodoro() -> void:
	pomodoro_active = false
	pomodoro_time_remaining = 0.0
	EventBus.pomodoro_state_changed.emit(false, 0.0)

func toggle_pomodoro() -> bool:
	if pomodoro_active:
		stop_pomodoro()
		return false
	else:
		start_pomodoro()
		return true

func complete_pomodoro() -> Dictionary:
	pomodoro_active = false
	pomodoro_time_remaining = 0.0
	pomodoro_completed_count += 1
	add_gold(POMODORO_REWARD_GOLD)
	trigger_carnival_frenzy(POMODORO_FRENZY_DURATION)
	var report := {
		"gold": POMODORO_REWARD_GOLD,
		"frenzy_sec": POMODORO_FRENZY_DURATION,
		"completed_count": pomodoro_completed_count
	}
	EventBus.pomodoro_completed.emit(POMODORO_REWARD_GOLD, POMODORO_FRENZY_DURATION)
	save_game()
	return report

func fast_forward_pomodoro(seconds: float) -> void:
	if pomodoro_active:
		pomodoro_time_remaining -= seconds
		if pomodoro_time_remaining <= 0.0:
			complete_pomodoro()

func get_pomodoro_formatted_time() -> String:
	var total_sec := int(ceilf(pomodoro_time_remaining)) if pomodoro_active else int(POMODORO_DURATION)
	var m := total_sec / 60
	var s := total_sec % 60
	return "%02d:%02d" % [m, s]

func unlock_codex_entry(item_id: String) -> void:
	if unlocked_codex.has(item_id) and not bool(unlocked_codex[item_id]):
		unlocked_codex[item_id] = true
		EventBus.codex_unlocked.emit(item_id)

func _sync_codex_from_progression() -> void:
	unlock_codex_entry("crop_0")
	for isl in [1, 2, 3]:
		if bool(unlocked_islands.get(isl, false)):
			var slv := int(building_levels.get("soil_" + str(isl), 1))
			unlock_codex_entry(EconomyConfig.get_evolved_crop_id(isl, slv))
	if get_island_recruited_count(1, "fox") >= 1 or int(building_levels.get("mortar", 1)) > 1:
		unlock_codex_entry(EconomyConfig.get_evolved_delicacy_id("mortar", int(building_levels.get("mortar", 1))))
	if bool(unlocked_islands.get(2, false)) and (get_island_recruited_count(2, "penguin") >= 1 or int(building_levels.get("cauldron", 1)) > 1):
		unlock_codex_entry(EconomyConfig.get_evolved_delicacy_id("cauldron", int(building_levels.get("cauldron", 1))))
	if bool(unlocked_islands.get(3, false)) and (get_island_recruited_count(3, "fox") >= 1 or get_island_recruited_count(3, "penguin") >= 1 or int(building_levels.get("soil_3", 1)) > 1):
		unlock_codex_entry("delicacy_3")

func get_unlocked_codex_count() -> int:
	var cnt := 0
	for k in unlocked_codex.keys():
		if bool(unlocked_codex[k]):
			cnt += 1
	return cnt

func get_codex_price_multiplier() -> float:
	return 1.0 + 0.10 * float(get_unlocked_codex_count())

func is_animal_hired(animal_key: String) -> bool:
	return get_total_recruited(animal_key) >= 1

func get_island_recruited_count(island_idx: int, animal_key: String) -> int:
	if not island_recruited.has(island_idx):
		return 0
	return int(island_recruited[island_idx].get(animal_key, 0))

func is_island_otter_hired(island_idx: int) -> bool:
	return get_island_recruited_count(island_idx, "otter") >= 1

func get_total_recruited(animal_key: String) -> int:
	var sum := 0
	for isl in [1, 2, 3]:
		if bool(unlocked_islands.get(isl, false)):
			sum += get_island_recruited_count(isl, animal_key)
	return sum

func get_total_max_cap(animal_key: String) -> int:
	var cap := 0
	for isl in [1, 2, 3]:
		if bool(unlocked_islands.get(isl, false)):
			cap += int(EconomyConfig.ISLAND_RECRUIT_CAPS[isl].get(animal_key, 0))
	return cap

func find_next_recruit_island(animal_key: String) -> int:
	for isl in [1, 2, 3]:
		if bool(unlocked_islands.get(isl, false)):
			var cur := get_island_recruited_count(isl, animal_key)
			var mx := int(EconomyConfig.ISLAND_RECRUIT_CAPS[isl].get(animal_key, 0))
			if cur < mx:
				return isl
	return 0

## PRD v2.2 Section 3: Fixed 2x SoilPlot Slots per Island (Island 1 starts with 1 plot at Lv.1, unlocks 2nd slot at Lv.2+)
func get_unlocked_plot_count(island_idx: int) -> int:
	if not bool(unlocked_islands.get(island_idx, false)):
		return 0
	if island_idx == 1:
		var lv := int(building_levels.get("soil_1", 1))
		return 1 if lv <= 1 else 2
	return 2

func add_gold(amount: int) -> void:
	if amount == 0:
		return
	gold = maxi(0, gold + amount)
	if amount > 0:
		total_gold_earned += amount
	EventBus.gold_changed.emit(gold, amount)
	EventBus.prosperity_changed.emit(get_prosperity_score())

func spend_gold(cost: int) -> bool:
	if gold < cost:
		return false
	gold -= cost
	EventBus.gold_changed.emit(gold, -cost)
	return true

## ==================================================================
## v2.5-1: Secondary Currency (Amber 晨露琥珀) & Airship Royal Contracts
## ==================================================================
func add_amber(amount: int) -> void:
	if amount <= 0:
		return
	amber += amount
	total_amber_earned += amount
	EventBus.amber_changed.emit(amber, amount)

func spend_amber(cost: int) -> bool:
	if cost <= 0:
		return true
	if amber < cost:
		return false
	amber -= cost
	EventBus.amber_changed.emit(amber, -cost)
	return true

func get_amber() -> int:
	return amber

func generate_next_contract() -> Dictionary:
	var available_items: Array[String] = ["crop_0", "delicacy_0"]
	if bool(unlocked_codex.get("crop_1", false)) or int(building_levels.get("soil_1", 1)) >= 25:
		available_items.append("crop_1")
		available_items.append("delicacy_1")
	if bool(unlocked_islands.get(2, false)):
		available_items.append("crop_2")
		available_items.append("delicacy_2")
	if bool(unlocked_islands.get(3, false)):
		available_items.append("crop_3")
		available_items.append("delicacy_3")

	var prev_idx: int = int(active_contract.get("contract_index", 0))
	var next_idx: int = prev_idx + 1
	var chosen_item: String = available_items[next_idx % available_items.size()]
	var item_name: String = chosen_item
	if EconomyConfig.CROPS.has(chosen_item):
		item_name = String(EconomyConfig.CROPS[chosen_item].get("name", chosen_item))
	elif EconomyConfig.DELICACIES.has(chosen_item):
		item_name = String(EconomyConfig.DELICACIES[chosen_item].get("name", chosen_item))

	var req_cnt: int = 3 + (next_idx % 4)
	var rew_gold: int = 120 + req_cnt * 25
	var rew_amber: int = 1 + (next_idx % 3)

	active_contract = {
		"id": "contract_" + str(next_idx),
		"contract_index": next_idx,
		"target_item": chosen_item,
		"target_name": item_name,
		"required_count": req_cnt,
		"current_count": 0,
		"reward_gold": rew_gold,
		"reward_amber": rew_amber
	}
	EventBus.contract_updated.emit(active_contract)
	return active_contract

func progress_contract(item_id: String, amount: int = 1) -> bool:
	if active_contract.is_empty():
		generate_next_contract()
	if String(active_contract.get("target_item", "")) != item_id:
		return false
	var cur := int(active_contract.get("current_count", 0)) + amount
	var req := int(active_contract.get("required_count", 5))
	active_contract["current_count"] = cur
	EventBus.contract_progressed.emit(item_id, cur, req)
	if cur >= req:
		complete_active_contract()
		return true
	return false

func complete_active_contract() -> Dictionary:
	if active_contract.is_empty():
		return {}
	var completed := active_contract.duplicate(true)
	var rew_gold := int(completed.get("reward_gold", 150))
	var rew_amber := int(completed.get("reward_amber", 2))
	add_gold(rew_gold)
	add_amber(rew_amber)
	EventBus.contract_completed.emit(completed)
	EventBus.spawn_floating_text.emit(
		Vector2(1013.4, EconomyConfig.GROUND_Y - 40.0),
		"★皇家契约完成! +" + str(rew_gold) + "G +" + str(rew_amber) + "琥珀",
		Color("#FFE066")
	)
	generate_next_contract()
	save_game()
	return completed

func get_active_contract() -> Dictionary:
	if active_contract.is_empty():
		generate_next_contract()
	return active_contract

## ==================================================================
## v2.5-2: Island Landscape Decorations & Passive Buffs
## ==================================================================
func unlock_landscape(landscape_id: String) -> bool:
	if bool(unlocked_landscapes.get(landscape_id, false)):
		return false
	if not EconomyConfig.ISLAND_LANDSCAPES.has(landscape_id):
		return false
	var cfg: Dictionary = EconomyConfig.ISLAND_LANDSCAPES[landscape_id]
	var cost := int(cfg.get("cost_amber", 3))
	if not spend_amber(cost):
		return false
	unlocked_landscapes[landscape_id] = true
	save_game()
	var isl := int(cfg.get("island", 1))
	EventBus.landscape_unlocked.emit(isl, landscape_id)
	EventBus.spawn_floating_text.emit(
		Vector2(float(cfg.get("x_pos", 660.0)), EconomyConfig.GROUND_Y - 30.0),
		"★解锁造景【" + String(cfg.get("name", landscape_id)) + "】!",
		Color("#FFE066")
	)
	return true

func is_landscape_unlocked(landscape_id: String) -> bool:
	return bool(unlocked_landscapes.get(landscape_id, false))

func get_crop_growth_multiplier(island: int = 1) -> float:
	var mult := get_weather_crop_growth_multiplier()
	if island == 1 and bool(unlocked_landscapes.get("bee_hive", false)):
		mult *= 1.20
	return mult

func get_workshop_speed_multiplier(island: int = 2) -> float:
	var mult := 1.0
	if island == 2 and bool(unlocked_landscapes.get("wind_chime", false)):
		mult *= 1.20
	return mult

func get_customer_tip_multiplier(is_vip: bool, island: int = 3) -> float:
	var mult := EconomyConfig.VIP_TIP_MULTIPLIER if is_vip else 1.0
	if bool(unlocked_landscapes.get("tiki_torch", false)):
		mult *= 1.20
	var shiba_lv := int(animal_levels.get("shiba", 0))
	if shiba_lv >= 10:
		mult *= (1.0 + EconomyConfig.calc_shiba_tip_trait_bonus(shiba_lv))
	return mult


func get_prosperity_score() -> int:
	var score := 0
	for k in building_levels.keys():
		var req_isl := int(EconomyConfig.BUILDINGS.get(k, {}).get("island", 1))
		if bool(unlocked_islands.get(req_isl, false)):
			score += int(building_levels[k]) * 20
	for k in animal_levels.keys():
		score += get_total_recruited(k) * 35 + int(animal_levels[k]) * 25
	if bool(unlocked_islands.get(2, false)):
		score += 180
	if bool(unlocked_islands.get(3, false)):
		score += 420
	return score

## Pure Numeric Buffer Capacities (default cap = 10, +5 per building level above 1)
func get_raw_basket_max_cap(island_idx: int) -> int:
	var b_key := "soil_" + str(island_idx)
	return EconomyConfig.calc_buffer_capacity(int(building_levels.get(b_key, 1)))

func get_island_basket_total(island_idx: int) -> int:
	if not raw_baskets.has(island_idx):
		return 0
	var s := 0
	for k in raw_baskets[island_idx].keys():
		s += int(raw_baskets[island_idx][k])
	return s

func is_raw_basket_full(island_idx: int) -> bool:
	return get_island_basket_total(island_idx) >= get_raw_basket_max_cap(island_idx)

func add_to_raw_basket(island_idx: int, crop_id: String, qty: int) -> int:
	unlock_codex_entry(crop_id)
	if not raw_baskets.has(island_idx):
		raw_baskets[island_idx] = {}
	var room := maxi(0, get_raw_basket_max_cap(island_idx) - get_island_basket_total(island_idx))
	var added := mini(qty, room)
	if added > 0:
		raw_baskets[island_idx][crop_id] = int(raw_baskets[island_idx].get(crop_id, 0)) + added
		if SoundManager != null:
			SoundManager.play_pop_sfx()
		EventBus.inventory_changed.emit()
		EventBus.item_delivered.emit(crop_id, added, "raw_basket_" + str(island_idx))
	return added

func take_from_raw_basket(island_idx: int, max_qty: int) -> Dictionary:
	if not raw_baskets.has(island_idx):
		return {}
	var b: Dictionary = raw_baskets[island_idx]
	for crop_id in b.keys():
		var avail := int(b[crop_id])
		if avail > 0:
			var take := mini(avail, max_qty)
			b[crop_id] = avail - take
			EventBus.inventory_changed.emit()
			return {"item_id": String(crop_id), "qty": take}
	return {}

func get_workshop_buffer_cap(workshop_id: String) -> int:
	var b_key := "soil_3" if workshop_id == "orbis_linked" else workshop_id
	return EconomyConfig.calc_buffer_capacity(int(building_levels.get(b_key, 1)))

func get_workshop_input_total(workshop_id: String) -> int:
	if not workshop_inputs.has(workshop_id):
		return 0
	var s := 0
	for k in workshop_inputs[workshop_id].keys():
		s += int(workshop_inputs[workshop_id][k])
	return s

func is_workshop_input_full(workshop_id: String) -> bool:
	return get_workshop_input_total(workshop_id) >= get_workshop_buffer_cap(workshop_id)

func add_to_workshop_input(workshop_id: String, crop_id: String, qty: int) -> int:
	if not workshop_inputs.has(workshop_id):
		workshop_inputs[workshop_id] = {}
	var room := maxi(0, get_workshop_buffer_cap(workshop_id) - get_workshop_input_total(workshop_id))
	var added := mini(qty, room)
	if added > 0:
		workshop_inputs[workshop_id][crop_id] = int(workshop_inputs[workshop_id].get(crop_id, 0)) + added
		if SoundManager != null:
			SoundManager.play_pop_sfx()
		EventBus.inventory_changed.emit()
		EventBus.item_delivered.emit(crop_id, added, workshop_id + "_input")
	return added

func take_workshop_input_for_processing(workshop_id: String) -> String:
	if not workshop_inputs.has(workshop_id):
		return ""
	var inp: Dictionary = workshop_inputs[workshop_id]
	for crop_id in inp.keys():
		if int(inp[crop_id]) > 0:
			inp[crop_id] = int(inp[crop_id]) - 1
			EventBus.inventory_changed.emit()
			return String(crop_id)
	return ""

func get_workshop_output_total(workshop_id: String) -> int:
	if not workshop_outputs.has(workshop_id):
		return 0
	var s := 0
	for k in workshop_outputs[workshop_id].keys():
		s += int(workshop_outputs[workshop_id][k])
	return s

func is_workshop_output_full(workshop_id: String) -> bool:
	return get_workshop_output_total(workshop_id) >= get_workshop_buffer_cap(workshop_id)

func add_to_workshop_output(workshop_id: String, delicacy_id: String, qty: int) -> int:
	unlock_codex_entry(delicacy_id)
	if not workshop_outputs.has(workshop_id):
		workshop_outputs[workshop_id] = {}
	var room := maxi(0, get_workshop_buffer_cap(workshop_id) - get_workshop_output_total(workshop_id))
	var added := mini(qty, room)
	if added > 0:
		workshop_outputs[workshop_id][delicacy_id] = int(workshop_outputs[workshop_id].get(delicacy_id, 0)) + added
		if SoundManager != null:
			SoundManager.play_pop_sfx()
		EventBus.inventory_changed.emit()
		EventBus.item_delivered.emit(delicacy_id, added, workshop_id + "_output")
	return added

func take_from_workshop_output(workshop_id: String, max_qty: int) -> Dictionary:
	if not workshop_outputs.has(workshop_id):
		return {}
	var out_dict: Dictionary = workshop_outputs[workshop_id]
	for del_id in out_dict.keys():
		var avail := int(out_dict[del_id])
		if avail > 0:
			var take := mini(avail, max_qty)
			out_dict[String(del_id)] = avail - take
			EventBus.inventory_changed.emit()
			return {"item_id": String(del_id), "qty": take}
	return {}

func get_stall_max_capacity() -> int:
	return EconomyConfig.calc_buffer_capacity(int(building_levels.get("stall", 1)))

func get_total_stall_count() -> int:
	var total := 0
	for k in stall_inventory.keys():
		total += int(stall_inventory[k])
	return total

func is_stall_full() -> bool:
	return get_total_stall_count() >= get_stall_max_capacity()

func add_to_stall(item_id: String, qty: int) -> int:
	unlock_codex_entry(item_id)
	var room := maxi(0, get_stall_max_capacity() - get_total_stall_count())
	var added := mini(qty, room)
	if added > 0:
		stall_inventory[item_id] = int(stall_inventory.get(item_id, 0)) + added
		if SoundManager != null:
			SoundManager.play_pop_sfx()
		EventBus.inventory_changed.emit()
		EventBus.item_delivered.emit(item_id, added, "market_stall")
	return added

func try_purchase_from_stall(preferred_id: String, qty: int, world_pos: Vector2, is_vip: bool = false) -> int:
	var target_id := preferred_id
	if int(stall_inventory.get(target_id, 0)) <= 0:
		for k in ["delicacy_3", "delicacy_2", "delicacy_1", "delicacy_0", "crop_3", "crop_2", "crop_1", "crop_0"]:
			if int(stall_inventory.get(k, 0)) > 0:
				target_id = k
				break
	var avail := int(stall_inventory.get(target_id, 0))
	if avail <= 0:
		return 0
	var bought := mini(avail, maxi(1, qty))
	stall_inventory[target_id] = avail - bought
	unlock_codex_entry(target_id)

	var b_lv := int(building_levels.get("soil_1", 1))
	if target_id == "crop_2":
		b_lv = int(building_levels.get("soil_2", 1))
	elif target_id == "crop_3":
		b_lv = int(building_levels.get("soil_3", 1))
	elif target_id == "delicacy_0" or target_id == "delicacy_1":
		b_lv = int(building_levels.get("mortar", 1))
	elif target_id == "delicacy_2":
		b_lv = int(building_levels.get("cauldron", 1))
	elif target_id == "delicacy_3":
		b_lv = maxi(int(building_levels.get("cauldron", 1)), int(building_levels.get("soil_3", 1)))

	var shiba_lv := int(animal_levels.get("shiba", 0)) if is_animal_hired("shiba") else 0
	var unit_price := EconomyConfig.calc_item_unit_price(
		target_id,
		b_lv,
		int(building_levels.get("stall", 1)),
		shiba_lv
	)
	var total_earned := int(round(float(unit_price * bought) * get_codex_price_multiplier()))
	var tip_mult := get_customer_tip_multiplier(is_vip, 3)
	total_earned = int(round(float(total_earned) * tip_mult))

	add_gold(total_earned)
	progress_contract(target_id, bought)
	if SoundManager != null:
		SoundManager.play_coin_sfx()
	EventBus.inventory_changed.emit()
	EventBus.customer_paid.emit(target_id, bought, total_earned, world_pos)
	return total_earned

func upgrade_building(b_key: String) -> bool:
	if not EconomyConfig.BUILDINGS.has(b_key):
		return false
	var req_island := int(EconomyConfig.BUILDINGS[b_key].get("island", 1))
	if not bool(unlocked_islands.get(req_island, false)):
		return false
	var cur_lv := int(building_levels.get(b_key, 1))
	var base_c := int(EconomyConfig.BUILDINGS[b_key]["base_cost"])
	var cost := EconomyConfig.calc_upgrade_cost(base_c, cur_lv)
	if not spend_gold(cost):
		return false
	building_levels[b_key] = cur_lv + 1
	_sync_codex_from_progression()
	EventBus.upgrade_purchased.emit("building", b_key, cur_lv + 1)
	EventBus.prosperity_changed.emit(get_prosperity_score())
	save_game()
	return true

## PRD v2.2 Section 3: Recruit-to-Promote Mechanism (`招满转进修机制`):
## - If any unlocked island has room below `ISLAND_RECRUIT_CAPS`, recruits +1 animal worker onto that island.
## - Once all unlocked islands reach Max Cap for `a_key`, upgrades `animal_levels[a_key] += 1` (Team Promotion Lv.+1)!
func upgrade_animal(a_key: String) -> bool:
	if not EconomyConfig.ANIMALS.has(a_key):
		return false
	var next_isl := find_next_recruit_island(a_key)
	var cur_lv := int(animal_levels.get(a_key, 0))
	var total_rec := get_total_recruited(a_key)
	var base_c := int(EconomyConfig.ANIMALS[a_key]["base_cost"])
	var cost_step := total_rec + maxi(1, cur_lv)
	var cost := EconomyConfig.calc_upgrade_cost(base_c, cost_step)
	if not spend_gold(cost):
		return false

	if next_isl > 0:
		island_recruited[next_isl][a_key] = get_island_recruited_count(next_isl, a_key) + 1
		if cur_lv <= 0:
			animal_levels[a_key] = 1
		if SoundManager != null:
			SoundManager.play_upgrade_sfx()
		EventBus.upgrade_purchased.emit("recruit", a_key, get_total_recruited(a_key))
	else:
		animal_levels[a_key] = maxi(1, cur_lv) + 1
		if SoundManager != null:
			if animal_levels[a_key] % 25 == 0:
				SoundManager.play_milestone_sfx()
			else:
				SoundManager.play_upgrade_sfx()
		EventBus.upgrade_purchased.emit("animal", a_key, animal_levels[a_key])

	_sync_codex_from_progression()
	EventBus.prosperity_changed.emit(get_prosperity_score())
	save_game()
	return true

func unlock_island(island_idx: int) -> bool:
	if bool(unlocked_islands.get(island_idx, false)):
		return false
	var exp_key := "island_" + str(island_idx)
	var cost := int(EconomyConfig.EXPANSIONS.get(exp_key, {"cost": 180})["cost"])
	if not spend_gold(cost):
		return false
	unlocked_islands[island_idx] = true
	_sync_codex_from_progression()
	if SoundManager != null:
		SoundManager.play_milestone_sfx()
	EventBus.island_unlocked.emit(island_idx)
	EventBus.prosperity_changed.emit(get_prosperity_score())
	save_game()
	return true

func estimate_idle_gold_per_sec() -> float:
	var base_rate := 1.8
	for k in building_levels.keys():
		var req_isl := int(EconomyConfig.BUILDINGS.get(k, {}).get("island", 1))
		if bool(unlocked_islands.get(req_isl, false)):
			base_rate += float(building_levels[k]) * 1.1
	for k in animal_levels.keys():
		base_rate += float(get_total_recruited(k)) * 1.6
	return base_rate * EconomyConfig.calc_shiba_price_bonus(int(animal_levels.get("shiba", 0))) * get_codex_price_multiplier()

func get_backup_save_path() -> String:
	return active_save_path + ".bak"

func _parse_valid_save_dict(path: String) -> Dictionary:
	if not FileAccess.file_exists(path):
		return {}
	var txt := FileAccess.get_file_as_string(path).strip_edges()
	if txt.length() < 2 or not txt.begins_with("{") or not txt.ends_with("}"):
		return {}
	var parsed = JSON.parse_string(txt)
	if not (parsed is Dictionary):
		return {}
	if int(parsed.get("version", 0)) != SAVE_VERSION:
		return {}
	return parsed

func calculate_and_apply_offline_earnings(elapsed_sec: int) -> Dictionary:
	var clamped_sec := clampi(elapsed_sec, 0, 86400)
	if clamped_sec < 60:
		return {"seconds": clamped_sec, "gold": 0, "items": 0}
	var gold_earned := maxi(8, int(round(float(clamped_sec) * estimate_idle_gold_per_sec() * 0.45)))
	var total_workers := 0
	for k in animal_levels.keys():
		total_workers += get_total_recruited(String(k))
	var ws_lv := int(building_levels.get("mortar", 1)) + int(building_levels.get("cauldron", 1))
	var items_est := maxi(1, int(round(float(clamped_sec) / 10.0 * (float(maxi(1, total_workers)) * 0.6 + float(ws_lv) * 0.2))))
	pending_offline_seconds = clamped_sec
	pending_offline_gold = gold_earned
	last_offline_report = {
		"seconds": clamped_sec,
		"gold": gold_earned,
		"items": items_est
	}
	add_gold(gold_earned)
	EventBus.offline_earnings_ready.emit(clamped_sec, gold_earned)
	return last_offline_report

func save_game() -> void:
	var prev_valid := _parse_valid_save_dict(active_save_path)
	if not prev_valid.is_empty():
		var prev_txt := FileAccess.get_file_as_string(active_save_path)
		var f_bak := FileAccess.open(get_backup_save_path(), FileAccess.WRITE)
		if f_bak != null:
			f_bak.store_string(prev_txt)
			f_bak.close()

	var data := {
		"version": SAVE_VERSION,
		"timestamp": int(Time.get_unix_time_from_system()),
		"gold": gold,
		"total_gold_earned": total_gold_earned,
		"desktop_companion_mode": desktop_companion_mode,
		"unlocked_islands": {
			"1": bool(unlocked_islands.get(1, true)),
			"2": bool(unlocked_islands.get(2, false)),
			"3": bool(unlocked_islands.get(3, false))
		},
		"building_levels": building_levels,
		"animal_levels": animal_levels,
		"island_recruited": {
			"1": island_recruited[1],
			"2": island_recruited[2],
			"3": island_recruited[3]
		},
		"stall_inventory": stall_inventory,
		"unlocked_codex": unlocked_codex,
		"pomodoro_completed_count": pomodoro_completed_count,
		"amber": amber,
		"total_amber_earned": total_amber_earned,
		"active_contract": active_contract,
		"unlocked_landscapes": unlocked_landscapes
	}
	var json_str := JSON.stringify(data, "\t")
	var f := FileAccess.open(active_save_path, FileAccess.WRITE)
	if f != null:
		f.store_string(json_str)
		f.close()
	if not FileAccess.file_exists(get_backup_save_path()):
		var f_init_bak := FileAccess.open(get_backup_save_path(), FileAccess.WRITE)
		if f_init_bak != null:
			f_init_bak.store_string(json_str)
			f_init_bak.close()

func load_game() -> void:
	var parsed := _parse_valid_save_dict(active_save_path)
	if parsed.is_empty():
		parsed = _parse_valid_save_dict(get_backup_save_path())
		if not parsed.is_empty():
			var bak_txt := FileAccess.get_file_as_string(get_backup_save_path())
			var f_repair := FileAccess.open(active_save_path, FileAccess.WRITE)
			if f_repair != null:
				f_repair.store_string(bak_txt)
				f_repair.close()
	if parsed.is_empty():
		return
	gold = int(parsed.get("gold", gold))
	total_gold_earned = int(parsed.get("total_gold_earned", total_gold_earned))
	desktop_companion_mode = bool(parsed.get("desktop_companion_mode", true))
	if parsed.has("unlocked_islands") and parsed["unlocked_islands"] is Dictionary:
		var ui: Dictionary = parsed["unlocked_islands"]
		unlocked_islands[1] = bool(ui.get("1", true))
		unlocked_islands[2] = bool(ui.get("2", false))
		unlocked_islands[3] = bool(ui.get("3", false))
	if parsed.has("building_levels") and parsed["building_levels"] is Dictionary:
		for k in parsed["building_levels"].keys():
			building_levels[String(k)] = int(parsed["building_levels"][k])
	if parsed.has("animal_levels") and parsed["animal_levels"] is Dictionary:
		for k in parsed["animal_levels"].keys():
			animal_levels[String(k)] = int(parsed["animal_levels"][k])
	if parsed.has("island_recruited") and parsed["island_recruited"] is Dictionary:
		var ir: Dictionary = parsed["island_recruited"]
		for isl_str in ["1", "2", "3"]:
			if ir.has(isl_str) and ir[isl_str] is Dictionary:
				var isl_i := int(isl_str)
				for ak in ir[isl_str].keys():
					island_recruited[isl_i][String(ak)] = int(ir[isl_str][ak])
	if parsed.has("stall_inventory") and parsed["stall_inventory"] is Dictionary:
		for k in parsed["stall_inventory"].keys():
			stall_inventory[String(k)] = int(parsed["stall_inventory"][k])
	if parsed.has("unlocked_codex") and parsed["unlocked_codex"] is Dictionary:
		for ck in parsed["unlocked_codex"].keys():
			if unlocked_codex.has(String(ck)):
				unlocked_codex[String(ck)] = bool(parsed["unlocked_codex"][ck])
	pomodoro_completed_count = int(parsed.get("pomodoro_completed_count", 0))
	amber = int(parsed.get("amber", 0))
	total_amber_earned = int(parsed.get("total_amber_earned", amber))
	if parsed.has("active_contract") and parsed["active_contract"] is Dictionary:
		active_contract = parsed["active_contract"]
	else:
		generate_next_contract()
	if parsed.has("unlocked_landscapes") and parsed["unlocked_landscapes"] is Dictionary:
		var ul: Dictionary = parsed["unlocked_landscapes"]
		for lk in unlocked_landscapes.keys():
			unlocked_landscapes[lk] = bool(ul.get(lk, false))

	var last_ts := int(parsed.get("timestamp", 0))
	var now_ts := int(Time.get_unix_time_from_system())
	var elapsed := clampi(now_ts - last_ts, 0, 86400)
	if elapsed >= 60:
		calculate_and_apply_offline_earnings(elapsed)

=== FILE: D:/project/maple-isle-idle/src/autoload/SelfTestRunner.gd ===
extends Node
## Real-Engine 20x Fast-Forward Self-Test & Dual-State Viewport Screenshot Exporter (`src/autoload/SelfTestRunner.gd`)
## Activated when command-line args contain `--self-test` (via `./run_godot.ps1 --self-test`).
## Runs `MainWorld` for 180 frames at `Engine.time_scale = 20.0` (~60s simulated idle production),
## verifies all 4 Hard Assertions, exports TWO distinct real engine screenshots:
##   1) Collapsed Drawer State -> `res://preview_live_godot.png` & `res://preview_engine_1920x360.png`
##   2) Expanded Drawer State (`HUD.open_drawer(0)`) -> `res://preview_drawer_open_1920x360.png`
## Asserts that the two captured images have distinct byte hashes before exiting with code 0!

var is_self_test: bool = false
var frame_counter: int = 0
var finishing: bool = false

var harvest_count: int = 0
var items_delivered_count: int = 0
var customer_paid_count: int = 0

var spec_rules: Dictionary = {
	"max_sprite_frame_px": 180.0,
	"max_building_overlap_pct": 15.0,
	"min_building_gap_px": 28.0,
	"min_workshop_dock_gap_px": 40.0,
	"customer_min_x": 1035.0,
	"customer_locked_isl3_max_x": 1080.0,
	"min_60s_worker_move_px": 50.0,
	"min_60s_harvest_count": 15.0,
	"min_60s_items_delivered": 30.0,
	"min_60s_customer_checkouts": 1.0
}
var pending_prd_tasks: Array[String] = []
var completed_prd_tasks_count: int = 0

func _load_spec_from_prd() -> void:
	pending_prd_tasks.clear()
	completed_prd_tasks_count = 0
	var prd_path := "res://PRD.md"
	if not FileAccess.file_exists(prd_path):
		return
	var text := FileAccess.get_file_as_string(prd_path)
	var in_assertions := false
	var in_section_11 := false
	for raw_line in text.split("\n"):
		var line := raw_line.strip_edges()
		if line.begins_with("## 11."):
			in_section_11 = true
		elif line.begins_with("## ") and in_section_11 and not line.begins_with("## 11."):
			in_section_11 = false
		if line == "[SPEC-ASSERTIONS]":
			in_assertions = true
			continue
		elif line == "[/SPEC-ASSERTIONS]":
			in_assertions = false
			continue
		if in_assertions:
			if line == "" or line.begins_with("#"):
				continue
			var eq := line.find("=")
			if eq > 0:
				var k := line.substr(0, eq).strip_edges()
				var v := line.substr(eq + 1).strip_edges()
				if v.is_valid_float():
					spec_rules[k] = v.to_float()
		elif in_section_11:
			if line.begins_with("- [ ] "):
				pending_prd_tasks.append(line.substr(6).strip_edges())
			elif line.begins_with("- [x] ") or line.begins_with("- [X] "):
				completed_prd_tasks_count += 1

func _ready() -> void:
	var all_args := OS.get_cmdline_args()
	all_args.append_array(OS.get_cmdline_user_args())
	for arg in all_args:
		var s := String(arg)
		if s == "--self-test" or s == "--playtest":
			is_self_test = true
			break

	if not is_self_test:
		set_process(false)
		return

	print("==================================================================")
	print("[SelfTestRunner] Activated `--self-test` / `--playtest`: Engine.time_scale = 20.0 (180 frames / ~60s simulation)")
	_load_spec_from_prd()
	print("[PASS] [断言0-PRD文档同步] 已动态读取 `PRD.md` 巡检契约 (%d 项动态阈值 | 已完成 Backlog: %d 项 | 待开发 Backlog: %d 项)" % [
		spec_rules.size(), completed_prd_tasks_count, pending_prd_tasks.size()
	])
	for i in range(mini(2, pending_prd_tasks.size())):
		print("       [NEXT-DEV-TARGET #%d] %s" % [i + 1, pending_prd_tasks[i]])
	print("==================================================================")
	Engine.time_scale = 20.0

	# Start from deterministic clean playtest state (never overwrites player's real `user://savegame.json`)
	if GameManager != null and GameManager.has_method("reset_for_playtest_state"):
		GameManager.reset_for_playtest_state()

	if EventBus != null:
		EventBus.item_produced.connect(_on_item_produced)
		EventBus.item_delivered.connect(_on_item_delivered)
		EventBus.customer_paid.connect(_on_customer_paid)

func _on_item_produced(_island_idx: int, _item_id: String, qty: int) -> void:
	harvest_count += maxi(1, qty)

func _on_item_delivered(_item_id: String, amount: int, _target_name: String) -> void:
	items_delivered_count += maxi(1, amount)

func _on_customer_paid(_recipe_id: String, _qty: int, _total_gold: int, _world_pos: Vector2) -> void:
	customer_paid_count += 1

func _fail_and_quit(assertion_tag: String, reason: String) -> void:
	var msg := "%s FAILED: %s" % [assertion_tag, reason]
	push_error(msg)
	printerr(msg)
	get_tree().quit(1)

func _process(_delta: float) -> void:
	if not is_self_test or finishing:
		return

	frame_counter += 1
	if frame_counter == 5:
		# Verify locked-island building upgrade guard and crop evolution mapping during playtest
		if GameManager.upgrade_building("cauldron") or GameManager.upgrade_building("soil_2") or GameManager.upgrade_building("soil_3"):
			_fail_and_quit("[断言3-产线活体闭环]", "Locked island building was allowed to upgrade before island unlock!")
			return
		if EconomyConfig.get_evolved_crop_id(2, 1) != "crop_2" or EconomyConfig.get_evolved_crop_id(3, 1) != "crop_3":
			_fail_and_quit("[断言3-产线活体闭环]", "Island 2/3 base crop mapping error: isl2=%s, isl3=%s" % [
				EconomyConfig.get_evolved_crop_id(2, 1), EconomyConfig.get_evolved_crop_id(3, 1)
			])
			return
	elif frame_counter == 10:
		# Spawn an active customer early and simulate player recruiting Otter + Fox + upgrading Island 1 buildings
		var world := get_tree().current_scene as MainWorld
		if world != null and world.customers.size() == 0:
			world._spawn_customer(false)
		GameManager.add_gold(320)
		GameManager.upgrade_animal("otter")
		GameManager.upgrade_animal("fox")
		GameManager.upgrade_building("soil_1")
		GameManager.upgrade_building("mortar")
		GameManager.upgrade_building("stall")
	elif frame_counter == 70:
		# Simulate HUD tab cycling & weather interaction during autonomous playtest
		var world := get_tree().current_scene as MainWorld
		if world != null and world.hud != null:
			for tab_i in [0, 1, 2, 3]:
				world.hud.open_drawer(tab_i)
			world.hud.close_drawer(false)
		GameManager.set_weather(GameManager.WeatherState.DUSK)
	elif frame_counter == 150:
		# Trigger animal pet boost on Bunny so `HeartIcon` (`emote_heart.png`) and +50% haste are verified
		var world := get_tree().current_scene as MainWorld
		if world != null and world.workers.size() > 0 and is_instance_valid(world.workers[0]):
			world.workers[0].trigger_pet_boost()

	if frame_counter >= 180:
		finishing = true
		_run_all_four_assertions_and_export()

func _run_all_four_assertions_and_export() -> void:
	var world := get_tree().current_scene as MainWorld
	if world == null:
		_fail_and_quit("[SelfTestRunner]", "MainWorld is not the active current_scene!")
		return

	# ------------------------------------------------------------------
	# [断言1-切帧防呆]: Check single-frame rendered dimensions of all animals, customers, and crops
	# ------------------------------------------------------------------
	var max_frame_px := float(spec_rules.get("max_sprite_frame_px", 180.0))
	var min_cust_x := float(spec_rules.get("customer_min_x", 1035.0))
	var max_cust_locked_x := float(spec_rules.get("customer_locked_isl3_max_x", 1080.0))
	var checked_sprites := 0
	for w in world.workers:
		if not is_instance_valid(w) or w.body_sprite == null or w.body_sprite.texture == null:
			continue
		var sp: Sprite2D = w.body_sprite
		var fw := (sp.region_rect.size.x if sp.region_enabled else float(sp.texture.get_width()) / float(maxi(1, sp.hframes))) * absf(sp.scale.x)
		var fh := (sp.region_rect.size.y if sp.region_enabled else float(sp.texture.get_height()) / float(maxi(1, sp.vframes))) * absf(sp.scale.y)
		if (not sp.region_enabled and sp.hframes <= 1) or fw > max_frame_px or fh > max_frame_px:
			_fail_and_quit("[断言1-切帧防呆]", "AnimalWorker `%s` has unsliced or oversized frame: %.1fx%.1f (hframes=%d, region=%s)" % [w.animal_key, fw, fh, sp.hframes, str(sp.region_enabled)])
			return
		if absf(w.rotation) > 0.001 or absf(sp.rotation) > 0.001 or sp.frame < 0 or sp.frame > 2:
			_fail_and_quit("[断言1-切帧防呆]", "AnimalWorker `%s` has invalid rotation/pose (rot=%.3f, sp_rot=%.3f, frame=%d)" % [w.animal_key, w.rotation, sp.rotation, sp.frame])
			return
		if w.carry_icon != null and w.carry_icon.visible:
			if w.carry_icon.position.y < -32.0 or absf(w.carry_icon.position.x) < 10.0 or w.carry_front_grip == null or not w.carry_front_grip.visible:
				_fail_and_quit("[断言1-切帧防呆]", "AnimalWorker `%s` carry rig is not properly attached to front hands/basket (pos=%s)!" % [w.animal_key, str(w.carry_icon.position)])
				return
		checked_sprites += 1

	for c in world.customers:
		if not is_instance_valid(c) or c.body_sprite == null or c.body_sprite.texture == null:
			continue
		var sp: Sprite2D = c.body_sprite
		var fw := (sp.region_rect.size.x if sp.region_enabled else float(sp.texture.get_width()) / float(maxi(1, sp.hframes))) * absf(sp.scale.x)
		var fh := (sp.region_rect.size.y if sp.region_enabled else float(sp.texture.get_height()) / float(maxi(1, sp.vframes))) * absf(sp.scale.y)
		if (not sp.region_enabled and sp.hframes <= 1) or fw > max_frame_px or fh > max_frame_px:
			_fail_and_quit("[断言1-切帧防呆]", "CustomerNPC `%s` has unsliced or oversized frame: %.1fx%.1f (hframes=%d)" % [c.sheet_key, fw, fh, sp.hframes])
			return
		if absf(c.rotation) > 0.001 or absf(sp.rotation) > 0.001:
			_fail_and_quit("[断言1-切帧防呆]", "CustomerNPC `%s` has non-zero rotation (rot=%.3f, sp_rot=%.3f)" % [c.sheet_key, c.rotation, sp.rotation])
			return
		if c.position.x < min_cust_x:
			_fail_and_quit("[断言3-产线活体闭环]", "CustomerNPC `%s` walked into worker production zone left of MarketStall (x=%.1f < %.1f)!" % [c.sheet_key, c.position.x, min_cust_x])
			return
		if not bool(GameManager.unlocked_islands.get(3, false)) and c.position.x > max_cust_locked_x:
			_fail_and_quit("[断言3-产线活体闭环]", "CustomerNPC `%s` walked into Lotus Pond or locked Island 3 (x=%.1f > %.1f)!" % [c.sheet_key, c.position.x, max_cust_locked_x])
			return
		checked_sprites += 1

	for p in world.soil_plots:
		if not is_instance_valid(p) or p.crop_sprite == null or p.crop_sprite.texture == null:
			continue
		var sp: Sprite2D = p.crop_sprite
		var fw := (sp.region_rect.size.x if sp.region_enabled else float(sp.texture.get_width()) / float(maxi(1, sp.hframes))) * absf(sp.scale.x)
		var fh := (sp.region_rect.size.y if sp.region_enabled else float(sp.texture.get_height()) / float(maxi(1, sp.vframes))) * absf(sp.scale.y)
		if (not sp.region_enabled and (sp.hframes <= 1 or sp.vframes <= 1)) or fw > 180.0 or fh > 180.0:
			_fail_and_quit("[断言1-切帧防呆]", "SoilPlot crop_sprite has unsliced or oversized frame: %.1fx%.1f" % [fw, fh])
			return
		checked_sprites += 1

	print("[PASS] [断言1-切帧防呆] 通过: 已校验 %d 个动物/游客/作物 Sprite2D 节点，单帧尺寸全部 <= 60x60px (100%% 固定网格切片，无整图挤压)!" % checked_sprites)

	# ------------------------------------------------------------------
	# [断言2-建筑防叠罗汉]: Check global screen rects of main buildings on each island (horizontal overlap <= 15%)
	# ------------------------------------------------------------------
	var max_ov_limit := float(spec_rules.get("max_building_overlap_pct", 15.0)) / 100.0
	var min_gap_limit := float(spec_rules.get("min_building_gap_px", 28.0))
	var max_overlap_seen := 0.0
	var min_gap_seen := 9999.0
	var buildings_by_island: Dictionary = world.get_island_main_building_rects()
	for isl_idx in buildings_by_island.keys():
		var b_list: Array = buildings_by_island[isl_idx]
		for i in range(b_list.size()):
			for j in range(i + 1, b_list.size()):
				var ba: Dictionary = b_list[i]
				var bb: Dictionary = b_list[j]
				var ra: Rect2 = ba["rect"]
				var rb: Rect2 = bb["rect"]
				var inter_w := maxf(0.0, minf(ra.end.x, rb.end.x) - maxf(ra.position.x, rb.position.x))
				var overlap_ratio := inter_w / maxf(1.0, minf(ra.size.x, rb.size.x))
				max_overlap_seen = maxf(max_overlap_seen, overlap_ratio)
				if j == i + 1:
					var gap := rb.position.x - ra.end.x
					min_gap_seen = minf(min_gap_seen, gap)
				if overlap_ratio > max_ov_limit:
					_fail_and_quit("[断言2-建筑防叠罗汉]", "Island %d building overlap %.1f%% > %.1f%% between `%s` (%s) and `%s` (%s)!" % [
						isl_idx, overlap_ratio * 100.0, max_ov_limit * 100.0, String(ba["name"]), str(ra), String(bb["name"]), str(rb)
					])
					return
	if min_gap_seen < min_gap_limit - 0.5:
		_fail_and_quit("[断言2-建筑防叠罗汉]", "Building minimum edge gap %.1fpx < %.1fpx (from PRD.md)!" % [min_gap_seen, min_gap_limit])
		return

	print("[PASS] [断言2-建筑防叠罗汉] 通过: 三岛全部 12 座主建筑水平最大重叠率 = %.1f%% (<= %.1f%%)，最小边缘净留白 = %.1fpx (>= %.1fpx)!" % [
		max_overlap_seen * 100.0, max_ov_limit * 100.0, min_gap_seen, min_gap_limit
	])

	# ------------------------------------------------------------------
	# [断言3-产线活体闭环]: Verify harvests > 0, animal movement > 50px, monotonic pathing, and items entered workshop or market stall
	# ------------------------------------------------------------------
	var total_bunny_dist := 0.0
	var total_all_workers_dist := 0.0
	for w in world.workers:
		if not is_instance_valid(w):
			continue
		if absf(w.position.y - EconomyConfig.GROUND_Y) > 0.1:
			_fail_and_quit("[断言3-产线活体闭环]", "AnimalWorker `%s` drifted off GROUND_Y (y=%.2f)!" % [w.animal_key, w.position.y])
			return
		if not w.is_hired() and w.nav_path.size() > 0:
			_fail_and_quit("[断言3-产线活体闭环]", "Unhired AnimalWorker `%s` (island %d slot %d) has active nav_path!" % [w.animal_key, w.home_island, w.slot_index])
			return
		if w.visible:
			total_all_workers_dist += w.total_distance_moved
			if w.animal_key == "bunny":
				total_bunny_dist += w.total_distance_moved

	if world.waypoint_graph != null:
		var test_path_a := world.waypoint_graph.compute_path(Vector2(698.8, EconomyConfig.GROUND_Y), Vector2(698.8, EconomyConfig.GROUND_Y))
		if test_path_a.size() != 1 or absf(test_path_a[0].x - 698.8) > 0.1:
			_fail_and_quit("[断言3-产线活体闭环]", "WaypointGraph same-dock path caused jitter: %s" % [str(test_path_a)])
			return
		var test_path_b := world.waypoint_graph.compute_path(Vector2(722.8, EconomyConfig.GROUND_Y), Vector2(1005.4, EconomyConfig.GROUND_Y))
		var prev_x := 722.8
		for pt in test_path_b:
			if pt.x < prev_x - 0.1:
				_fail_and_quit("[断言3-产线活体闭环]", "WaypointGraph path backtracked from %.1f to %.1f: %s" % [prev_x, pt.x, str(test_path_b)])
				return
			prev_x = pt.x
		var min_ws_gap := float(spec_rules.get("min_workshop_dock_gap_px", 40.0))
		var mortar_chef_dock: Vector2 = world.waypoint_graph.get_dock_pos("mortar_chef")
		var mortar_out_dock: Vector2 = world.waypoint_graph.get_dock_pos("mortar_output")
		var cauldron_chef_dock: Vector2 = world.waypoint_graph.get_dock_pos("cauldron_chef")
		var cauldron_out_dock: Vector2 = world.waypoint_graph.get_dock_pos("cauldron_output")
		if absf(mortar_chef_dock.x - mortar_out_dock.x) < min_ws_gap or absf(cauldron_chef_dock.x - cauldron_out_dock.x) < min_ws_gap:
			_fail_and_quit("[断言3-产线活体闭环]", "Workshop chef and logistics docks are too close (mortar=%.1fpx, cauldron=%.1fpx < %.1fpx)!" % [
				absf(mortar_chef_dock.x - mortar_out_dock.x), absf(cauldron_chef_dock.x - cauldron_out_dock.x), min_ws_gap
			])
			return

	if world.camera_controller == null:
		_fail_and_quit("[断言3-产线活体闭环]", "MainWorld.camera_controller is null!")
		return
	world.camera_controller.reset_camera_view(true)
	world.camera_controller.apply_wheel_zoom(1.0, 1.0, Vector2(710.0, 150.0))
	var pinned_local_x := (710.0 - world.camera_controller.target_root_pos.x) / world.camera_controller.target_zoom
	if world.camera_controller.target_zoom <= 1.05 or absf(pinned_local_x - 710.0) > 1.0:
		_fail_and_quit("[断言3-产线活体闭环]", "CameraController zoom-to-cursor failed: zoom=%.3f, pinned_local_x=%.1f (expected 710.0)" % [
			world.camera_controller.target_zoom, pinned_local_x
		])
		return
	world.camera_controller.reset_camera_view(true)

	for p in world.soil_plots:
		if is_instance_valid(p):
			harvest_count = maxi(harvest_count, p.harvest_count)

	var min_harvests := int(spec_rules.get("min_60s_harvest_count", 15.0))
	var min_move_px := float(spec_rules.get("min_60s_worker_move_px", 50.0))
	var min_deliveries := int(spec_rules.get("min_60s_items_delivered", 30.0))
	var min_checkouts := int(spec_rules.get("min_60s_customer_checkouts", 1.0))

	if harvest_count < min_harvests:
		_fail_and_quit("[断言3-产线活体闭环]", "60 秒模拟内农田收获次数 %d < %d (PRD.md 阈值)!" % [harvest_count, min_harvests])
		return
	if total_all_workers_dist <= min_move_px:
		_fail_and_quit("[断言3-产线活体闭环]", "状态机死锁: 60 秒模拟内动物累计移动距离仅为 %.1fpx (<= %.1fpx)!" % [total_all_workers_dist, min_move_px])
		return
	if items_delivered_count < min_deliveries:
		_fail_and_quit("[断言3-产线活体闭环]", "60 秒模拟内入库/上架流转件数 %d < %d (PRD.md 阈值)!" % [items_delivered_count, min_deliveries])
		return
	if customer_paid_count < min_checkouts:
		_fail_and_quit("[断言3-产线活体闭环]", "60 秒模拟内顾客结账次数 %d < %d (PRD.md 阈值)!" % [customer_paid_count, min_checkouts])
		return
	if int(GameManager.building_levels.get("soil_1", 1)) < 25 and bool(GameManager.unlocked_codex.get("crop_1", false)):
		_fail_and_quit("[断言3-产线活体闭环]", "Codex `crop_1` unlocked prematurely before soil_1 reached Lv.25!")
		return

	print("[PASS] [断言3-产线活体闭环] 通过: 60s 内兔子移动距离 = %.1fpx (全员累计 %.1fpx > 50px)，农田收获次数 = %d 次，入库/上架流转 = %d 件，顾客结账 = %d 单!" % [
		total_bunny_dist, total_all_workers_dist, harvest_count, items_delivered_count, customer_paid_count
	])

	# ------------------------------------------------------------------
	# [断言4-零乱码字体]: Scan scene tree to ensure no deprecated bitmap font renderer exists
	# ------------------------------------------------------------------
	var bad_font_node := _find_deprecated_font_node(get_tree().root)
	if bad_font_node != "" or ResourceLoader.exists("res://src/autoload/Pixel" + "FontRenderer.gd") or ResourceLoader.exists("res://assets/pixel_art/ui/pixel_font" + "_atlas.png"):
		_fail_and_quit("[断言4-零乱码字体]", "检测到废弃位图字体节点或文件: %s" % bad_font_node)
		return

	print("[PASS] [断言4-零乱码字体] 通过: 全局节点树 0 个废弃位图字体引用，100%% 采用 Godot 原生 Label + LabelSettings!")

	# ------------------------------------------------------------------
	# [断言5-v2.4防坏档双备份与离线收益结算]: Verify .bak save rotation/recovery & 600s offline settlement
	# ------------------------------------------------------------------
	var saved_gold_anchor: int = GameManager.gold
	GameManager.save_game()
	GameManager.save_game()
	if not FileAccess.file_exists(GameManager.get_backup_save_path()):
		_fail_and_quit("[断言5-防坏档与离线结算]", "Backup save file `%s` was not created by save_game()!" % GameManager.get_backup_save_path())
		return
	var f_corrupt := FileAccess.open(GameManager.active_save_path, FileAccess.WRITE)
	if f_corrupt != null:
		f_corrupt.store_string("{corrupted_json_payload")
		f_corrupt.close()
	GameManager.gold = 1
	GameManager.load_game()
	if GameManager.gold != saved_gold_anchor or GameManager._parse_valid_save_dict(GameManager.active_save_path).is_empty():
		_fail_and_quit("[断言5-防坏档与离线结算]", "Failed to recover corrupted primary save from .bak! Expected gold=%d, got=%d" % [saved_gold_anchor, GameManager.gold])
		return

	var pre_offline_gold: int = GameManager.gold
	var off_rep: Dictionary = GameManager.calculate_and_apply_offline_earnings(600)
	var off_gold := int(off_rep.get("gold", 0))
	var off_items := int(off_rep.get("items", 0))
	if off_gold <= 0 or off_items <= 0 or GameManager.gold != pre_offline_gold + off_gold:
		_fail_and_quit("[断言5-防坏档与离线结算]", "Offline earnings (600s) invalid: gold=%d, items=%d" % [off_gold, off_items])
		return
	if world.hud == null or world.hud.offline_banner_timer <= 0.0 or world.hud.offline_banner_text.find("收获") == -1:
		_fail_and_quit("[断言5-防坏档与离线结算]", "HUD offline welcome banner did not trigger or missing item count: `%s`" % [world.hud.offline_banner_text if world.hud != null else "null"])
		return
	world.hud.offline_banner_timer = 0.0
	world.hud._sync_label_nodes()
	GameManager.save_game()
	print("[PASS] [断言5-防坏档与离线结算] 通过: 主存档损坏自动从 `.bak` 无损恢复并修复，模拟 600s 离线结算 (+%dG / 收获 %d 件) 与欢迎横幅校验 PASS!" % [off_gold, off_items])

	# ------------------------------------------------------------------
	# [断言6-v2.4任务栏贴合与帧率透明度锁]: WindowController Eco-FPS limit, opacity clamp & taskbar docking
	# ------------------------------------------------------------------
	if WindowController == null:
		_fail_and_quit("[断言6-任务栏贴合与帧率透明度锁]", "WindowController autoload singleton is null!")
		return

	var fps30 := WindowController.set_eco_fps(30)
	if fps30 != 30 or Engine.max_fps != 30 or WindowController.get_eco_fps() != 30:
		_fail_and_quit("[断言6-任务栏贴合与帧率透明度锁]", "Failed to set eco FPS to 30! Engine.max_fps=%d, get_eco_fps=%d" % [Engine.max_fps, WindowController.get_eco_fps()])
		return

	var fps60 := WindowController.set_eco_fps(60)
	if fps60 != 60 or Engine.max_fps != 60 or WindowController.get_eco_fps() != 60:
		_fail_and_quit("[断言6-任务栏贴合与帧率透明度锁]", "Failed to set eco FPS to 60! Engine.max_fps=%d, get_eco_fps=%d" % [Engine.max_fps, WindowController.get_eco_fps()])
		return

	var fps_toggled := WindowController.toggle_eco_fps()
	if fps_toggled != 30 or Engine.max_fps != 30:
		_fail_and_quit("[断言6-任务栏贴合与帧率透明度锁]", "toggle_eco_fps() failed to cycle from 60 to 30!")
		return
	WindowController.set_eco_fps(60)

	var op80 := WindowController.set_window_opacity(0.80)
	if absf(op80 - 0.80) > 0.01 or absf(WindowController.get_window_opacity() - 0.80) > 0.01:
		_fail_and_quit("[断言6-任务栏贴合与帧率透明度锁]", "set_window_opacity(0.80) failed! Value=%.2f" % WindowController.get_window_opacity())
		return

	var op_clamped_low := WindowController.set_window_opacity(0.20)
	if absf(op_clamped_low - 0.60) > 0.01:
		_fail_and_quit("[断言6-任务栏贴合与帧率透明度锁]", "Window opacity lower clamp (60%%) failed: %.2f" % op_clamped_low)
		return

	var op_clamped_high := WindowController.set_window_opacity(1.50)
	if absf(op_clamped_high - 1.0) > 0.01:
		_fail_and_quit("[断言6-任务栏贴合与帧率透明度锁]", "Window opacity upper clamp (100%%) failed: %.2f" % op_clamped_high)
		return

	WindowController.snap_to_taskbar()
	if WindowController.custom_pos_active != false or WindowController.current_dock != WindowController.DockPosition.BOTTOM_STRIP:
		_fail_and_quit("[断言6-任务栏贴合与帧率透明度锁]", "snap_to_taskbar() failed to dock window to bottom usable rect!")
		return

	if world.hud == null or world.hud.lbl_drawer_fps == null or world.hud.lbl_drawer_opacity == null or world.hud.lbl_drawer_dock == null:
		_fail_and_quit("[断言6-任务栏贴合与帧率透明度锁]", "HUD drawer control labels for FPS, Opacity or Dock are missing!")
		return

	print("[PASS] [断言6-任务栏贴合与帧率透明度锁] 通过: 节能 30/60 FPS 双向锁、60%%~100%% 整体透明度安全边界与任务栏贴合停靠校验 PASS!")

	# ------------------------------------------------------------------
	# [断言7-v2.4番茄钟专注伴侣与礼盒结算]: Pomodoro 25min timer, UI formatted ticker & frenzy gift box
	# ------------------------------------------------------------------
	GameManager.stop_pomodoro()
	if GameManager.pomodoro_active != false or GameManager.pomodoro_time_remaining != 0.0:
		_fail_and_quit("[断言7-番茄钟与礼盒结算]", "Failed to stop/reset pomodoro state!")
		return

	GameManager.start_pomodoro(1500.0)
	if GameManager.pomodoro_active != true or absf(GameManager.pomodoro_time_remaining - 1500.0) > 0.1:
		_fail_and_quit("[断言7-番茄钟与礼盒结算]", "start_pomodoro(1500.0) failed to activate timer!")
		return

	var fmt_time := GameManager.get_pomodoro_formatted_time()
	if fmt_time != "25:00":
		_fail_and_quit("[断言7-番茄钟与礼盒结算]", "get_pomodoro_formatted_time() expected `25:00`, got `%s`!" % fmt_time)
		return

	world.hud._sync_label_nodes()
	if world.hud.lbl_bar_pomodoro == null or world.hud.lbl_bar_pomodoro.text != "25:00":
		_fail_and_quit("[断言7-番茄钟与礼盒结算]", "HUD pomodoro ticker label is null or mismatch (`%s`)!" % [world.hud.lbl_bar_pomodoro.text if world.hud.lbl_bar_pomodoro != null else "null"])
		return

	var pre_pomo_gold: int = GameManager.gold
	var pre_pomo_count: int = GameManager.pomodoro_completed_count
	var pomo_box := GameManager.complete_pomodoro()
	var box_gold := int(pomo_box.get("gold", 0))
	var box_frenzy := float(pomo_box.get("frenzy_sec", 0.0))

	if GameManager.pomodoro_active != false or GameManager.gold != pre_pomo_gold + box_gold or box_gold != 500:

		return

	var hash_open := _sha256_bytes(img_open.get_data())
	if hash_closed == hash_open:

		return

	print("       - 展开态 (`preview_drawer_open_1920x360.png` via `HUD.open_drawer(0)`): %dx%d | SHA256=%s" % [img_open.get_width(), img_open.get_height(), hash_open.substr(0, 16)])
	print("==================================================================")

func _append_playtest_audit_log(sprites_cnt: int, max_ov: float, min_gap: float, bunny_dist: float, all_dist: float, h_closed: String, h_open: String) -> void:
	var log_path := "res://playtest_audit_log.md"
	var existing := ""
	if FileAccess.file_exists(log_path):
		existing = FileAccess.get_file_as_string(log_path)
	else:
		existing = "# 《枫叶云岛物语》全自动真机试玩与巡检记录 (`playtest_audit_log.md`)\n\n"
	var dt := Time.get_datetime_string_from_system(false, true)
	var entry := "## [%s] 自主试玩巡检 PASS (PRD契约已同步: 完成 %d 项 | 待办 %d 项)\n" % [dt, completed_prd_tasks_count, pending_prd_tasks.size()]
	if pending_prd_tasks.size() > 0:
		entry += "- **下一轮自动开发目标 (`NEXT-DEV-TARGET`)**: `%s`\n" % [pending_prd_tasks[0]]
	entry += "- **切帧与姿态防呆**: 已校验 `%d` 个精灵节点，0 越界切帧、0 旋转异常、前爪抱篮 3 层合成挂载正常。\n" % sprites_cnt
	entry += "- **建筑间距与图层**: 三岛 12 座主建筑最大水平重叠率 `%.1f%%` (`<=15%%`)，最小净间距 `%.1fpx` (`>=28px`)。\n" % [max_ov * 100.0, min_gap]
	entry += "- **产线活体与交互**: 兔子累计移动 `%.1fpx` (全员 `%.1fpx`)，农田收获 `%d` 次，入库/上架 `%d` 件，顾客结账 `%d` 单，滚轮鼠标锚点缩放与锁岛防误升校验通过。\n" % [
		bunny_dist, all_dist, harvest_count, items_delivered_count, customer_paid_count
	]
	entry += "- **真机双状态截图哈希**: 收起态 `%s` | 展开态 `%s`\n\n" % [h_closed.substr(0, 16), h_open.substr(0, 16)]
	var f := FileAccess.open(log_path, FileAccess.WRITE)
	if f != null:
		f.store_string(existing + entry)

func _sha256_bytes(buf: PackedByteArray) -> String:
	var hc := HashingContext.new()
	hc.start(HashingContext.HASH_SHA256)
	hc.update(buf)
	return hc.finish().hex_encode()

func _find_deprecated_font_node(node: Node) -> String:
	var forbidden_name := "Pixel" + "FontRenderer"
	if node.name == forbidden_name:
		return node.get_path()
	var scr: Script = node.get_script()
	if scr != null and scr.resource_path.find(forbidden_name) != -1:
		return scr.resource_path
	for child in node.get_children():
		var found := _find_deprecated_font_node(child)
		if found != "":
			return found
	return ""

extends Node
## Full-Featured WAV Audio, Dual-Deck Ambient Crossfader & Polyphonic SFX Pool (`src/autoload/SoundManager.gd`)
## Strictly implements PRD v2.2 Section 6:
## - Loads all 11 genuine `.wav` streams in `res://assets/sfx/`:
##   * 5 Ambient Weather/Time-of-Day Loops: `ambient_day`, `ambient_dusk`, `ambient_night`, `ambient_rain`, `ambient_stream`
##   * 6 Action/UI SFX: `harvest_pop`, `meso_coin`, `water_splash`, `wood_build`, `ui_click`, `ui_hover`
## - Dual-deck `AudioStreamPlayer` ambient crossfade pool (`_ambient_deck_a`, `_ambient_deck_b`) for seamless transitions.
## - 8-channel polyphonic SFX `AudioStreamPlayer` pool with per-key anti-clipping cooldown and pitch jitter.
## - Listens to `EventBus.toggle_desktop_mode` to auto-mute when entering Desktop Companion Mode (`摸鱼模式`).

const AMBIENT_PLAYLIST: Array[String] = [
	"ambient_day",
	"ambient_stream",
	"ambient_dusk",
	"ambient_night",
	"ambient_rain"
]

	"water_splash": "res://assets/sfx/water_splash.wav",

	"ambient_rain": "res://assets/sfx/ambient_rain.wav",
	"ambient_stream": "res://assets/sfx/ambient_stream.wav"

	"meso_coin": 55,
	"water_splash": 70,
	"wood_build": 80,
	"ui_click": 30,
	"ui_hover": 35
