	"meso_coin": -4.5,
	"water_splash": -7.0,
	"wood_build": -5.0,
	"ui_click": -6.5,
	"ui_hover": -10.5
}

const SFX_POOL_SIZE := 8
const AMBIENT_BASE_DB := -14.0
const AMBIENT_ROTATE_INTERVAL := 65.0
const CROSSFADE_DURATION := 2.4

var is_muted: bool = true # Default auto-muted in Desktop Companion Mode
var master_sfx_volume_linear: float = 1.0
var master_ambient_volume_linear: float = 1.0

var current_ambient_idx: int = 0
var ambient_cycle_timer: float = 0.0
var _active_deck_is_a: bool = true
var _crossfade_t: float = 1.0
var _is_crossfading: bool = false

var _streams: Dictionary = {}
var _last_play_ticks: Dictionary = {}
var _sfx_players: Array[AudioStreamPlayer] = []
var _rr_cursor: int = 0

var _ambient_deck_a: AudioStreamPlayer
var _ambient_deck_b: AudioStreamPlayer

func _ready() -> void:
	_load_all_wav_streams()
	_build_audio_pools()

	if GameManager != null:
		is_muted = GameManager.desktop_companion_mode
	_apply_initial_ambient_track(0)

	if EventBus != null:
		EventBus.toggle_desktop_mode.connect(_on_toggle_desktop_mode)
		EventBus.island_unlocked.connect(_on_island_unlocked)

func _load_all_wav_streams() -> void:
	for key in SFX_FILE_MAP.keys():
		var res_path := String(SFX_FILE_MAP[key])
		if ResourceLoader.exists(res_path):
			var st := load(res_path) as AudioStream
			if st != null:
				_streams[key] = st

func _build_audio_pools() -> void:
	for i in range(SFX_POOL_SIZE):
		var p := AudioStreamPlayer.new()
		p.name = "SFXPlayer_" + str(i)
		p.bus = "Master"
		p.volume_db = -6.0
		add_child(p)
		_sfx_players.append(p)

	_ambient_deck_a = AudioStreamPlayer.new()
	_ambient_deck_a.name = "AmbientDeckA"
	_ambient_deck_a.bus = "Master"
	_ambient_deck_a.volume_db = AMBIENT_BASE_DB
	add_child(_ambient_deck_a)

	_ambient_deck_b = AudioStreamPlayer.new()
	_ambient_deck_b.name = "AmbientDeckB"
	_ambient_deck_b.bus = "Master"
	_ambient_deck_b.volume_db = -60.0
	add_child(_ambient_deck_b)

func _on_toggle_desktop_mode(enabled: bool) -> void:
	if enabled:
		set_muted(true)
	else:
		set_muted(false)

func _on_island_unlocked(_island_idx: int) -> void:
	play_water_sfx()
	advance_ambient_weather()

func get_current_ambient_key() -> String:
	return AMBIENT_PLAYLIST[posmod(current_ambient_idx, AMBIENT_PLAYLIST.size())]

func get_current_ambient_label() -> String:
	var k := get_current_ambient_key()
	return String(AMBIENT_LABELS.get(k, k))

func get_loaded_stream_count() -> int:
	return _streams.size()

func _apply_initial_ambient_track(idx: int) -> void:
	current_ambient_idx = posmod(idx, AMBIENT_PLAYLIST.size())
	var key := AMBIENT_PLAYLIST[current_ambient_idx]
	_active_deck_is_a = true
	_is_crossfading = false
	_crossfade_t = 1.0
	if _ambient_deck_a != null and _streams.has(key):
		_ambient_deck_a.stream = _streams[key]
		_ambient_deck_a.volume_db = _compute_ambient_db(1.0)
		if not is_muted:
			_ambient_deck_a.play()
	if _ambient_deck_b != null:
		_ambient_deck_b.stop()

func advance_ambient_weather() -> void:
	crossfade_to_ambient(current_ambient_idx + 1)

func crossfade_to_ambient(next_idx: int) -> void:
	current_ambient_idx = posmod(next_idx, AMBIENT_PLAYLIST.size())
	var key := AMBIENT_PLAYLIST[current_ambient_idx]
	if not _streams.has(key):
		return

	_active_deck_is_a = not _active_deck_is_a
	var incoming := _ambient_deck_a if _active_deck_is_a else _ambient_deck_b
	incoming.stream = _streams[key]
	incoming.volume_db = _compute_ambient_db(0.05)
	if not is_muted:
		incoming.play()
	_crossfade_t = 0.0
	_is_crossfading = true

func _compute_ambient_db(fade_weight: float) -> float:
	var lin := clampf(fade_weight * master_ambient_volume_linear, 0.0001, 1.5)
	return AMBIENT_BASE_DB + linear_to_db(lin)

func _process(delta: float) -> void:
	ambient_cycle_timer += delta
	if ambient_cycle_timer >= AMBIENT_ROTATE_INTERVAL:
		ambient_cycle_timer = 0.0
		advance_ambient_weather()

	if _is_crossfading:
		_crossfade_t = minf(1.0, _crossfade_t + delta / maxf(0.2, CROSSFADE_DURATION))
		var in_deck := _ambient_deck_a if _active_deck_is_a else _ambient_deck_b
		var out_deck := _ambient_deck_b if _active_deck_is_a else _ambient_deck_a
		if in_deck != null:
			in_deck.volume_db = _compute_ambient_db(_crossfade_t)
		if out_deck != null:
			out_deck.volume_db = _compute_ambient_db(1.0 - _crossfade_t)
		if _crossfade_t >= 1.0:
			_is_crossfading = false
			if out_deck != null:
				out_deck.stop()

	if not is_muted:
		var active_deck := _ambient_deck_a if _active_deck_is_a else _ambient_deck_b
		if active_deck != null and active_deck.stream != null and not active_deck.playing:
			active_deck.play()

func set_muted(mute: bool) -> void:
	is_muted = mute
	if is_muted:
		if _ambient_deck_a != null:
			_ambient_deck_a.stop()
		if _ambient_deck_b != null:
			_ambient_deck_b.stop()
		for p in _sfx_players:
			if p.playing:
				p.stop()
	else:
		var active_deck := _ambient_deck_a if _active_deck_is_a else _ambient_deck_b
		if active_deck != null and active_deck.stream != null:
			active_deck.volume_db = _compute_ambient_db(1.0)
			active_deck.play()

func toggle_mute() -> bool:
	set_muted(not is_muted)
	if not is_muted:
		play_click_sfx()
	return is_muted

func play_sfx(sfx_name: String, pitch_variance: float = 0.05, extra_db: float = 0.0) -> void:
	if is_muted or not _streams.has(sfx_name):
		return

	var now_ms := Time.get_ticks_msec()
	var min_cd := int(SFX_COOLDOWNS_MS.get(sfx_name, 35))
	var prev_ms := int(_last_play_ticks.get(sfx_name, -9999))
	if now_ms - prev_ms < min_cd:
		return
	_last_play_ticks[sfx_name] = now_ms

	var base_db := float(SFX_BASE_VOL_DB.get(sfx_name, -6.0)) + extra_db
	var final_db := base_db + linear_to_db(clampf(master_sfx_volume_linear, 0.05, 1.5))

	var chosen: AudioStreamPlayer = null
	for p in _sfx_players:
		if not p.playing:
			chosen = p
			break
	if chosen == null and _sfx_players.size() > 0:
		chosen = _sfx_players[_rr_cursor % _sfx_players.size()]
		_rr_cursor += 1

	if chosen != null:
		chosen.stream = _streams[sfx_name]
		chosen.volume_db = final_db
		chosen.pitch_scale = randf_range(1.0 - pitch_variance, 1.0 + pitch_variance)
		chosen.play()

func play_coin_sfx() -> void:
	play_sfx("meso_coin", 0.05, 0.0)

func play_upgrade_sfx() -> void:
	play_sfx("wood_build", 0.05, 0.0)

func play_milestone_sfx() -> void:
	play_sfx("wood_build", 0.02, 1.5)
	play_sfx("meso_coin", 0.03, -1.0)

func play_pop_sfx() -> void:
	play_sfx("harvest_pop", 0.06, 0.0)

func play_water_sfx() -> void:
	play_sfx("water_splash", 0.05, 0.0)

func play_click_sfx() -> void:
	play_sfx("ui_click", 0.03, 0.0)

func play_hover_sfx() -> void:
	play_sfx("ui_hover", 0.03, 0.0)

=== FILE: D:/project/maple-isle-idle/src/autoload/WindowController.gd ===
extends Node
## Desktop Companion Window, Multi-Island + UI Polygon Mouse Passthrough & Alt+Drag Controller (`src/autoload/WindowController.gd`)
## Strictly implements PRD v2.2 Section 1 & Section 5:
## - Controls `WINDOW_FLAG_TRANSPARENT`, `WINDOW_FLAG_BORDERLESS`, `WINDOW_FLAG_ALWAYS_ON_TOP` and `get_tree().root.transparent_bg`.
## - Dynamically computes the non-rectangular hull polygon covering the 3 islands AND the active HUD/drawer panel,
##   and passes it to `DisplayServer.window_set_mouse_passthrough(polygon)` so clicking transparent sky passes through to desktop apps.
## - Implements `Alt + Mouse Left-Click` (or HUD bar drag) window position dragging with multi-monitor clamping.

enum DockPosition { BOTTOM_STRIP, TOP_STRIP }

const VIEWPORT_W := 1920.0
const VIEWPORT_H := 300.0
const SCALE_PRESETS: Array[float] = [0.75, 1.0, 1.25]
const SCALE_LABELS: Array[String] = ["75%", "100%", "125%"]

## Per-island logical bounding rectangles in the 1920x300 panoramic strip
const ISLAND_2_LOCAL_RECT := Rect2(56.0, 58.0, 480.0, 178.0)
const ISLAND_1_LOCAL_RECT := Rect2(548.0, 42.0, 768.0, 198.0)
const ISLAND_3_LOCAL_RECT := Rect2(1310.0, 58.0, 560.0, 178.0)

var current_dock: DockPosition = DockPosition.BOTTOM_STRIP
var scale_idx: int = 1
var target_screen_idx: int = 0
var scale_factor: float = 1.0
var custom_pos_active: bool = false
var custom_pos: Vector2i = Vector2i.ZERO

var is_dragging: bool = false
var boss_stealth_active: bool = false
var target_fps: int = 60
var window_opacity: float = 1.0
var _drag_start_mouse: Vector2i = Vector2i.ZERO
var _drag_start_win_pos: Vector2i = Vector2i.ZERO

var _world_ref: Node2D = null
var _hud_ref: Control = null
var _last_island_rect: Rect2 = Rect2(40.0, 38.0, 1840.0, 210.0)
var _last_hud_rect: Rect2 = Rect2(480.0, 230.0, 960.0, 58.0)
var _last_drawer_rect: Rect2 = Rect2()
var _last_passthrough_polygon: PackedVector2Array = PackedVector2Array()

func _ready() -> void:
	var companion_on := true
	if GameManager != null:
		companion_on = GameManager.desktop_companion_mode
	apply_window_mode(companion_on)
	target_screen_idx = DisplayServer.window_get_current_screen()
	snap_window_to_desktop(current_dock)

	if EventBus != null:
		EventBus.toggle_desktop_mode.connect(_on_toggle_desktop_mode)

func _on_toggle_desktop_mode(enabled: bool) -> void:
	apply_window_mode(enabled)

func apply_window_mode(companion_enabled: bool) -> void:
	if companion_enabled:
		get_tree().root.transparent_bg = true
		DisplayServer.window_set_flag(DisplayServer.WINDOW_FLAG_TRANSPARENT, true)
		DisplayServer.window_set_flag(DisplayServer.WINDOW_FLAG_BORDERLESS, true)
		DisplayServer.window_set_flag(DisplayServer.WINDOW_FLAG_ALWAYS_ON_TOP, true)
		_refresh_passthrough()
	else:
		get_tree().root.transparent_bg = false
		DisplayServer.window_set_flag(DisplayServer.WINDOW_FLAG_ALWAYS_ON_TOP, false)
		DisplayServer.window_set_flag(DisplayServer.WINDOW_FLAG_BORDERLESS, false)
		DisplayServer.window_set_mouse_passthrough(PackedVector2Array())

func _input(event: InputEvent) -> void:
	# Global F9 Boss Key Stealth Toggle (hides islands & shrinks HUD while keeping 100% idle production running)
	if event is InputEventKey and event.pressed and not event.echo and event.keycode == KEY_F9:
		toggle_boss_stealth()
		return
	# Global Alt + Left-Click window drag support anywhere over the window
	if event is InputEventMouseButton and event.button_index == MOUSE_BUTTON_LEFT:
		if event.pressed and Input.is_key_pressed(KEY_ALT):
			start_window_drag()
		elif not event.pressed and is_dragging:
			stop_window_drag()

func toggle_boss_stealth() -> bool:
	boss_stealth_active = not boss_stealth_active
	if _world_ref != null and "world_root" in _world_ref and _world_ref.world_root != null:
		_world_ref.world_root.modulate.a = 0.0 if boss_stealth_active else window_opacity
	if _hud_ref != null and _hud_ref.has_method("set_boss_stealth"):
		_hud_ref.call("set_boss_stealth", boss_stealth_active)
	_refresh_passthrough()
	return boss_stealth_active

func set_eco_fps(fps: int) -> int:
	target_fps = 30 if fps <= 30 else 60
	Engine.max_fps = target_fps
	return target_fps

func toggle_eco_fps() -> int:
	return set_eco_fps(30 if target_fps == 60 else 60)

func get_eco_fps() -> int:
	return target_fps

func set_window_opacity(alpha: float) -> float:
	window_opacity = clampf(alpha, 0.60, 1.0)
	_apply_window_opacity()
	return window_opacity

func cycle_window_opacity() -> float:
	if window_opacity >= 0.95:
		return set_window_opacity(0.80)
	elif window_opacity >= 0.75:
		return set_window_opacity(0.60)
	else:
		return set_window_opacity(1.0)

func get_window_opacity() -> float:
	return window_opacity

func _apply_window_opacity() -> void:
	if boss_stealth_active:
		return
	if _world_ref != null and "world_root" in _world_ref and _world_ref.world_root != null:
		_world_ref.world_root.modulate.a = window_opacity
	elif _world_ref != null:
		_world_ref.modulate.a = window_opacity
	if _hud_ref != null and "hud_opacity" in _hud_ref:
		_hud_ref.modulate.a = _hud_ref.hud_opacity * window_opacity

func snap_to_taskbar() -> void:
	custom_pos_active = false
	snap_window_to_desktop(DockPosition.BOTTOM_STRIP)

func _process(_delta: float) -> void:
	if not is_dragging:
		return
	if not Input.is_mouse_button_pressed(MOUSE_BUTTON_LEFT) and not Input.is_mouse_button_pressed(MOUSE_BUTTON_RIGHT):
		stop_window_drag()
		return
	var cur_mouse := DisplayServer.mouse_get_position()
	var raw_next := _drag_start_win_pos + (cur_mouse - _drag_start_mouse)
	var clamped_pos := _clamp_window_to_desktop_bounds(raw_next)
	custom_pos_active = true
	custom_pos = clamped_pos
	if DisplayServer.window_get_position() != clamped_pos:
		DisplayServer.window_set_position(clamped_pos)

func _clamp_window_to_desktop_bounds(pos: Vector2i) -> Vector2i:
	var count := maxi(1, DisplayServer.get_screen_count())
	var screen_idx := clampi(target_screen_idx, 0, count - 1)
	var usable := DisplayServer.screen_get_usable_rect(screen_idx)
	var win_size := DisplayServer.window_get_size()
	var min_x := usable.position.x - int(win_size.x * 0.45)
	var max_x := usable.position.x + usable.size.x - int(win_size.x * 0.55)
	var min_y := usable.position.y
	var max_y := usable.position.y + usable.size.y - int(win_size.y * 0.35)
	return Vector2i(clampi(pos.x, min_x, maxi(min_x, max_x)), clampi(pos.y, min_y, maxi(min_y, max_y)))

func setup(world: Node = null, hud: Node = null) -> void:
	if world is Node2D:
		_world_ref = world
	if hud is Control:
		_hud_ref = hud
	_apply_window_opacity()
	_refresh_passthrough()

func begin_window_drag() -> void:
	start_window_drag()

func end_window_drag() -> void:
	stop_window_drag()

func start_window_drag() -> void:
	if is_dragging:
		return
	is_dragging = true
	_drag_start_mouse = DisplayServer.mouse_get_position()
	_drag_start_win_pos = DisplayServer.window_get_position()
	DisplayServer.window_set_mouse_passthrough(PackedVector2Array())

func stop_window_drag() -> void:
	if not is_dragging:
		return
	is_dragging = false
	_refresh_passthrough()

func cycle_window_scale() -> float:
	scale_idx = (scale_idx + 1) % SCALE_PRESETS.size()
	snap_window_to_desktop(current_dock)
	return SCALE_PRESETS[scale_idx]

func toggle_dock_position() -> DockPosition:
	current_dock = DockPosition.TOP_STRIP if current_dock == DockPosition.BOTTOM_STRIP else DockPosition.BOTTOM_STRIP
	custom_pos_active = false
	snap_window_to_desktop(current_dock)
	return current_dock

func snap_window_to_desktop(dock: DockPosition) -> void:
	var count := maxi(1, DisplayServer.get_screen_count())
	var screen_idx := clampi(target_screen_idx, 0, count - 1)
	var usable_rect := DisplayServer.screen_get_usable_rect(screen_idx)
	var base_scale := float(usable_rect.size.x) / VIEWPORT_W
	var user_mult: float = SCALE_PRESETS[clampi(scale_idx, 0, SCALE_PRESETS.size() - 1)]
	scale_factor = minf(base_scale * 1.25, base_scale * user_mult)
	var win_w := int(roundf(VIEWPORT_W * scale_factor))
	var win_h := int(roundf(VIEWPORT_H * scale_factor))
	var pos_x := usable_rect.position.x + int((usable_rect.size.x - win_w) * 0.5)
	var pos_y := usable_rect.position.y + usable_rect.size.y - win_h
	if dock == DockPosition.TOP_STRIP:
		pos_y = usable_rect.position.y
	if custom_pos_active:
		pos_x = custom_pos.x
		pos_y = custom_pos.y
	DisplayServer.window_set_size(Vector2i(win_w, win_h))
	DisplayServer.window_set_position(Vector2i(pos_x, pos_y))
	_refresh_passthrough()

func update_passthrough_polygon(logical_island_rect: Rect2, logical_hud_rect: Rect2, logical_drawer_rect: Rect2 = Rect2()) -> void:
	if _last_island_rect == logical_island_rect and _last_hud_rect == logical_hud_rect and _last_drawer_rect == logical_drawer_rect and not _last_passthrough_polygon.is_empty():
		return
	_last_island_rect = logical_island_rect
	_last_hud_rect = logical_hud_rect
	_last_drawer_rect = logical_drawer_rect
	if not is_dragging:
		_refresh_passthrough()

func get_current_passthrough_polygon() -> PackedVector2Array:
	return _last_passthrough_polygon

func _compute_active_islands_span() -> Rect2:
	var span := ISLAND_1_LOCAL_RECT
	if GameManager != null:
		if bool(GameManager.unlocked_islands.get(2, false)):
			span = span.merge(ISLAND_2_LOCAL_RECT)
		else:
			# Include silhouette bridge/island click preview zone
			span = span.merge(Rect2(60.0, 74.0, 460.0, 150.0))
		if bool(GameManager.unlocked_islands.get(3, false)):
			span = span.merge(ISLAND_3_LOCAL_RECT)
		else:
			span = span.merge(Rect2(1312.0, 74.0, 540.0, 150.0))
	return span.merge(_last_island_rect)

func _refresh_passthrough() -> void:
	if is_dragging:
		return
	if GameManager != null and not GameManager.desktop_companion_mode:
		DisplayServer.window_set_mouse_passthrough(PackedVector2Array())
		return

	var s := maxf(0.1, scale_factor)
	if boss_stealth_active:
		# In F9 Boss Stealth Mode, only the tiny bottom capsule strip receives mouse input; 99% of desktop passes through!
		var stealth_poly := PackedVector2Array([
			Vector2(760.0 * s, 232.0 * s),
			Vector2(1160.0 * s, 232.0 * s),
			Vector2(1160.0 * s, 276.0 * s),
			Vector2(760.0 * s, 276.0 * s)
		])
		_last_passthrough_polygon = stealth_poly
		DisplayServer.window_set_mouse_passthrough(stealth_poly)
		return

	var isl_rect := _compute_active_islands_span()
	var ui_rect := _last_hud_rect
	if _last_drawer_rect.size.x > 0.0 and _last_drawer_rect.size.y > 0.0:
		ui_rect = ui_rect.merge(_last_drawer_rect)

	var top_y := maxf(0.0, minf(isl_rect.position.y, ui_rect.position.y))
	var bot_y := minf(VIEWPORT_H, maxf(isl_rect.end.y, ui_rect.end.y))
	var isl_l := clampf(isl_rect.position.x, 0.0, VIEWPORT_W)
	var isl_r := clampf(isl_rect.end.x, 0.0, VIEWPORT_W)
	var ui_l := clampf(ui_rect.position.x, isl_l, VIEWPORT_W)
	var ui_r := clampf(ui_rect.end.x, 0.0, isl_r)
	var split_y := clampf(ui_rect.position.y, top_y + 12.0, bot_y - 12.0)

	# 8-vertex stepped polygon tightly wrapping the 3 islands + bottom HUD/drawer panel
	var poly := PackedVector2Array([
		Vector2(isl_l * s, top_y * s),
		Vector2(isl_r * s, top_y * s),
		Vector2(isl_r * s, split_y * s),
		Vector2(maxf(isl_r, ui_r) * s, split_y * s),
		Vector2(maxf(isl_r, ui_r) * s, bot_y * s),
		Vector2(minf(isl_l, ui_l) * s, bot_y * s),
		Vector2(minf(isl_l, ui_l) * s, split_y * s),
		Vector2(isl_l * s, split_y * s)
	])
	_last_passthrough_polygon = poly
	DisplayServer.window_set_mouse_passthrough(poly)

=== FILE: D:/project/maple-isle-idle/src/entities/buildings/BuildingVisualUpgrader.gd ===
extends Node2D
class_name BuildingVisualUpgrader
## 4-Tier Building Visual Evolution, Outer-Edge Decor, Particles, Star Badge & Legendary Shader (`src/entities/buildings/BuildingVisualUpgrader.gd`)
## Strictly enforces PRD v2.2 Section 4 & GEMINI.md Laws 2.2, 2.3 & 3.2:
## - Upgrade feedback: Q-bouncy scale Tween (`1.22x -> 0.92x -> 1.0x/1.08x`) + `"LV UP!"` / `"★里程碑突破 Lv.XX!"` floating text.
## - 4-Tier Visual Evolution:
##   * Tier 1 (Lv.1~24): Standard pixel building.
##   * Tier 2 (Lv.25~49): Mounts outer-edge `+16px` (`DECOR_OUTER_OFFSET`) decor sprite (`decor_scarecrow.png` on SoilPlot,
##     `decor_signpost.png` + top steam/alchemy spark `CPUParticles2D` on Workshop, `decor_lamp.png` + warm streetlamp glow on MarketStall).
##   * Tier 3 (Lv.50~99): Elevates building scale to `1.08x`, renders a 5-pointed Silver Star Badge at base, and doubles (`2x`) particle emission.
##   * Tier 4 (Lv.100+): Mounts custom Golden Shimmer Outline `ShaderMaterial`, emits Golden Stardust `CPUParticles2D`, and upgrades base badge to Gold Star.

@export var building_kind: String = "soil" # "soil", "mortar", "cauldron", "orbis_linked", "stall"

var current_level: int = 1
var current_tier: int = 1
var target_parent: Node2D

var decor_sprite: Sprite2D
var particles: CPUParticles2D
var gold_particles: CPUParticles2D
var legendary_shader_mat: ShaderMaterial
var shimmer_time: float = 0.0

func setup_upgrader(p_parent: Node2D, p_kind: String, p_initial_level: int = 1) -> void:
	target_parent = p_parent
	building_kind = p_kind
	current_level = p_initial_level
	_init_child_nodes()
	apply_level_visuals(current_level, false)

func _init_child_nodes() -> void:
	decor_sprite = Sprite2D.new()
	decor_sprite.name = "Tier2DecorSprite"
	decor_sprite.visible = false
	decor_sprite.centered = true
	decor_sprite.z_index = EconomyConfig.Z_BUILDING
	decor_sprite.z_as_relative = false
	add_child(decor_sprite)

	# GEMINI.md Law 2.2 & 3.2: Bottom-Center Pivot (`offset = Vector2(0.0, -float(frame_h) * 0.5)`) + outer edge + 16px
	if building_kind == "soil":
		decor_sprite.texture = load("res://assets/pixel_art/buildings/decor_scarecrow.png")
		decor_sprite.offset = Vector2(0.0, -float(56) * 0.5)
		decor_sprite.scale = Vector2(0.65, 0.65)
		decor_sprite.position = Vector2(-(SoilPlot.VISUAL_WIDTH * 0.5 + EconomyConfig.DECOR_OUTER_OFFSET), 0.0)
	elif building_kind == "mortar" or building_kind == "cauldron" or building_kind == "orbis_linked":
		decor_sprite.texture = load("res://assets/pixel_art/buildings/decor_signpost.png")
		decor_sprite.offset = Vector2(0.0, -float(54) * 0.5)
		decor_sprite.scale = Vector2(0.65, 0.65)
		decor_sprite.position = Vector2(-(Workshop.VISUAL_WIDTH * 0.5 + EconomyConfig.DECOR_OUTER_OFFSET), 0.0)
	elif building_kind == "stall":
		decor_sprite.texture = load("res://assets/pixel_art/buildings/decor_lamp.png")
		decor_sprite.offset = Vector2(0.0, -float(66) * 0.5)
		decor_sprite.scale = Vector2(0.75, 0.75)
		decor_sprite.position = Vector2(MarketStall.VISUAL_WIDTH * 0.5 + EconomyConfig.DECOR_OUTER_OFFSET, 0.0)

	# Tier 2/3 Workshop Steam / Alchemy Spark CPUParticles2D
	particles = CPUParticles2D.new()
	particles.name = "TierParticles"
	particles.emitting = false
	particles.amount = 8
	particles.lifetime = 1.4
	particles.position = Vector2(0.0, -38.0)
	particles.direction = Vector2(0.0, -1.0)
	particles.spread = 28.0
	particles.gravity = Vector2(0.0, -12.0)
	particles.initial_velocity_min = 8.0
	particles.initial_velocity_max = 18.0
	particles.scale_amount_min = 2.0
	particles.scale_amount_max = 3.5
	if building_kind == "cauldron" or building_kind == "orbis_linked":
		particles.color = Color(0.55, 0.96, 1.0, 0.85)
	else:
		particles.color = Color(0.98, 0.96, 0.90, 0.75)
	add_child(particles)

	# Tier 4 (Lv.100+) Golden Stardust CPUParticles2D & Golden Shimmer Outline ShaderMaterial
	gold_particles = CPUParticles2D.new()
	gold_particles.name = "Tier4GoldParticles"
	gold_particles.emitting = false
	gold_particles.amount = 16
	gold_particles.lifetime = 1.6
	gold_particles.emission_shape = CPUParticles2D.EMISSION_SHAPE_SPHERE
	gold_particles.emission_sphere_radius = 24.0
	gold_particles.position = Vector2(0.0, -24.0)
	gold_particles.gravity = Vector2(0.0, -8.0)
	gold_particles.initial_velocity_min = 4.0
	gold_particles.initial_velocity_max = 10.0
	gold_particles.scale_amount_min = 2.0
	gold_particles.scale_amount_max = 3.5
	gold_particles.color = Color(1.0, 0.88, 0.25, 0.92)
	add_child(gold_particles)

	var sh := Shader.new()
	sh.code = """
shader_type canvas_item;
uniform float shimmer_speed : hint_range(0.5, 5.0) = 2.4;
uniform vec4 outline_gold : source_color = vec4(1.0, 0.88, 0.28, 1.0);
void fragment() {
	vec4 tex = texture(TEXTURE, UV);
	float wave = 0.5 + 0.5 * sin(TIME * shimmer_speed + (UV.x + UV.y) * 9.0);
	vec2 ps = TEXTURE_PIXEL_SIZE;
	float edge_a = max(
		max(texture(TEXTURE, UV + vec2(ps.x, 0.0)).a, texture(TEXTURE, UV - vec2(ps.x, 0.0)).a),
		max(texture(TEXTURE, UV + vec2(0.0, ps.y)).a, texture(TEXTURE, UV - vec2(0.0, ps.y)).a)
	);
	vec3 gold_tint = mix(tex.rgb, outline_gold.rgb, wave * 0.30);
	if (tex.a < 0.1 && edge_a > 0.4) {
		COLOR = vec4(outline_gold.rgb, edge_a * (0.55 + 0.35 * wave));
	} else {
		COLOR = vec4(gold_tint, tex.a);
	}
}
"""
	legendary_shader_mat = ShaderMaterial.new()
	legendary_shader_mat.shader = sh

func get_tier_for_level(lv: int) -> int:
	if lv >= 100:
		return 4
	elif lv >= 50:
		return 3
	elif lv >= 25:
		return 2
	return 1

func get_base_scale_for_tier(tier: int) -> Vector2:
	return Vector2(1.08, 1.08) if tier >= 3 else Vector2.ONE

func apply_level_visuals(new_level: int, play_bounce: bool = true) -> void:
	var old_tier := current_tier
	current_level = new_level
	current_tier = get_tier_for_level(new_level)
	var base_sc := get_base_scale_for_tier(current_tier)

	if decor_sprite != null:
		decor_sprite.visible = (current_tier >= 2)

	if particles != null:
		var is_workshop := (building_kind == "mortar" or building_kind == "cauldron" or building_kind == "orbis_linked")
		particles.emitting = is_workshop and (current_tier >= 2)
		particles.amount = 16 if current_tier >= 3 else 8

	if gold_particles != null:
		gold_particles.emitting = (current_tier >= 4)

	if target_parent != null:
		if current_tier >= 4:
			target_parent.material = legendary_shader_mat
		else:
			target_parent.material = null

		if play_bounce and is_inside_tree():
			var tw := create_tween()
			tw.tween_property(target_parent, "scale", base_sc * 1.22, 0.08).set_trans(Tween.TRANS_QUAD).set_ease(Tween.EASE_OUT)
			tw.tween_property(target_parent, "scale", base_sc * 0.92, 0.08).set_trans(Tween.TRANS_QUAD).set_ease(Tween.EASE_IN_OUT)
			tw.tween_property(target_parent, "scale", base_sc, 0.10).set_trans(Tween.TRANS_BACK).set_ease(Tween.EASE_OUT)

			var gpos := target_parent.position
			if current_tier > old_tier or (new_level % 25 == 0):
				EventBus.spawn_floating_text.emit(gpos + Vector2(0, -54), "★里程碑突破 Lv." + str(new_level) + "!", Color("#FFE066"))
				if SoundManager != null:
					SoundManager.play_milestone_sfx()
			else:
				EventBus.spawn_floating_text.emit(gpos + Vector2(0, -48), "LV UP!", Color("#FFE066"))
				if SoundManager != null:
					SoundManager.play_upgrade_sfx()
		else:
			target_parent.scale = base_sc

	queue_redraw()

func _process(delta: float) -> void:
	if current_tier >= 2:
		shimmer_time += delta
		queue_redraw()

func _draw_five_pointed_star(center: Vector2, outer_r: float, inner_r: float, col: Color) -> void:
	var pts := PackedVector2Array()
	for i in range(10):
		var r := outer_r if (i % 2 == 0) else inner_r
		var ang := -PI * 0.5 + float(i) * PI / 5.0
		pts.append(center + Vector2(cos(ang) * r, sin(ang) * r))
	draw_colored_polygon(pts, col)

func _draw() -> void:
	# Tier 2+ MarketStall Streetlamp Warm Light Halo
	if current_tier >= 2 and building_kind == "stall" and decor_sprite != null:
		var pulse := 0.85 + 0.15 * sin(shimmer_time * 3.2)
		var lamp_top := decor_sprite.position + Vector2(0.0, -36.0)
		draw_circle(lamp_top, 18.0 * pulse, Color(1.0, 0.88, 0.42, 0.22 * pulse))
		draw_circle(lamp_top, 9.0 * pulse, Color(1.0, 0.95, 0.68, 0.34 * pulse))

	# Tier 3+ (Lv.50~99 Silver Star Badge / Lv.100+ Gold Star Badge) at Building Base
	if current_tier >= 3:
		var badge_center := Vector2(0.0, -4.0)
		var star_col := Color(1.0, 0.88, 0.24, 0.98) if current_tier >= 4 else Color(0.86, 0.94, 1.0, 0.96)
		draw_circle(badge_center, 7.0, Color(0.14, 0.08, 0.04, 0.88))
		draw_arc(badge_center, 7.0, 0.0, TAU, 16, star_col, 1.4)
		_draw_five_pointed_star(badge_center, 5.2, 2.3, star_col)

=== FILE: D:/project/maple-isle-idle/src/entities/buildings/MarketStall.gd ===
extends Node2D
class_name MarketStall
## Central Cloud Isle Market Stall, Queue Dispatcher & Shiba Manager Checkout Hub (`src/entities/buildings/MarketStall.gd`)
## Strictly enforces PRD v2.2 & GEMINI.md Laws 2, 3, 5:
## - Root `position = Vector2(slot_x, GROUND_Y)` is the bottom-center grounding point (`z_index = 0`).
## - `body_sprite`: `centered = true`, `offset = Vector2(0.0, -float(104) * 0.5) = Vector2(0.0, -52.0)`.
## - 5:1 Ultra-Flat Polygon Drop Shadow at `Vector2(0, 0)` (`shadow_width = w * 0.55`, `shadow_height = shadow_width * 0.18`, `Color(0, 0, 0, 0.20)`).
## - Supports zero-Shiba self-service checkout (`1.8s` duration, `1.0x` price bonus) and Shiba Manager accelerated checkout (`1.0s` duration, `PriceBonus = 1.0 + 0.1 * L`).
## - Pure numeric inventory buffer (`GameManager.stall_inventory`, default cap = 10, +5 per building level) with zero icon clutter on the counter.

const BASE_SPRITE_SCALE := 0.72
const VISUAL_WIDTH := 92.16 # 128 * 0.72
const VISUAL_HEIGHT := 74.88 # 104 * 0.72
const QUEUE_BASE_OFFSET_X := 28.0
const QUEUE_SPACING_X := 16.0
const MAX_VISIBLE_QUEUE := 5

const SELF_SERVICE_CHECKOUT_SEC := 1.8
const SHIBA_MANAGER_CHECKOUT_SEC := 1.0

var tex_stall: Texture2D
var body_sprite: Sprite2D
var visual_upgrader: BuildingVisualUpgrader
var level_label: Label

var queue_customers: Array[Node2D] = []
var anim_time: float = 0.0
var checkout_flash_timer: float = 0.0
var total_customers_served: int = 0
var total_gold_collected: int = 0

func _ready() -> void:
	z_index = EconomyConfig.Z_BUILDING
	z_as_relative = false
	tex_stall = load("res://assets/pixel_art/buildings/market_stall.png")

	# GEMINI.md Law 2.2: Bottom-Center Pivot (`centered = true`, `offset = Vector2(0.0, -float(frame_h) * 0.5)`)
	body_sprite = Sprite2D.new()
	body_sprite.name = "BodySprite"
	body_sprite.texture = tex_stall
	body_sprite.centered = true
	body_sprite.offset = Vector2(0.0, -float(104) * 0.5)
	body_sprite.scale = Vector2(BASE_SPRITE_SCALE, BASE_SPRITE_SCALE)
	body_sprite.z_index = EconomyConfig.Z_BUILDING
	body_sprite.z_as_relative = false
	add_child(body_sprite)

	var cur_lv := get_stall_level()

	visual_upgrader = BuildingVisualUpgrader.new()
	add_child(visual_upgrader)
	visual_upgrader.setup_upgrader(self, "stall", cur_lv)

	level_label = EconomyConfig.create_pixel_label("Lv." + str(cur_lv), 14, Color("#FFF4D6"), true)
	level_label.position = Vector2(-28.0, 2.0)
	level_label.custom_minimum_size = Vector2(56.0, 20.0)
	add_child(level_label)

	if EventBus != null:
		EventBus.upgrade_purchased.connect(_on_upgrade_purchased)
		EventBus.customer_paid.connect(_on_customer_paid)

func get_stall_level() -> int:
	return maxi(1, int(GameManager.building_levels.get("stall", 1)))

func get_shiba_level() -> int:
	if not GameManager.is_animal_hired("shiba"):
		return 0
	return maxi(1, int(GameManager.animal_levels.get("shiba", 1)))

func is_shiba_manager_active() -> bool:
	return GameManager.is_animal_hired("shiba")

func get_checkout_duration() -> float:
	if not is_shiba_manager_active():
		return SELF_SERVICE_CHECKOUT_SEC
	var shiba_lv := get_shiba_level()
	return maxf(0.45, SHIBA_MANAGER_CHECKOUT_SEC * pow(0.97, float(shiba_lv - 1)))

func get_price_bonus_multiplier() -> float:
	return EconomyConfig.calc_shiba_price_bonus(get_shiba_level())

func get_current_stock_count() -> int:
	return GameManager.get_total_stall_count()

func get_max_stock_capacity() -> int:
	return GameManager.get_stall_max_capacity()

func has_available_stock() -> bool:
	return get_current_stock_count() > 0

func get_inventory_snapshot() -> Dictionary:
	return GameManager.stall_inventory.duplicate()

func execute_customer_checkout(preferred_item_id: String, desired_qty: int, customer_pos: Vector2, is_vip: bool = false) -> int:
	return GameManager.try_purchase_from_stall(preferred_item_id, desired_qty, customer_pos, is_vip)

func get_screen_rect() -> Rect2:
	var w := VISUAL_WIDTH * scale.x
	var h := VISUAL_HEIGHT * scale.y
	return Rect2(position.x - w * 0.5, position.y - h, w, h)

func _on_upgrade_purchased(category: String, key: String, new_level: int) -> void:
	if category == "building" and key == "stall":
		if level_label != null:
			level_label.text = "Lv." + str(new_level)
		if visual_upgrader != null:
			visual_upgrader.apply_level_visuals(new_level, true)

func _on_customer_paid(_recipe_id: String, _qty: int, total_gold: int, _world_pos: Vector2) -> void:
	total_customers_served += 1
	total_gold_collected += total_gold
	checkout_flash_timer = 0.35

func register_in_queue(cust: Node2D) -> int:
	_cleanup_queue()
	if not queue_customers.has(cust):
		queue_customers.append(cust)
	return queue_customers.find(cust)

func unregister_from_queue(cust: Node2D) -> void:
	queue_customers.erase(cust)
	_cleanup_queue()

func get_queue_slot_pos(cust: Node2D) -> Vector2:
	var idx := clampi(register_in_queue(cust), 0, MAX_VISIBLE_QUEUE)
	return Vector2(position.x + QUEUE_BASE_OFFSET_X + float(idx) * QUEUE_SPACING_X, EconomyConfig.GROUND_Y)

func is_front_of_queue(cust: Node2D) -> bool:
	_cleanup_queue()
	return queue_customers.size() > 0 and queue_customers[0] == cust

func get_queue_length() -> int:
	_cleanup_queue()
	return queue_customers.size()

func _cleanup_queue() -> void:
	for i in range(queue_customers.size() - 1, -1, -1):
		if not is_instance_valid(queue_customers[i]):
			queue_customers.remove_at(i)

func _process(delta: float) -> void:
	anim_time += delta
	if checkout_flash_timer > 0.0:
		checkout_flash_timer = maxf(0.0, checkout_flash_timer - delta)
	queue_redraw()

func _draw() -> void:
	if tex_stall == null:
		return
	# GEMINI.md Law 3.3: 5:1 Ultra-Flat Polygon Drop Shadow at origin `Vector2(0, 0)`
	EconomyConfig.draw_drop_shadow(self, VISUAL_WIDTH)

	# Subtle warm lantern glow under the stall awning (enhanced when Shiba manager or Lv.25+ lamp is active)
	var pulse := 0.85 + 0.15 * sin(anim_time * 3.0)
	var base_alpha := (0.14 if is_shiba_manager_active() else 0.08) * pulse
	if checkout_flash_timer > 0.0:
		base_alpha += 0.18 * (checkout_flash_timer / 0.35)
	draw_circle(Vector2(-10.0, -34.0), 14.0, Color(1.0, 0.86, 0.38, base_alpha))

=== FILE: D:/project/maple-isle-idle/src/entities/buildings/SoilPlot.gd ===
extends Node2D
class_name SoilPlot
## Enlarged 1.5D Soil Bed (`64x26`), 3-Stage Crop Growth, Buffer Cap 10 & Lv.25 Auto-Evolution (`src/entities/buildings/SoilPlot.gd`)
## Strictly enforces PRD v2.2 & GEMINI.md Laws 2, 3, 4, 5:
## - Root `position = Vector2(slot_x, GROUND_Y)` is the bottom-center grounding point.
## - `soil_sprite`: `z_index = -2`, `hframes = 4, vframes = 1`, `region_enabled = false`, `centered = true`, `offset = Vector2(0.0, -float(18) * 0.5)`.
## - `crop_sprite`: `z_index = 1`, `hframes = 4, vframes = 4`, `region_enabled = false`, `centered = true`, `offset = Vector2(0.0, -float(40) * 0.5)`.
## - Implements 3 growth stages (`Stage 0: Seed -> Stage 1: Sprout -> Stage 2: Mature`) on `crops_sheet_normalized.png`.
## - Enforces pure numeric buffer cap (default `10`, `+5/lv`), pausing growth/harvest when full so Bunny displays `"Zzz..."` bubble.
## - Every 25 building levels (`Lv.25 / Lv.50 / Lv.75`), automatically evolves crop species to the next row and doubles (`2x`) harvest yield!

const VISUAL_WIDTH := 64.0
const VISUAL_HEIGHT := 26.0
const DEFAULT_BASKET_CAP := 10

enum GrowthStage {
	SEED = 0,
	SPROUT = 1,
	MATURE = 2
}

@export var island_idx: int = 1
@export var plot_index_on_island: int = 0
@export var crop_id: String = "crop_0"

var tex_soil: Texture2D
var tex_crops: Texture2D
var soil_sprite: Sprite2D
var crop_sprite: Sprite2D
var visual_upgrader: BuildingVisualUpgrader
var level_label: Label

var sown: bool = true
var watered: bool = true
var growth: float = 0.35
var current_stage: GrowthStage = GrowthStage.SEED
var reserved_by_worker: bool = false
var sway_time: float = 0.0
var harvest_count: int = 0
var total_crops_harvested: int = 0

func _ready() -> void:
	z_index = EconomyConfig.Z_BUILDING
	z_as_relative = false
	tex_soil = load("res://assets/pixel_art/islands/soil_plot_normalized.png")
	tex_crops = load("res://assets/pixel_art/crops/crops_sheet_normalized.png")
	sway_time = randf() * TAU

	# GEMINI.md Law 2.2 & 2.3 & 4.2: Soil base Sprite2D (`z_index = -2`, `hframes = 4, vframes = 1`, `region_enabled = false`, bottom-center pivot)
	soil_sprite = Sprite2D.new()
	soil_sprite.name = "SoilBaseSprite"
	soil_sprite.texture = tex_soil
	soil_sprite.region_enabled = false
	soil_sprite.hframes = 4
	soil_sprite.vframes = 1
	soil_sprite.frame = 2
	soil_sprite.centered = true
	soil_sprite.offset = Vector2(0.0, -float(18) * 0.5)
	soil_sprite.scale = Vector2(1.6, 1.44)
	soil_sprite.z_index = EconomyConfig.Z_SOIL_BASE
	soil_sprite.z_as_relative = false
	add_child(soil_sprite)

	# GEMINI.md Law 2.2 & 2.3 & 4.2: Crop Sprite2D (`z_index = 1`, `hframes = 4, vframes = 4`, `region_enabled = false`, bottom-center pivot)
	crop_sprite = Sprite2D.new()
	crop_sprite.name = "CropSprite"
	crop_sprite.texture = tex_crops
	crop_sprite.region_enabled = false
	crop_sprite.hframes = 4
	crop_sprite.vframes = 4
	crop_sprite.frame = 0
	crop_sprite.centered = true
	crop_sprite.offset = Vector2(0.0, -float(40) * 0.5)
	crop_sprite.position = Vector2(0.0, -6.0)
	crop_sprite.scale = Vector2(0.85, 0.85)
	crop_sprite.z_index = EconomyConfig.Z_CROP
	crop_sprite.z_as_relative = false
	add_child(crop_sprite)

	var cur_lv := get_building_level()

	visual_upgrader = BuildingVisualUpgrader.new()
	add_child(visual_upgrader)
	visual_upgrader.setup_upgrader(self, "soil", cur_lv)

	if plot_index_on_island == 0:
		level_label = EconomyConfig.create_pixel_label("Lv." + str(cur_lv), 14, Color("#FFF4D6"), true)
		level_label.position = Vector2(-28.0, 2.0)

		add_child(level_label)

	if EventBus != null:
		EventBus.upgrade_purchased.connect(_on_upgrade_purchased)
	_refresh_evolved_crop()
	_sync_sprites()

func setup_plot(p_island: int, p_idx_on_island: int, p_crop_id: String, p_pos: Vector2) -> void:
	island_idx = p_island
	plot_index_on_island = p_idx_on_island
	crop_id = p_crop_id
	position = Vector2(p_pos.x, EconomyConfig.GROUND_Y)
	growth = 0.62 if (GameManager != null and GameManager.is_automated_test_mode) else randf_range(0.35, 0.88)
	watered = true
	_refresh_evolved_crop()
	_sync_sprites()
	var cur_lv := get_building_level()
	if level_label != null:
		level_label.visible = (plot_index_on_island == 0)
		level_label.text = "Lv." + str(cur_lv)
	if visual_upgrader != null:
		visual_upgrader.visible = (plot_index_on_island == 0)
		visual_upgrader.apply_level_visuals(cur_lv, false)

func get_building_key() -> String:
	return "soil_" + str(island_idx)

func get_building_level() -> int:
	return maxi(1, int(GameManager.building_levels.get(get_building_key(), 1)))

func get_milestone_tier() -> int:
	return int(floor(float(get_building_level()) / 25.0))

func get_milestone_yield_multiplier() -> int:
	return int(round(pow(2.0, float(get_milestone_tier()))))

func get_basket_capacity() -> int:
	if GameManager != null:
		return GameManager.get_raw_basket_max_cap(island_idx)
	return DEFAULT_BASKET_CAP

func is_output_buffer_blocked() -> bool:
	if GameManager == null:
		return false
	var otter_hired := GameManager.is_island_otter_hired(island_idx)
	if otter_hired:
		return GameManager.is_raw_basket_full(island_idx)
	elif island_idx == 1:
		return GameManager.is_stall_full()
	return GameManager.is_raw_basket_full(island_idx)

func get_screen_rect() -> Rect2:
	var w := VISUAL_WIDTH * scale.x
	var h := VISUAL_HEIGHT * scale.y
	return Rect2(position.x - w * 0.5, position.y - h, w, h)

func _on_upgrade_purchased(category: String, key: String, new_level: int) -> void:
	if category == "building" and key == get_building_key():
		_refresh_evolved_crop()
		if level_label != null:
			level_label.text = "Lv." + str(new_level)
		if visual_upgrader != null and plot_index_on_island == 0:
			visual_upgrader.apply_level_visuals(new_level, true)

func _refresh_evolved_crop() -> void:
	crop_id = EconomyConfig.get_evolved_crop_id(island_idx, get_building_level())

func is_plot_active() -> bool:
	if not bool(GameManager.unlocked_islands.get(island_idx, false)):
		return false
	return plot_index_on_island < GameManager.get_unlocked_plot_count(island_idx)

func _compute_growth_stage(g: float) -> GrowthStage:
	if g >= 0.72:
		return GrowthStage.MATURE
	elif g >= 0.34:
		return GrowthStage.SPROUT
	return GrowthStage.SEED

func is_ready_for_harvest() -> bool:
	return sown and growth >= 0.72

func apply_farmer_tending(tending_delta: float) -> void:
	watered = true
	if sown and growth < 1.0:
		growth = minf(1.0, growth + tending_delta * 0.24)
		current_stage = _compute_growth_stage(growth)
		_sync_sprites()

func _process(delta: float) -> void:
	sway_time += delta
	visible = is_plot_active()
	if not visible:
		return

	if sown and growth < 1.0:
		var c_cfg: Dictionary = EconomyConfig.CROPS.get(crop_id, EconomyConfig.CROPS["crop_0"])
		var base_dur := float(c_cfg.get("grow_time", 5.2))
		var b_lv := get_building_level()
		var growth_mult := GameManager.get_crop_growth_multiplier(island_idx) if GameManager != null else 1.0
		var speed_mult := (1.35 if watered else 1.0) * (1.0 + 0.06 * float(b_lv - 1)) * growth_mult
		growth = minf(1.0, growth + (delta * speed_mult) / maxf(1.0, base_dur))

	current_stage = _compute_growth_stage(growth)
	_sync_sprites()

func _sync_sprites() -> void:
	if soil_sprite != null:
		var st_idx := 0
		if sown:
			if growth >= 0.92:
				st_idx = 3
			elif watered:
				st_idx = 2
			else:
				st_idx = 1
		soil_sprite.frame = clampi(st_idx, 0, 3)

	if crop_sprite != null:
		crop_sprite.visible = sown
		if sown:
			var c_data: Dictionary = EconomyConfig.CROPS.get(crop_id, EconomyConfig.CROPS["crop_0"])
			var row := clampi(int(c_data.get("crop_row", 0)), 0, 3)
			var stage_col := int(current_stage) # 0 = Seed, 1 = Sprout, 2 = Mature in-ground
			crop_sprite.frame = row * 4 + clampi(stage_col, 0, 2)
			var sway_amp := 1.2 if current_stage == GrowthStage.MATURE else (0.5 if current_stage == GrowthStage.SPROUT else 0.0)
			var sway_x := roundf(sin(sway_time * 1.6 + position.x * 0.05) * sway_amp)
			crop_sprite.position = Vector2(sway_x, -6.0)

func harvest_crop_yield() -> int:
	_refresh_evolved_crop()
	# PRD v2.2 Section 3 & 4: Multiplies unlocked ridge slot count (`1x -> 2x` at Lv.2+) and Lv.25 milestone (`2x` per 25 levels)!
	var ridge_slots := maxi(1, GameManager.get_unlocked_plot_count(island_idx)) if GameManager != null else 1
	var qty := maxi(1, get_milestone_yield_multiplier() * ridge_slots)
	harvest_count += 1
	total_crops_harvested += qty
	sown = true
	watered = true
	growth = 0.05
	current_stage = GrowthStage.SEED
	reserved_by_worker = false
	_sync_sprites()

	if crop_sprite != null and is_inside_tree():
		var tw := create_tween()
		tw.tween_property(crop_sprite, "scale", Vector2(1.02, 0.72), 0.06)
		tw.tween_property(crop_sprite, "scale", Vector2(0.85, 0.85), 0.10)

	if EventBus != null:
		EventBus.item_produced.emit(island_idx, crop_id, qty)
	return qty

=== FILE: D:/project/maple-isle-idle/src/entities/buildings/Workshop.gd ===
extends Node2D
class_name Workshop
## Mortar, Cauldron & Island 3 Dual Workshop (`src/entities/buildings/Workshop.gd`)
## Strictly enforces PRD v2.2 & GEMINI.md Laws 2, 3, 5:
## - Root `position = Vector2(slot_x, GROUND_Y)` is the bottom-center grounding point (`z_index = 0`).
## - Supports `workshop_mortar.png` (3.5x), `workshop_cauldron.png` (12x), and Island 3 linked dual workshop (`orbis_linked`, 52x).
## - Manages dual numeric Input & Output buffers (`GameManager.workshop_inputs` & `workshop_outputs`, base cap = 10, +5/lv).
## - Renders both a circular progress ring and a crisp horizontal progress bar above the workshop during active crafting.
## - Every 25 workshop levels (`Lv.25 / Lv.50 / Lv.75`), automatically evolves output recipe along `delicacies_sheet_normalized.png` and doubles value (`2x`)!

const BASE_SPRITE_SCALE := 0.85
const VISUAL_WIDTH := 57.8 # 68 * 0.85
const VISUAL_HEIGHT := 51.0
const DEFAULT_BUFFER_CAP := 10

@export var workshop_id: String = "mortar" # "mortar", "cauldron", or "orbis_linked"
@export var island_idx: int = 1

var tex_building: Texture2D
var tex_secondary: Texture2D
var tex_delicacies: Texture2D
var body_sprite: Sprite2D
var secondary_sprite: Sprite2D
var visual_upgrader: BuildingVisualUpgrader
var level_label: Label

var is_processing: bool = false
var _was_processing: bool = false
var process_progress: float = 0.0
var _chef_progress: Dictionary = {}
var work_anim_time: float = 0.0
var crafted_count: int = 0

func set_chef_progress(chef_key: String, prog: float, active: bool) -> void:
	if active:
		_chef_progress[chef_key] = clampf(prog, 0.0, 1.0)
	else:
		_chef_progress.erase(chef_key)
	if _chef_progress.is_empty():
		is_processing = false
		process_progress = 0.0
	else:
		is_processing = true
		var best_p := 0.0
		for k in _chef_progress.keys():
			best_p = maxf(best_p, float(_chef_progress[k]))
		process_progress = best_p

func _ready() -> void:
	z_index = EconomyConfig.Z_BUILDING
	z_as_relative = false
	tex_delicacies = load("res://assets/pixel_art/buildings/delicacies_sheet_normalized.png")

	body_sprite = Sprite2D.new()
	body_sprite.name = "BodySprite"
	body_sprite.centered = true
	body_sprite.scale = Vector2(BASE_SPRITE_SCALE, BASE_SPRITE_SCALE)
	body_sprite.z_index = EconomyConfig.Z_BUILDING
	body_sprite.z_as_relative = false
	add_child(body_sprite)

	secondary_sprite = Sprite2D.new()
	secondary_sprite.name = "SecondarySprite"
	secondary_sprite.centered = true
	secondary_sprite.visible = false
	secondary_sprite.scale = Vector2(0.68, 0.68)
	secondary_sprite.position = Vector2(18.0, 0.0)
	secondary_sprite.z_index = EconomyConfig.Z_BUILDING
	secondary_sprite.z_as_relative = false
	add_child(secondary_sprite)

	_reload_texture()
	var cur_lv := get_workshop_level()

	visual_upgrader = BuildingVisualUpgrader.new()
	add_child(visual_upgrader)
	visual_upgrader.setup_upgrader(self, workshop_id, cur_lv)

	level_label = EconomyConfig.create_pixel_label("Lv." + str(cur_lv), 14, Color("#FFF4D6"), true)
	level_label.position = Vector2(-28.0, 2.0)
	level_label.custom_minimum_size = Vector2(56.0, 20.0)
	add_child(level_label)

	if EventBus != null:
		EventBus.upgrade_purchased.connect(_on_upgrade_purchased)

func setup_workshop(p_id: String, p_island: int, p_pos: Vector2, _p_offset: Vector2 = Vector2.ZERO) -> void:
	workshop_id = p_id
	island_idx = p_island
	position = Vector2(p_pos.x, EconomyConfig.GROUND_Y)
	_reload_texture()
	var cur_lv := get_workshop_level()
	if level_label != null:
		level_label.text = "Lv." + str(cur_lv)
	if visual_upgrader != null:
		visual_upgrader.building_kind = workshop_id
		visual_upgrader.apply_level_visuals(cur_lv, false)

func get_building_key() -> String:
	return "soil_3" if workshop_id == "orbis_linked" else workshop_id

func get_workshop_level() -> int:
	return maxi(1, int(GameManager.building_levels.get(get_building_key(), 1)))

func get_buffer_capacity() -> int:
	if GameManager != null:
		return GameManager.get_workshop_buffer_cap(workshop_id)
	return DEFAULT_BUFFER_CAP

func get_input_buffer_count() -> int:
	return GameManager.get_workshop_input_total(workshop_id) if GameManager != null else 0

func get_output_buffer_count() -> int:
	return GameManager.get_workshop_output_total(workshop_id) if GameManager != null else 0

func is_input_buffer_full() -> bool:
	return GameManager.is_workshop_input_full(workshop_id) if GameManager != null else false

func is_output_buffer_full() -> bool:
	return GameManager.is_workshop_output_full(workshop_id) if GameManager != null else false

func get_value_multiplier() -> float:
	if workshop_id == "orbis_linked":
		return EconomyConfig.MULTIPLIER_ORBIS_LINKED
	elif workshop_id == "cauldron":
		return EconomyConfig.MULTIPLIER_CAULDRON
	return EconomyConfig.MULTIPLIER_MORTAR

func get_screen_rect() -> Rect2:
	var w := VISUAL_WIDTH * scale.x
	var h := VISUAL_HEIGHT * scale.y
	return Rect2(position.x - w * 0.5, position.y - h, w, h)

func _on_upgrade_purchased(category: String, key: String, new_level: int) -> void:
	if category == "building" and key == get_building_key():
		if level_label != null:
			level_label.text = "Lv." + str(new_level)
		if visual_upgrader != null:
			visual_upgrader.apply_level_visuals(new_level, true)

func get_evolved_output_delicacy() -> String:
	return EconomyConfig.get_evolved_delicacy_id(workshop_id, get_workshop_level())

func _reload_texture() -> void:
	var frame_h := 58
	if workshop_id == "cauldron":
		tex_building = load("res://assets/pixel_art/buildings/workshop_cauldron.png")
		tex_secondary = null
		frame_h = 62
	elif workshop_id == "orbis_linked":
		tex_building = load("res://assets/pixel_art/buildings/workshop_mortar.png")
		tex_secondary = load("res://assets/pixel_art/buildings/workshop_cauldron.png")
		frame_h = 58
	else:
		tex_building = load("res://assets/pixel_art/buildings/workshop_mortar.png")
		tex_secondary = null
		frame_h = 58

	if body_sprite != null and tex_building != null:
		body_sprite.texture = tex_building
		body_sprite.centered = true
		# GEMINI.md Law 2.2: sprite.offset = Vector2(0.0, -float(frame_h) * 0.5)
		body_sprite.offset = Vector2(0.0, -float(frame_h) * 0.5)

	if secondary_sprite != null:
		secondary_sprite.visible = (tex_secondary != null)
		if tex_secondary != null:
			secondary_sprite.texture = tex_secondary
			secondary_sprite.centered = true
			secondary_sprite.offset = Vector2(0.0, -float(62) * 0.5)

func _process(delta: float) -> void:
	visible = bool(GameManager.unlocked_islands.get(island_idx, false))
	if not visible:
		return

	if is_processing:
		work_anim_time += delta * 10.0
		if body_sprite != null:
			var squash := sin(work_anim_time) * 0.03
			body_sprite.scale = Vector2(BASE_SPRITE_SCALE * (1.0 + squash), BASE_SPRITE_SCALE * (1.0 - squash))
		queue_redraw()
	else:
		if body_sprite != null:
			body_sprite.scale = Vector2(BASE_SPRITE_SCALE, BASE_SPRITE_SCALE)
		if _was_processing:
			queue_redraw()
	_was_processing = is_processing

func _draw() -> void:
	if tex_building == null:
		return
	# GEMINI.md Law 3.3: 5:1 Ultra-Flat Polygon Drop Shadow at origin `Vector2(0, 0)`
	EconomyConfig.draw_drop_shadow(self, VISUAL_WIDTH)

	if is_processing:
		var prog := clampf(process_progress, 0.0, 1.0)
		var bar_w := 36.0
		var bar_h := 5.0
		var bx := -bar_w * 0.5
		var by := -VISUAL_HEIGHT - 10.0
		draw_rect(Rect2(bx - 1.0, by - 1.0, bar_w + 2.0, bar_h + 2.0), Color(0.10, 0.07, 0.04, 0.92), true)
		var fill_col := Color(0.45, 0.95, 0.42, 0.96) if workshop_id == "mortar" else Color(0.42, 0.88, 1.0, 0.96)
		draw_rect(Rect2(bx, by, bar_w * prog, bar_h), fill_col, true)

		# Circular progress ring beside the bar
		var ring_center := Vector2(bx - 9.0, by + 2.5)
		draw_circle(ring_center, 5.5, Color(0.10, 0.07, 0.04, 0.90))
		draw_arc(ring_center, 4.2, -PI * 0.5, -PI * 0.5 + prog * TAU, 16, Color("#FFE066"), 1.6)

## Per-Island Multi-Slot Specialized Pipeline Animal Worker (`src/entities/units/AnimalWorker.gd`)

func get_pet_hit_rect() -> Rect2:

func trigger_pet_boost() -> void:
	if not visible:
		return
	boost_timer = 15.0
	if SoundManager != null:
		SoundManager.play_pop_sfx()
	EventBus.spawn_floating_text.emit(position + Vector2(0.0, -64.0), "★摸头元气+50%!", Color("#FFE066"))

func setup_worker(p_role: RoleType, p_island: int, p_slot: int, p_pos: Vector2, p_graph: WaypointGraph, p_world: Node2D) -> void:
	role_type = p_role
	home_island = p_island
	slot_index = p_slot
	waypoint_graph = p_graph
	world_ref = p_world
	match role_type:
		RoleType.BUNNY_FARMER:
			animal_key = "bunny"
		RoleType.OTTER_LOGISTICS:
			animal_key = "otter"
		RoleType.TOUCAN_TRADER:
			animal_key = "toucan"
		RoleType.FOX_MORTAR_CHEF:
			animal_key = "fox"
		RoleType.PENGUIN_ALCHEMIST:
			animal_key = "penguin"
		RoleType.SHIBA_MANAGER:
			animal_key = "shiba"
	var cfg: Dictionary = EconomyConfig.ANIMALS[animal_key]
	sheet_key = String(cfg["sheet"])
	var m_entry := EconomyConfig.get_manifest_entry(sheet_key)
	var norm_path := String(m_entry.get("normalized_sheet", "res://assets/pixel_art/" + sheet_key))
	tex_sheet = load(norm_path)
	if body_sprite != null:
		body_sprite.texture = tex_sheet
		body_sprite.region_enabled = false
		body_sprite.hframes = 4
		body_sprite.vframes = 1
		body_sprite.frame = 0
		body_sprite.offset = Vector2(0.0, -float(FRAME_H) * 0.5)
	position = Vector2(p_pos.x, EconomyConfig.GROUND_Y)
	anim_time = randf() * 3.14
	_was_hired = is_hired()
	visible = _was_hired
	if _was_hired:
		_assign_next_task()

func get_level() -> int:
	return maxi(1, int(GameManager.animal_levels.get(animal_key, 1)))

func get_effective_harvest_bonus() -> int:
	return EconomyConfig.calc_bunny_harvest_trait_bonus(get_level())

func is_hired() -> bool:
	if not bool(GameManager.unlocked_islands.get(home_island, false)):
		return false
	return GameManager.get_island_recruited_count(home_island, animal_key) > slot_index

func _set_carry_visual(item_id: String, qty: int) -> void:
	carrying_item_id = item_id
	carrying_qty = qty
	if item_id == "" or qty <= 0:
		carry_icon.visible = false
		carry_front_grip.visible = false
		return
	carry_icon.visible = true
	carry_icon.region_enabled = false
	if item_id.begins_with("delicacy_"):
		var d_data: Dictionary = EconomyConfig.DELICACIES.get(item_id, EconomyConfig.DELICACIES["delicacy_0"])
		carry_icon.texture = tex_delicacies
		carry_icon.hframes = 4
		carry_icon.vframes = 1
		carry_icon.frame = clampi(int(d_data.get("idx", 0)), 0, 3)
		carry_icon.scale = Vector2(0.50, 0.50)
	else:
		var c_data: Dictionary = EconomyConfig.CROPS.get(item_id, EconomyConfig.CROPS["crop_0"])
		var row := clampi(int(c_data.get("crop_row", 0)), 0, 3)
		carry_icon.texture = tex_crops
		carry_icon.hframes = 4
		carry_icon.vframes = 4
		carry_icon.frame = row * 4 + 3
		carry_icon.scale = Vector2(0.44, 0.44)

	carry_front_grip.visible = true
	carry_front_grip.region_enabled = false
	carry_front_grip.hframes = 3
	carry_front_grip.vframes = 1
	if role_type == RoleType.OTTER_LOGISTICS:
		carry_front_grip.frame = 1
	elif role_type == RoleType.TOUCAN_TRADER:
		carry_front_grip.frame = 2
	else:
		carry_front_grip.frame = 0
	_sync_carry_rig_transform(0.0)

func _sync_carry_rig_transform(hop_y: float) -> void:
	var dir_sign := -1.0 if facing_left else 1.0
	var rig_x := 14.0
	var rig_y := -18.0
	var item_x := 15.0
	var item_y := -22.0
	if role_type == RoleType.OTTER_LOGISTICS:
		rig_x = 15.0
		rig_y = -18.0
		item_x = 16.0
		item_y = -21.0
	elif role_type == RoleType.TOUCAN_TRADER:
		rig_x = 14.0
		rig_y = -15.0
		item_x = 15.0
		item_y = -19.0

	if carry_icon != null and carry_icon.visible:
		carry_icon.position = Vector2(dir_sign * item_x, item_y + hop_y)
		carry_icon.flip_h = facing_left
		carry_icon.rotation = 0.0

	if carry_front_grip != null and carry_front_grip.visible:
		carry_front_grip.position = Vector2(dir_sign * rig_x, rig_y + hop_y)
		carry_front_grip.flip_h = facing_left
		carry_front_grip.rotation = 0.0

func _navigate_to(dest: Vector2) -> void:
	var grounded_dest := Vector2(dest.x, EconomyConfig.GROUND_Y)
	if waypoint_graph != null:
		nav_path = waypoint_graph.compute_path(position, grounded_dest)
	else:
		nav_path = [grounded_dest]

func _get_home_workshop_id() -> String:
	if home_island == 1:
		return "mortar"
	elif home_island == 2:
		return "cauldron"
	return "orbis_linked"

func _get_home_workshop_pos() -> Vector2:
	if home_island == 1:
		return waypoint_graph.get_named_pos("mortar")
	elif home_island == 2:
		return waypoint_graph.get_named_pos("cauldron")
	return waypoint_graph.get_named_pos("orbis_linked")

func _get_workshop_dock_pos(dock_suffix: String) -> Vector2:
	var prefix := "mortar"
	if home_island == 2:
		prefix = "cauldron"
	elif home_island == 3:
		prefix = "orbis"
	if waypoint_graph != null:
		if home_island == 3 and dock_suffix == "chef":
			var chef_dock := "orbis_chef_penguin" if role_type == RoleType.PENGUIN_ALCHEMIST else "orbis_chef_fox"
			return waypoint_graph.get_dock_pos(chef_dock)
		return waypoint_graph.get_dock_pos(prefix + "_" + dock_suffix)
	return _get_home_workshop_pos()

func _get_home_basket_pos() -> Vector2:
	if waypoint_graph != null:
		return waypoint_graph.get_dock_pos("basket_%d_pickup" % home_island)
	return Vector2(785.2, EconomyConfig.GROUND_Y)

func _is_home_chef_hired() -> bool:
	if home_island == 1:
		return GameManager.get_island_recruited_count(1, "fox") >= 1
	elif home_island == 2:
		return GameManager.get_island_recruited_count(2, "penguin") >= 1
	return GameManager.get_island_recruited_count(3, "fox") >= 1 or GameManager.get_island_recruited_count(3, "penguin") >= 1

func _try_deposit_to_stall(item_id: String, qty: int, _float_prefix: String, _float_col: Color) -> bool:
	var safe_id := item_id if item_id != "" else "crop_0"
	var safe_qty := maxi(1, qty)
	var accepted := GameManager.add_to_stall(safe_id, safe_qty)
	var rem := safe_qty - accepted
	if rem > 0:
		# Route any stall-cap overflow back into the home buffer so no items are lost and workers never loiter at the stall counter
		if safe_id.begins_with("delicacy_"):
			GameManager.add_to_workshop_output(_get_home_workshop_id(), safe_id, rem)
		else:
			GameManager.add_to_raw_basket(home_island, safe_id, rem)
	_set_carry_visual("", 0)
	is_sleeping_full = false
	return true

func _process(delta: float) -> void:
	var now_hired := is_hired()
	if now_hired and not _was_hired:
		_was_hired = true
		visible = true
		_assign_next_task()
		queue_redraw()
	elif not now_hired and _was_hired:
		_was_hired = false
		visible = false
		queue_redraw()
	if not visible:
		return

	anim_time += delta
	if boost_timer > 0.0:
		boost_timer = maxf(0.0, boost_timer - delta)

	var haste_mult := 1.0
	if boost_timer > 0.0:
		haste_mult *= 1.5
	if GameManager != null and GameManager.frenzy_timer > 0.0:
		haste_mult *= 1.5

	if nav_path.size() > 0:
		is_moving = true
		var spd := EconomyConfig.calc_move_speed(animal_key, get_level()) * haste_mult
		var rem_move := spd * delta
		while rem_move > 0.0 and nav_path.size() > 0:
			var wp := Vector2(nav_path[0].x, EconomyConfig.GROUND_Y)
			var diff_x := wp.x - position.x
			var dist := absf(diff_x)
			if absf(diff_x) > 0.2:
				facing_left = diff_x < 0.0
			if dist <= rem_move or dist <= 1.5:
				total_distance_moved += dist
				position = wp
				rem_move -= dist
				nav_path.remove_at(0)
			else:
				var step_x := signf(diff_x) * rem_move
				total_distance_moved += absf(step_x)
				position = Vector2(position.x + step_x, EconomyConfig.GROUND_Y)
				rem_move = 0.0
		if nav_path.size() == 0:
			is_moving = false
			state_timer = 0.0
	else:
		is_moving = false
		position.y = EconomyConfig.GROUND_Y
		state_timer += delta * haste_mult
		_update_role_work_step(delta * haste_mult)

	var pose_idx := _get_pose_index()
	var hop_y := 0.0
	var scale_x := 1.0
	var scale_y := 1.0
	if is_moving:
		hop_y = -absf(sin(anim_time * 8.5)) * 2.5
		var squash := cos(anim_time * 17.0)
		scale_x = 1.0 - squash * 0.03
		scale_y = 1.0 + squash * 0.03
	else:
		var breath := sin(anim_time * 3.2)
		scale_x = 1.0 + breath * 0.02
		scale_y = 1.0 - breath * 0.02

	rotation = 0.0
	if body_sprite != null and tex_sheet != null:
		body_sprite.frame = clampi(pose_idx, 0, 2)
		body_sprite.flip_h = facing_left
		body_sprite.position = Vector2(0.0, hop_y)
		body_sprite.rotation = 0.0
		body_sprite.scale = Vector2(scale_x, scale_y)

	if carry_icon != null and carry_icon.visible:
		_sync_carry_rig_transform(hop_y)

	if heart_icon != null:
		heart_icon.visible = (boost_timer > 0.0)
		if heart_icon.visible:
			heart_icon.position = Vector2(0.0, -62.0 + hop_y + sin(anim_time * 6.0) * 2.0)
			heart_icon.rotation = 0.0

	if sleep_label != null:
		sleep_label.visible = is_sleeping_full and (boost_timer <= 0.0)
		if sleep_label.visible:
			sleep_label.position = Vector2(-30.0, -68.0 + sin(anim_time * 2.5) * 2.0)

func _assign_next_task() -> void:
	state_timer = 0.0
	is_sleeping_full = false
	work_duration = EconomyConfig.calc_work_duration(animal_key, get_level())

	match role_type:
		RoleType.BUNNY_FARMER:
			target_plot = world_ref.find_best_plot_for_island(home_island) if (world_ref != null and world_ref.has_method("find_best_plot_for_island")) else null
			var soil_dock_key := "soil_%d_slot_%d" % [home_island, clampi(slot_index, 0, 1)]
			var dock_dest := waypoint_graph.get_dock_pos(soil_dock_key) if waypoint_graph != null else (target_plot.position if target_plot != null else position)
			var otter_on_isl := GameManager.is_island_otter_hired(home_island)
			var downstream_blocked := GameManager.is_raw_basket_full(home_island) if (otter_on_isl or home_island != 1) else GameManager.is_stall_full()
			if downstream_blocked:
				is_sleeping_full = true
				worker_step = 99
				_navigate_to(dock_dest)
				return

			worker_step = 0
			_navigate_to(dock_dest)

		RoleType.OTTER_LOGISTICS:
			var wid := _get_home_workshop_id()
			var can_deliver_output := (home_island == 1) and (GameManager.get_workshop_output_total(wid) > 0) and (not GameManager.is_stall_full())
			var can_feed_workshop := _is_home_chef_hired() and (not GameManager.is_workshop_input_full(wid))
			var can_divert_to_stall := not GameManager.is_stall_full()
			if can_deliver_output:
				worker_step = 10
				_navigate_to(_get_workshop_dock_pos("output"))
			elif GameManager.get_island_basket_total(home_island) > 0 and (can_feed_workshop or can_divert_to_stall):
				worker_step = 0
				_navigate_to(_get_home_basket_pos() + Vector2(14.0 + float(slot_index * 8), 0.0))
			else:
				worker_step = 20
				is_sleeping_full = (GameManager.get_island_basket_total(home_island) > 0 and not can_feed_workshop and not can_divert_to_stall) or (home_island == 1 and GameManager.get_workshop_output_total(wid) > 0 and GameManager.is_stall_full())
				_navigate_to(_get_home_basket_pos() + Vector2(14.0 + float(slot_index * 8), 0.0))

		RoleType.TOUCAN_TRADER:
			var wid := "orbis_linked" if home_island == 3 else "cauldron"
			var can_take_raw_fallback := (not _is_home_chef_hired()) or GameManager.is_workshop_input_full(wid) or (not GameManager.is_island_otter_hired(home_island))
			if GameManager.get_workshop_output_total(wid) > 0 and not GameManager.is_stall_full():
				worker_step = 10
				_navigate_to(_get_workshop_dock_pos("output"))
			elif GameManager.get_island_basket_total(home_island) > 0 and can_take_raw_fallback and not GameManager.is_stall_full():
				worker_step = 0
				_navigate_to(_get_home_basket_pos())
			else:
				worker_step = 40
				is_sleeping_full = GameManager.is_stall_full() and (GameManager.get_workshop_output_total(wid) > 0 or GameManager.get_island_basket_total(home_island) > 0)
				_navigate_to(_get_workshop_dock_pos("output") + Vector2(float(slot_index * 12), 0.0))

		RoleType.FOX_MORTAR_CHEF:
			worker_step = 0
			_navigate_to(_get_workshop_dock_pos("chef"))

		RoleType.PENGUIN_ALCHEMIST:
			worker_step = 0
			_navigate_to(_get_workshop_dock_pos("chef"))

		RoleType.SHIBA_MANAGER:
			worker_step = 0
			_navigate_to(waypoint_graph.get_dock_pos("stall_manager") if waypoint_graph != null else (waypoint_graph.get_named_pos("market_stall") + Vector2(22.0, 0.0)))

func _update_role_work_step(eff_delta: float = 0.016) -> void:
	match role_type:
		RoleType.BUNNY_FARMER:
			if worker_step == 99:
				var otter_on_isl := GameManager.is_island_otter_hired(home_island)
				var still_full := GameManager.is_raw_basket_full(home_island) if (otter_on_isl or home_island != 1) else GameManager.is_stall_full()
				if not still_full and state_timer >= 0.6:
					is_sleeping_full = false
					_assign_next_task()
				return

			if worker_step == 0:
				facing_left = (slot_index > 0)
				if is_instance_valid(target_plot):
					target_plot.apply_farmer_tending(eff_delta)
				var plot_ready := (not is_instance_valid(target_plot)) or target_plot.is_ready_for_harvest() or (state_timer >= work_duration * 1.15)
				if state_timer >= work_duration * 0.65 and plot_ready:
					var b_lv := int(GameManager.building_levels.get("soil_" + str(home_island), 1))
					var crop_got := EconomyConfig.get_evolved_crop_id(home_island, b_lv)
					var qty_got := 1
					if is_instance_valid(target_plot):
						crop_got = target_plot.crop_id
						qty_got = target_plot.harvest_crop_yield()
					qty_got += get_effective_harvest_bonus()

					_set_carry_visual(crop_got, qty_got)
					if home_island == 1 and not GameManager.is_island_otter_hired(1):
						worker_step = 2
						_navigate_to(waypoint_graph.get_dock_pos("stall_restock"))
					else:
						worker_step = 1
						_navigate_to(_get_home_basket_pos() + Vector2(-14.0 + float(slot_index * 6), 0.0))

			elif worker_step == 1:
				facing_left = false
				if state_timer >= 0.35:
					var cid := carrying_item_id if carrying_item_id != "" else "crop_0"
					var q := maxi(1, carrying_qty)
					var accepted := GameManager.add_to_raw_basket(home_island, cid, q)
					var rem := q - accepted
					if rem > 0:
						_set_carry_visual(cid, rem)
						is_sleeping_full = true
						state_timer = 0.0
					else:
						_set_carry_visual("", 0)
						is_sleeping_full = false
						_assign_next_task()

			elif worker_step == 2:
				facing_left = false
				if state_timer >= 0.45:
					if _try_deposit_to_stall(carrying_item_id, carrying_qty, "鲜果直售+", Color("#FFE066")):
						_assign_next_task()

		RoleType.OTTER_LOGISTICS:
			var cap := EconomyConfig.calc_carry_capacity("otter", get_level())
			var wid := _get_home_workshop_id()
			if worker_step == 20:
				if state_timer >= 0.7:
					_assign_next_task()
				return

			if worker_step == 0:
				facing_left = true
				if state_timer >= 0.40:
					var can_feed := _is_home_chef_hired() and (not GameManager.is_workshop_input_full(wid))
					var can_stall := not GameManager.is_stall_full()
					if not can_feed and not can_stall:
						_assign_next_task()
						return
					var taken := GameManager.take_from_raw_basket(home_island, cap)
					if taken.is_empty():
						_assign_next_task()
						return
					var item_id := String(taken["item_id"])
					var qty := int(taken["qty"])
					_set_carry_visual(item_id, qty)

					if can_feed:
						worker_step = 1
						_navigate_to(_get_workshop_dock_pos("input"))
					else:
						worker_step = 2
						_navigate_to(waypoint_graph.get_dock_pos("stall_restock"))

			elif worker_step == 1:
				facing_left = false
				if state_timer >= 0.40:
					var cid := carrying_item_id if carrying_item_id != "" else "crop_0"
					var q := maxi(1, carrying_qty)
					var accepted := GameManager.add_to_workshop_input(wid, cid, q)
					var leftover := q - accepted
					if leftover > 0:
						_set_carry_visual(cid, leftover)
						worker_step = 2
						_navigate_to(waypoint_graph.get_dock_pos("stall_restock"))
					else:
						_set_carry_visual("", 0)
						_assign_next_task()

			elif worker_step == 2:
				facing_left = false
				if state_timer >= 0.45:
					if _try_deposit_to_stall(carrying_item_id, carrying_qty, "分流直售+", Color("#FFF4D6")):
						_assign_next_task()

			elif worker_step == 10:
				facing_left = true
				if state_timer >= 0.40:
					if GameManager.is_stall_full():
						_assign_next_task()
						return
					var out_taken := GameManager.take_from_workshop_output(wid, cap)
					if out_taken.is_empty():
						_assign_next_task()
						return
					_set_carry_visual(String(out_taken["item_id"]), int(out_taken["qty"]))
					worker_step = 11
					_navigate_to(waypoint_graph.get_dock_pos("stall_restock"))

			elif worker_step == 11:
				facing_left = false
				if state_timer >= 0.45:
					if _try_deposit_to_stall(carrying_item_id, carrying_qty, "3.5x美食+", Color("#FFE066")):
						_assign_next_task()

		RoleType.TOUCAN_TRADER:
			var cap := EconomyConfig.calc_carry_capacity("toucan", get_level())
			var wid := "orbis_linked" if home_island == 3 else "cauldron"
			if worker_step == 40:
				if state_timer >= 0.8:
					_assign_next_task()
				return

			if worker_step == 0:
				facing_left = true
				if state_timer >= 0.40:
					if GameManager.is_stall_full():
						_assign_next_task()
						return
					var taken := GameManager.take_from_raw_basket(home_island, cap)
					if taken.is_empty():
						_assign_next_task()
						return
					_set_carry_visual(String(taken["item_id"]), int(taken["qty"]))
					worker_step = 11
					_navigate_to(waypoint_graph.get_dock_pos("stall_restock"))

			elif worker_step == 10:
				facing_left = true
				if state_timer >= 0.40:
					if GameManager.is_stall_full():
						_assign_next_task()
						return
					var out_taken := GameManager.take_from_workshop_output(wid, cap)
					if out_taken.is_empty():
						_assign_next_task()
						return
					_set_carry_visual(String(out_taken["item_id"]), int(out_taken["qty"]))
					worker_step = 11
					_navigate_to(waypoint_graph.get_dock_pos("stall_restock"))

			elif worker_step == 11:
				facing_left = false
				if state_timer >= 0.45:
					var did := carrying_item_id if carrying_item_id != "" else "delicacy_2"
					var tag := "跨岛鲜果+"
					if did.begins_with("delicacy_"):
						tag = "52x盛宴+" if home_island == 3 else "12x甘露+"
					if _try_deposit_to_stall(did, carrying_qty, tag, Color("#FFE066")):
						_assign_next_task()

		RoleType.FOX_MORTAR_CHEF, RoleType.PENGUIN_ALCHEMIST:
			_update_chef_work_step()

		RoleType.SHIBA_MANAGER:
			facing_left = false

func _update_chef_work_step() -> void:
	var is_penguin := (role_type == RoleType.PENGUIN_ALCHEMIST)
	var wid := "orbis_linked" if home_island == 3 else ("cauldron" if is_penguin else "mortar")
	var w_node: Workshop = null
	if world_ref != null:
		if home_island == 3:
			w_node = world_ref.orbis_linked_workshop
		elif is_penguin:
			w_node = world_ref.cauldron_workshop
		else:
			w_node = world_ref.mortar_workshop

	# Face toward the center of the workshop (`fox`/`cauldron_chef` at dx=-20 face right; on Island 3 `penguin` at dx=+18 faces left)
	facing_left = (home_island == 3 and is_penguin)
	var chef_slot_key := animal_key + "_" + str(home_island)

	if worker_step == 0:
		if w_node != null:
			w_node.set_chef_progress(chef_slot_key, 0.0, false)
		if GameManager.is_workshop_output_full(wid):
			is_sleeping_full = (GameManager.get_workshop_input_total(wid) > 0)
			state_timer = 0.0
			return
		var claimed := GameManager.take_workshop_input_for_processing(wid)
		if claimed == "":
			is_sleeping_full = false
			state_timer = 0.0
			return
		cooking_crop_id = claimed
		is_sleeping_full = false
		worker_step = 1
		state_timer = 0.0

	elif worker_step == 1:
		var w_speed_mult := GameManager.get_workshop_speed_multiplier(home_island) if GameManager != null else 1.0
		var eff_duration := maxf(0.4, work_duration / w_speed_mult)
		var prog := clampf(state_timer / eff_duration, 0.0, 1.0)
		if w_node != null:
			w_node.set_chef_progress(chef_slot_key, prog, true)
		if state_timer >= eff_duration:
			worker_step = 2
			state_timer = 0.0

	elif worker_step == 2:
		if GameManager.is_workshop_output_full(wid):
			is_sleeping_full = true
			if w_node != null:
				w_node.set_chef_progress(chef_slot_key, 1.0, false)
			return
		var default_del := "delicacy_2" if is_penguin else "delicacy_0"
		var out_del := w_node.get_evolved_output_delicacy() if w_node != null else default_del
		var added := GameManager.add_to_workshop_output(wid, out_del, 1)
		if added > 0:
			cooking_crop_id = ""
			is_sleeping_full = false
			if w_node != null:
				w_node.crafted_count += 1
				w_node.set_chef_progress(chef_slot_key, 0.0, false)
			worker_step = 0
			state_timer = 0.0

func _get_pose_index() -> int:
	# Strictly keep all animal poses upright (0 = Idle/Stand, 1 = Walk, 2 = Active Work).
	# Never select frame 3 (horizontal lying-down sleep pose) to prevent sudden 90-degree sideways sprite flips.
	if is_sleeping_full or worker_step == 20 or worker_step == 40 or worker_step == 99:
		return 0
	if is_moving:
		return int(floor(anim_time * 7.5 / PI)) % 2
	if role_type == RoleType.SHIBA_MANAGER:
		return 0
	return 2 if (int(floor(anim_time * 4.2)) % 2 == 0) else 0

func _draw() -> void:
	if not is_hired():
		return
	# GEMINI.md Law 3.3: 5:1 Ultra-Flat Polygon Drop Shadow at origin `Vector2(0, 0)`
	EconomyConfig.draw_drop_shadow(self, VISUAL_WIDTH)

extends Node2D
class_name CustomerNPC
## Long-Cycle Island Customer & Crown VIP Tycoon (`src/entities/units/CustomerNPC.gd`)
## Strictly enforces GEMINI.md Laws 2, 3, 4, 5:
## - Root `position.y = GROUND_Y` (`z_index = 10`, `z_as_relative = false`).
## - `body_sprite`: `region_enabled = false`, `hframes = 2`, `vframes = 1`, `frame = frame_idx` (`48x48` per frame, aspect 1.0).
## - `body_sprite`: `centered = true`, `offset = Vector2(0.0, -float(48) * 0.5) = Vector2(0.0, -24.0)`.
## - 5:1 Ultra-Flat Polygon Drop Shadow at `Vector2(0, 0)` (`shadow_width = 40 * 0.55`, `shadow_height = shadow_width * 0.18`, `Color(0, 0, 0, 0.20)`).

enum State {
	SPAWN_AND_STROLL,
	QUEUE_AT_STALL,
	PURCHASE,
	LEAVE
}

	"travelers/traveler_mushroom.png",
	"travelers/traveler_pig.png"
]
