func _ready() -> void:
	z_index = EconomyConfig.Z_UNIT
	z_as_relative = false

	# GEMINI.md Law 2.2 & Law 4: Fixed grid slicing (`hframes = 2, vframes = 1, region_enabled = false`) + Bottom-Center Pivot
	body_sprite.name = "BodySprite"
	if tex_sheet != null:
		body_sprite.texture = tex_sheet
	body_sprite.region_enabled = false
	body_sprite.hframes = 2
	body_sprite.vframes = 1
	body_sprite.frame = 0
	body_sprite.centered = true
	body_sprite.offset = Vector2(0.0, -float(FRAME_H) * 0.5)
	body_sprite.z_index = EconomyConfig.Z_UNIT
	body_sprite.z_as_relative = false
	add_child(body_sprite)

	bubble_label = EconomyConfig.create_pixel_label("x1", 14, Color("#FFF4D6"), false)
	bubble_label.position = Vector2(2.0, -76.0)
	bubble_label.z_index = EconomyConfig.Z_CARRY_EMOTE
	bubble_label.z_as_relative = false
	add_child(bubble_label)

	tex_parchment = load("res://assets/pixel_art/ui/panel_parchment_9patch.png")
	tex_delicacies = load("res://assets/pixel_art/buildings/delicacies_sheet_normalized.png")
	tex_crops = load("res://assets/pixel_art/crops/crops_sheet_normalized.png")
	tex_heart = load("res://assets/pixel_art/ui/emote_heart.png")

func setup_customer(p_spawn: Vector2, p_graph: WaypointGraph, p_stall: MarketStall, p_is_vip: bool = false) -> void:
	waypoint_graph = p_graph
	stall_ref = p_stall
	is_vip = p_is_vip
	spawn_pos = Vector2(p_spawn.x, EconomyConfig.GROUND_Y)
	position = spawn_pos

	var t_idx := 2 if is_vip else (randi() % TRAVELER_KEYS.size())
	sheet_key = TRAVELER_KEYS[t_idx]
	tex_sheet = load(TRAVELER_NORMALIZED_PATHS[t_idx])
	if body_sprite != null:
		body_sprite.texture = tex_sheet
		body_sprite.region_enabled = false
		body_sprite.hframes = 2
		body_sprite.vframes = 1
		body_sprite.frame = 0
		body_sprite.offset = Vector2(0.0, -float(FRAME_H) * 0.5)

	if is_vip:
		if bool(GameManager.unlocked_islands.get(3, false)) and (GameManager.get_island_recruited_count(3, "fox") >= 1 or GameManager.get_island_recruited_count(3, "penguin") >= 1):
			desired_item_id = "delicacy_3"
		elif bool(GameManager.unlocked_islands.get(2, false)) and GameManager.get_island_recruited_count(2, "penguin") >= 1:
			desired_item_id = "delicacy_2"
		elif GameManager.is_animal_hired("fox"):
			desired_item_id = EconomyConfig.get_evolved_delicacy_id("mortar", int(GameManager.building_levels.get("mortar", 1)))
		else:
			desired_item_id = EconomyConfig.get_evolved_crop_id(1, int(GameManager.building_levels.get("soil_1", 1)))
		desired_qty = randi_range(3, 5)
	else:
		var pool: Array[String] = []
		for item_k in ["crop_0", "crop_1", "crop_2", "crop_3", "delicacy_0", "delicacy_1", "delicacy_2", "delicacy_3"]:
			if bool(GameManager.unlocked_codex.get(item_k, false)):
				pool.append(item_k)
		if pool.is_empty():
			pool.append("crop_0")
		desired_item_id = pool[randi() % pool.size()]
		desired_qty = randi_range(1, 3)

	state = State.SPAWN_AND_STROLL
	var approach_dest := waypoint_graph.get_dock_pos("stall_checkout") if waypoint_graph != null else Vector2(997.4, EconomyConfig.GROUND_Y)
	nav_path = waypoint_graph.compute_path(position, approach_dest) if waypoint_graph != null else [approach_dest]

func _exit_tree() -> void:
	if stall_ref != null:
		stall_ref.unregister_from_queue(self)

func _process(delta: float) -> void:
	anim_time += delta

	match state:
		State.SPAWN_AND_STROLL:
			if _follow_path(delta, 42.0):
				if stall_ref != null:
					stall_ref.register_in_queue(self)
				state = State.QUEUE_AT_STALL
				wait_timer = 0.0

		State.QUEUE_AT_STALL:
			if stall_ref != null:
				var slot_pos := stall_ref.get_queue_slot_pos(self)
				if absf(position.x - slot_pos.x) > 2.5:
					_move_directly_toward(slot_pos, delta, 38.0)
				else:
					facing_left = (position.x > stall_ref.position.x)
				if stall_ref.is_front_of_queue(self):
					state = State.PURCHASE
					interact_timer = 0.0
					wait_timer = 0.0
			else:
				state = State.PURCHASE

		State.PURCHASE:
			if stall_ref != null:
				var front_pos := stall_ref.get_queue_slot_pos(self)
				if absf(position.x - front_pos.x) > 2.5:
					_move_directly_toward(front_pos, delta, 38.0)
				else:
					facing_left = (position.x > stall_ref.position.x)
			var has_stock := stall_ref.has_available_stock() if stall_ref != null else (GameManager.get_total_stall_count() > 0)
			if has_stock:
				var frenzy_mult := 2.0 if (GameManager != null and GameManager.frenzy_timer > 0.0) else 1.0
				interact_timer += delta * frenzy_mult
				var req_time := stall_ref.get_checkout_duration() if stall_ref != null else (1.0 if GameManager.is_animal_hired("shiba") else 1.8)
				if interact_timer >= req_time:
					var earned := stall_ref.execute_customer_checkout(desired_item_id, desired_qty, position, is_vip) if stall_ref != null else GameManager.try_purchase_from_stall(desired_item_id, desired_qty, position, is_vip)
					if earned > 0:
						purchased_ok = true
						if is_vip:
							EventBus.spawn_floating_text.emit(position + Vector2(0, -76), "★富豪2x小费!", Color("#FFE066"))
						_begin_leave()
			else:
				wait_timer += delta
				if wait_timer >= 12.0:
					disappointed = true
					EventBus.customer_left_unhappy.emit(position)
					EventBus.spawn_floating_text.emit(position + Vector2(0, -54), "...缺货离开", Color("#FFF4D6"))
					_begin_leave()

		State.LEAVE:
			if _follow_path(delta, 42.0):
				fade_alpha -= delta * 1.8
				modulate.a = maxf(0.0, fade_alpha)
				if fade_alpha <= 0.02:
					queue_free()

	var is_walking := (state == State.SPAWN_AND_STROLL or state == State.LEAVE) and nav_path.size() > 0
	var frame_idx := (int(floor(anim_time * 6.0 / PI)) % 2) if is_walking else 0
	var hop_y := -absf(sin(anim_time * 6.0)) * 2.2 if is_walking else 0.0

	rotation = 0.0
	if body_sprite != null and tex_sheet != null:
		body_sprite.frame = clampi(frame_idx, 0, 1)
		body_sprite.flip_h = facing_left
		body_sprite.position = Vector2(0.0, hop_y)
		body_sprite.rotation = 0.0

	if bubble_label != null:
		if state == State.SPAWN_AND_STROLL or state == State.QUEUE_AT_STALL or state == State.PURCHASE:
			bubble_label.visible = true
			bubble_label.text = "x" + str(desired_qty)
			bubble_label.position = Vector2(2.0, -78.0 + hop_y)
		elif state == State.LEAVE and disappointed:
			bubble_label.visible = true
			bubble_label.text = "..."
			bubble_label.position = Vector2(-8.0, -72.0 + hop_y)
		else:
			bubble_label.visible = false

	queue_redraw()

func _begin_leave() -> void:
	if stall_ref != null:
		stall_ref.unregister_from_queue(self)
	state = State.LEAVE
	nav_path = waypoint_graph.compute_path(position, spawn_pos) if waypoint_graph != null else [spawn_pos]

func _follow_path(delta: float, spd: float) -> bool:
	if nav_path.size() == 0:
		return true
	var rem_move := spd * delta
	while rem_move > 0.0 and nav_path.size() > 0:
		var wp := Vector2(nav_path[0].x, EconomyConfig.GROUND_Y)
		var diff_x := wp.x - position.x
		var dist := absf(diff_x)
		if absf(diff_x) > 0.2:
			facing_left = diff_x < 0.0
		if dist <= rem_move or dist <= 1.5:
			position = wp
			rem_move -= dist
			nav_path.remove_at(0)
		else:
			position = Vector2(position.x + signf(diff_x) * rem_move, EconomyConfig.GROUND_Y)
			rem_move = 0.0
	return nav_path.size() == 0

func _move_directly_toward(dest: Vector2, delta: float, spd: float) -> void:
	var diff_x := dest.x - position.x
	if absf(diff_x) > 1.5:
		facing_left = diff_x < 0.0
		position = Vector2(position.x + signf(diff_x) * minf(absf(diff_x), spd * delta), EconomyConfig.GROUND_Y)

func _draw_vip_crown(top_y: float) -> void:
	var cx := 0.0
	var cy := top_y
	draw_rect(Rect2(cx - 9, cy - 6, 18, 6), Color(1.0, 0.84, 0.16, 0.98), true)
	draw_colored_polygon(PackedVector2Array([
		Vector2(cx - 9, cy - 6), Vector2(cx - 9, cy - 12), Vector2(cx - 4, cy - 7),
		Vector2(cx, cy - 14), Vector2(cx + 4, cy - 7), Vector2(cx + 9, cy - 12), Vector2(cx + 9, cy - 6)
	]), Color(1.0, 0.90, 0.25, 0.98))
	draw_circle(Vector2(cx, cy - 4), 2.2, Color(0.96, 0.18, 0.28, 1.0))

func _draw() -> void:
	if tex_sheet == null:
		return
	var is_walking := (state == State.SPAWN_AND_STROLL or state == State.LEAVE) and nav_path.size() > 0
	var hop_y := -absf(sin(anim_time * 6.0)) * 2.2 if is_walking else 0.0

	# GEMINI.md Law 3.3: 5:1 Ultra-Flat Polygon Drop Shadow at origin `Vector2(0, 0)`
	EconomyConfig.draw_drop_shadow(self, VISUAL_WIDTH)

	if is_vip:
		_draw_vip_crown(-46.0 + hop_y)

	if state == State.SPAWN_AND_STROLL or state == State.QUEUE_AT_STALL or state == State.PURCHASE:
		if tex_parchment != null:
			draw_texture_rect(tex_parchment, Rect2(-24, -78 + hop_y, 54, 26), false)
		if desired_item_id.begins_with("delicacy_") and tex_delicacies != null:
			var d_reg := EconomyConfig.get_delicacy_region(desired_item_id)
			draw_texture_rect_region(tex_delicacies, Rect2(-20, -75 + hop_y, 20, 20), d_reg)
		elif tex_crops != null:
			var c_reg := EconomyConfig.get_crop_stage_region(desired_item_id, 3)
			draw_texture_rect_region(tex_crops, Rect2(-20, -75 + hop_y, 20, 20), c_reg)
	elif state == State.LEAVE:
		if purchased_ok and tex_heart != null:
			var heart_y := -80.0 if is_vip else -68.0
			draw_texture_rect(tex_heart, Rect2(-10, heart_y + hop_y, 20, 20), false)
		elif disappointed and tex_parchment != null:
			draw_texture_rect(tex_parchment, Rect2(-16, -72 + hop_y, 32, 22), false)

=== FILE: D:/project/maple-isle-idle/src/ui/FloatingText.gd ===
extends Node2D
class_name FloatingText
## Native Label Floating Text & Parabolic Gold Coin Effect (`src/ui/FloatingText.gd`)
## Strictly enforces GEMINI.md Law 2.3 (`z_index = 50`) & Law 5 (`EconomyConfig.create_pixel_label`).

const MAX_POOL_SIZE := 24

var active_labels: Array[Dictionary] = []
var _label_pool: Array[Label] = []
var coin_arcs: Array[Dictionary] = []
var tex_icons: Texture2D

func _ready() -> void:
	z_index = EconomyConfig.Z_FLOATING_UI
	z_as_relative = false
	tex_icons = load("res://assets/pixel_art/ui/icons_ui.png")
	EventBus.spawn_floating_text.connect(_on_spawn_floating_text)
	EventBus.customer_paid.connect(_on_customer_paid)

func _acquire_pooled_label(text_str: String, final_col: Color) -> Label:
	var lbl: Label = null
	while _label_pool.size() > 0 and lbl == null:
		var cand: Label = _label_pool.pop_back()
		if is_instance_valid(cand):
			lbl = cand
	if lbl == null:
		lbl = EconomyConfig.create_pixel_label(text_str, 14, final_col, true)
		lbl.custom_minimum_size = Vector2(120.0, 22.0)
		lbl.z_index = EconomyConfig.Z_FLOATING_UI
		lbl.z_as_relative = false
		add_child(lbl)
	else:
		lbl.text = text_str
		lbl.label_settings = EconomyConfig.make_pixel_label_settings(14, final_col)
		lbl.visible = true
	lbl.modulate.a = 1.0
	return lbl

func _release_pooled_label(lbl: Label) -> void:
	if not is_instance_valid(lbl):
		return
	if _label_pool.size() < MAX_POOL_SIZE:
		lbl.visible = false
		_label_pool.append(lbl)
	else:
		lbl.queue_free()

func clear_all() -> void:
	for item in active_labels:
		var lbl: Label = item.get("node", null)
		if is_instance_valid(lbl):
			_release_pooled_label(lbl)
	active_labels.clear()
	coin_arcs.clear()
	queue_redraw()

func _on_spawn_floating_text(world_pos: Vector2, text_str: String, col: Color) -> void:
	var is_gold := text_str.begins_with("+") and text_str.ends_with("G")
	var final_col := Color("#FFE066") if is_gold else col
	var lbl := _acquire_pooled_label(text_str, final_col)
	lbl.position = world_pos + Vector2(-60.0, 0.0)
	active_labels.append({
		"node": lbl,
		"ttl": 1.8
	})

func _on_customer_paid(_recipe_id: String, _qty: int, total_gold: int, world_pos: Vector2) -> void:
	_on_spawn_floating_text(world_pos + Vector2(0.0, -58.0), "+" + str(total_gold) + "G", Color("#FFE066"))
	for i in range(3):
		coin_arcs.append({
			"x": world_pos.x,
			"y": world_pos.y - 24.0,
			"vx": randf_range(-32.0, 32.0),
			"vy": randf_range(-78.0, -48.0),
			"ttl": 0.95
		})

func _process(delta: float) -> void:
	for i in range(active_labels.size() - 1, -1, -1):
		var item: Dictionary = active_labels[i]
		var lbl: Label = item["node"]
		var ttl := float(item["ttl"]) - delta
		item["ttl"] = ttl
		if is_instance_valid(lbl):
			lbl.position.y -= 15.0 * delta
			lbl.modulate.a = clampf(ttl / 0.6, 0.0, 1.0)
			if ttl <= 0.0:
				_release_pooled_label(lbl)
				active_labels.remove_at(i)
		else:
			active_labels.remove_at(i)

	var coins_dirty := false
	for i in range(coin_arcs.size() - 1, -1, -1):
		coin_arcs[i]["vy"] = float(coin_arcs[i]["vy"]) + 135.0 * delta
		coin_arcs[i]["x"] = float(coin_arcs[i]["x"]) + float(coin_arcs[i]["vx"]) * delta
		coin_arcs[i]["y"] = float(coin_arcs[i]["y"]) + float(coin_arcs[i]["vy"]) * delta
		coin_arcs[i]["ttl"] = float(coin_arcs[i]["ttl"]) - delta
		coins_dirty = true
		if float(coin_arcs[i]["ttl"]) <= 0.0:
			coin_arcs.remove_at(i)

	if coins_dirty:
		queue_redraw()

func _draw() -> void:
	if tex_icons != null:
		for c in coin_arcs:
			draw_texture_rect_region(tex_icons, Rect2(float(c["x"]) - 11.0, float(c["y"]) - 11.0, 22.0, 22.0), Rect2(0, 0, 48, 48))

func _format_duration_cn(seconds: int) -> String:
	if seconds >= 3600:
		return str(seconds / 3600) + "小时" + str((seconds % 3600) / 60) + "分"
	elif seconds >= 60:
		return str(seconds / 60) + "分" + str(seconds % 60) + "秒"
	return str(seconds) + "秒"

	lbl_bar_stall.custom_minimum_size = Vector2(162.0, 22.0)
	lbl_bar_stall.scale = Vector2(0.84, 0.84)
	add_child(lbl_bar_stall)

	lbl_bar_pomodoro = EconomyConfig.create_pixel_label("[番茄专注]", 14, Color("#FFE066"), false)
	lbl_bar_pomodoro.position = Vector2(POMODORO_RECT.position.x + 24.0, POMODORO_RECT.position.y + 9.0)
	lbl_bar_pomodoro.scale = Vector2(0.84, 0.84)
	add_child(lbl_bar_pomodoro)

	for i in range(TAB_COUNT):
		var tl := EconomyConfig.create_pixel_label("", 14, Color("#FFE066"), true)
		tl.position = Vector2(1046.0 + float(i) * 74.0, 264.0)
		tl.custom_minimum_size = Vector2(76.0, 22.0)
		tl.scale = Vector2(0.92, 0.92)
		add_child(tl)
		lbl_tabs.append(tl)

	lbl_drawer_title = EconomyConfig.create_pixel_label("", 14, Color("#FFE066"), false)
	lbl_drawer_title.position = Vector2(540.0, 128.0)
	lbl_drawer_title.scale = Vector2(0.88, 0.88)
	add_child(lbl_drawer_title)

	lbl_drawer_fps = EconomyConfig.create_pixel_label("[60FPS]", 14, Color("#FFE066"), false)
	lbl_drawer_fps.position = Vector2(TOP_RIGHT_FPS_RECT.position.x + 4.0, TOP_RIGHT_FPS_RECT.position.y + 2.0)
	lbl_drawer_fps.scale = Vector2(0.86, 0.86)
	add_child(lbl_drawer_fps)

	lbl_drawer_opacity = EconomyConfig.create_pixel_label("[100%透]", 14, Color("#FFE066"), false)
	lbl_drawer_opacity.position = Vector2(TOP_RIGHT_OPACITY_RECT.position.x + 4.0, TOP_RIGHT_OPACITY_RECT.position.y + 2.0)
	lbl_drawer_opacity.scale = Vector2(0.86, 0.86)
	add_child(lbl_drawer_opacity)

	lbl_drawer_dock = EconomyConfig.create_pixel_label("[贴合任务栏]", 14, Color("#FFE066"), false)
	lbl_drawer_dock.position = Vector2(TOP_RIGHT_DOCK_RECT.position.x + 4.0, TOP_RIGHT_DOCK_RECT.position.y + 2.0)
	lbl_drawer_dock.scale = Vector2(0.86, 0.86)
	add_child(lbl_drawer_dock)

	lbl_drawer_weather = EconomyConfig.create_pixel_label("[天气:晴昼]", 14, Color("#FFE066"), false)
	lbl_drawer_weather.position = Vector2(TOP_RIGHT_WEATHER_RECT.position.x + 4.0, TOP_RIGHT_WEATHER_RECT.position.y + 2.0)
	lbl_drawer_weather.scale = Vector2(0.86, 0.86)
	add_child(lbl_drawer_weather)

	lbl_drawer_mute = EconomyConfig.create_pixel_label("[静音]", 14, Color("#FFF4D6"), false)
	lbl_drawer_mute.position = Vector2(TOP_RIGHT_MUTE_RECT.position.x + 4.0, TOP_RIGHT_MUTE_RECT.position.y + 2.0)
	lbl_drawer_mute.scale = Vector2(0.86, 0.86)
	add_child(lbl_drawer_mute)

	for i in range(6):
		var ct := EconomyConfig.create_pixel_label("", 14, Color("#FFE066"), false)
		var cs := EconomyConfig.create_pixel_label("", 14, Color("#FFF4D6"), false)
		var cc := EconomyConfig.create_pixel_label("", 14, Color("#FFE066"), false)
		cs.scale = Vector2(0.85, 0.85)
		cc.scale = Vector2(0.90, 0.90)
		add_child(ct)
		add_child(cs)
		add_child(cc)
		lbl_card_titles.append(ct)
		lbl_card_subs.append(cs)
		lbl_card_costs.append(cc)

func _process(delta: float) -> void:
	displayed_gold = lerpf(displayed_gold, float(GameManager.gold), minf(1.0, delta * 9.0))
	if absf(displayed_gold - float(GameManager.gold)) < 0.6:
		displayed_gold = float(GameManager.gold)

	if offline_banner_timer > 0.0:
		offline_banner_timer = maxf(0.0, offline_banner_timer - delta)

	var mpos := get_local_mouse_position()
	var hover_zone := _get_interactive_zone()
	is_mouse_over_hud = hover_zone.has_point(mpos)

	if boss_stealth_mode:
		is_collapsed_capsule = true
		drawer_expanded = false
		drawer_anim_t = 0.0
		hud_opacity = 0.25
	elif force_pin_open:
		is_collapsed_capsule = false
		drawer_expanded = true
		drawer_anim_t = 1.0
		hud_opacity = 1.0
	elif GameManager.desktop_companion_mode:
		if is_mouse_over_hud:
			mouse_outside_timer = 0.0
			is_collapsed_capsule = false
			hud_opacity = lerpf(hud_opacity, 1.0, minf(1.0, delta * 10.0))
		else:
			mouse_outside_timer += delta
			if mouse_outside_timer >= 3.0:
				drawer_expanded = false
				is_collapsed_capsule = true
				hud_opacity = lerpf(hud_opacity, 0.40, minf(1.0, delta * 5.0))
	else:
		is_collapsed_capsule = false
		hud_opacity = 1.0

	if not force_pin_open and not boss_stealth_mode:
		drawer_anim_t = lerpf(drawer_anim_t, 1.0 if drawer_expanded else 0.0, minf(1.0, delta * 10.0))
	var win_alpha: float = WindowController.get_window_opacity() if WindowController != null else 1.0
	modulate.a = (0.25 if boss_stealth_mode else hud_opacity) * win_alpha

	hovered_tab = -1
	hovered_card = -1
	if not is_collapsed_capsule:
		for i in range(TAB_COUNT):
			if _get_tab_button_rect(i).has_point(mpos):
				hovered_tab = i
				break
		if drawer_expanded:
			var cards := _get_current_tab_cards()
			for i in range(cards.size()):
				if _get_card_rect(i, cards.size()).has_point(mpos):
					hovered_card = i
					break

	if (hovered_tab != -1 and hovered_tab != prev_hovered_tab) or (hovered_card != -1 and hovered_card != prev_hovered_card):
		if SoundManager != null:
			SoundManager.play_hover_sfx()
	prev_hovered_tab = hovered_tab
	prev_hovered_card = hovered_card

	_sync_label_nodes()
	queue_redraw()

func _sync_label_nodes() -> void:
	var amber_cnt := GameManager.get_amber() if GameManager != null else 0
	var gold_str := str(int(round(displayed_gold))) + "G·珀" + str(amber_cnt)
	var mute_lbl := "[静音]" if (SoundManager != null and SoundManager.is_muted) else "[音效]"
	var weather_names := ["晴昼", "黄昏", "夜市", "微雨"]
	var cur_w_name: String = weather_names[clampi(GameManager.current_weather, 0, 3)]

	var contract: Dictionary = GameManager.get_active_contract() if GameManager != null else {}
	var c_target: String = String(contract.get("target_name", "作物"))
	var c_cur: int = int(contract.get("current_count", 0))
	var c_req: int = int(contract.get("required_count", 5))
	var c_amb: int = int(contract.get("reward_amber", 2))

	lbl_offline.visible = (offline_banner_timer > 0.0 and not boss_stealth_mode)
	lbl_offline.text = offline_banner_text

	lbl_capsule_gold.visible = is_collapsed_capsule
	lbl_capsule_info.visible = is_collapsed_capsule
	lbl_capsule_mute.visible = is_collapsed_capsule
	if is_collapsed_capsule:
		lbl_capsule_gold.text = gold_str
		if boss_stealth_mode:
			lbl_capsule_info.text = "后台挂机产金中 · 按F9或点击恢复"
			lbl_capsule_mute.text = "[恢复]"
		else:
			var p_prefix := ("专注" + GameManager.get_pomodoro_formatted_time() + " · ") if (GameManager != null and GameManager.pomodoro_active) else ""
			lbl_capsule_info.text = p_prefix + "契约:" + c_target + str(c_cur) + "/" + str(c_req) + " · 悬停展开"
			lbl_capsule_mute.text = mute_lbl

	var show_bar := not is_collapsed_capsule
	lbl_bar_gold.visible = show_bar
	lbl_bar_pipeline.visible = show_bar
	lbl_bar_stall.visible = show_bar
	lbl_bar_pomodoro.visible = show_bar
	lbl_bar_gold.text = gold_str
	lbl_bar_pipeline.text = "契约:" + c_target + " " + str(c_cur) + "/" + str(c_req) + "(+" + str(c_amb) + "珀)"
	var codex_pct := GameManager.get_unlocked_codex_count() * 10
	lbl_bar_stall.text = "存" + str(GameManager.get_island_basket_total(1)) + " · 摊" + str(GameManager.get_total_stall_count()) + "/" + str(GameManager.get_stall_max_capacity()) + " · 图鉴+" + str(codex_pct) + "%"
	if GameManager != null and GameManager.pomodoro_active:
		lbl_bar_pomodoro.text = GameManager.get_pomodoro_formatted_time()
	else:
		lbl_bar_pomodoro.text = "[专注25m]"

	var tab_names := ["建筑升级", "动物编制", "开拓造景", "山海图鉴", ("★摸鱼置顶" if GameManager.desktop_companion_mode else "★常规窗口")]
	for i in range(TAB_COUNT):
		lbl_tabs[i].visible = show_bar
		lbl_tabs[i].text = tab_names[i]

	var show_drawer := show_bar and drawer_expanded
	var slide_y := lerpf(40.0, 0.0, drawer_anim_t)
	lbl_drawer_title.visible = show_drawer
	lbl_drawer_fps.visible = show_drawer
	lbl_drawer_opacity.visible = show_drawer
	lbl_drawer_dock.visible = show_drawer
	lbl_drawer_weather.visible = show_drawer
	lbl_drawer_mute.visible = show_drawer
	if show_drawer:
		var codex_cnt := GameManager.get_unlocked_codex_count()
		var titles := [
			"【建筑升级】每25级作物/美食自动换代 + 建筑四阶外观蜕变!",
			"【动物编制与特质】点击摸头加速 · Lv.10/25/50激活专精特质!",
			"【开拓与造景】开拓跨岛木桥 · 晨露琥珀点亮三岛专属增益造景!",
			"【山海图鉴】已点亮 " + str(codex_cnt) + "/8 种食材料理 · 永久售价 +" + str(codex_cnt * 10) + "%!"
		]
		lbl_drawer_title.text = titles[clampi(active_tab, 0, 3)]
		lbl_drawer_title.position = Vector2(540.0, 129.0 + slide_y)

		var cur_fps: int = WindowController.get_eco_fps() if WindowController != null else 60
		var cur_op: float = WindowController.get_window_opacity() if WindowController != null else 1.0
		lbl_drawer_fps.text = "[" + str(cur_fps) + "FPS]"
		lbl_drawer_fps.position = Vector2(TOP_RIGHT_FPS_RECT.position.x + 4.0, TOP_RIGHT_FPS_RECT.position.y + 2.0 + slide_y)
		lbl_drawer_opacity.text = "[" + str(int(round(cur_op * 100.0))) + "%透]"
		lbl_drawer_opacity.position = Vector2(TOP_RIGHT_OPACITY_RECT.position.x + 4.0, TOP_RIGHT_OPACITY_RECT.position.y + 2.0 + slide_y)
		lbl_drawer_dock.text = "[贴合任务栏]"
		lbl_drawer_dock.position = Vector2(TOP_RIGHT_DOCK_RECT.position.x + 4.0, TOP_RIGHT_DOCK_RECT.position.y + 2.0 + slide_y)

		lbl_drawer_weather.text = "[天气:" + cur_w_name + "]"
		lbl_drawer_weather.position = Vector2(TOP_RIGHT_WEATHER_RECT.position.x + 4.0, TOP_RIGHT_WEATHER_RECT.position.y + 3.0 + slide_y)
		lbl_drawer_mute.text = mute_lbl
		lbl_drawer_mute.position = Vector2(TOP_RIGHT_MUTE_RECT.position.x + 4.0, TOP_RIGHT_MUTE_RECT.position.y + 2.0 + slide_y)

	var cards: Array[Dictionary] = []
	if show_drawer:
		cards = _get_current_tab_cards()
	for i in range(6):
		var vis := show_drawer and (i < cards.size())
		lbl_card_titles[i].visible = vis
		lbl_card_subs[i].visible = vis
		lbl_card_costs[i].visible = vis
		if vis:
			var crect := _get_card_rect(i, cards.size())
			crect.position.y += slide_y
			var cdata: Dictionary = cards[i]
			lbl_card_titles[i].text = String(cdata["title"])
			lbl_card_titles[i].position = crect.position + Vector2(8.0, 3.0)
			lbl_card_subs[i].text = String(cdata["sub"])
			lbl_card_subs[i].position = crect.position + Vector2(8.0, 22.0)
			lbl_card_costs[i].text = String(cdata["cost"])
			lbl_card_costs[i].position = crect.position + Vector2(8.0, 38.0)

func _get_interactive_zone() -> Rect2:
	if drawer_expanded:
		return Rect2(476.0, 118.0, 968.0, 174.0)
	if is_collapsed_capsule:
		return CAPSULE_RECT.grow(8.0)
	return FULL_BAR_RECT.grow(6.0)

func get_hud_Hit_rect() -> Rect2:
	return _get_interactive_zone()

func _gui_input(event: InputEvent) -> void:
	if event is InputEventMouseButton and event.button_index == MOUSE_BUTTON_LEFT:
		var mpos: Vector2 = event.position
		if event.pressed:
			is_pressing_btn = true
			if Input.is_key_pressed(KEY_ALT) and WindowController != null:
				WindowController.begin_window_drag()
				accept_event()
				return
			var hitting_top_btns := (drawer_expanded and (TOP_RIGHT_MUTE_RECT.has_point(mpos) or TOP_RIGHT_WEATHER_RECT.has_point(mpos) or TOP_RIGHT_FPS_RECT.has_point(mpos) or TOP_RIGHT_OPACITY_RECT.has_point(mpos) or TOP_RIGHT_DOCK_RECT.has_point(mpos))) or (is_collapsed_capsule and CAPSULE_MUTE_RECT.has_point(mpos)) or (not is_collapsed_capsule and POMODORO_RECT.has_point(mpos))
			if hovered_tab == -1 and hovered_card == -1 and not hitting_top_btns and _get_interactive_zone().has_point(mpos):
				if WindowController != null:
					WindowController.begin_window_drag()
					accept_event()
					return
		else:
			is_pressing_btn = false
			if WindowController != null and WindowController.is_dragging:
				WindowController.end_window_drag()
				accept_event()
				return

			if boss_stealth_mode and CAPSULE_RECT.has_point(mpos):
				if WindowController != null:
					WindowController.toggle_boss_stealth()
				else:
					set_boss_stealth(false)
				accept_event()
				return

			if drawer_expanded and TOP_RIGHT_FPS_RECT.has_point(mpos):
				if SoundManager != null:
					SoundManager.play_click_sfx()
				if WindowController != null:
					WindowController.toggle_eco_fps()
				_sync_label_nodes()
				accept_event()
				return

			if drawer_expanded and TOP_RIGHT_OPACITY_RECT.has_point(mpos):
				if SoundManager != null:
					SoundManager.play_click_sfx()
				if WindowController != null:
					WindowController.cycle_window_opacity()
				_sync_label_nodes()
				accept_event()
				return

			if drawer_expanded and TOP_RIGHT_DOCK_RECT.has_point(mpos):
				if SoundManager != null:
					SoundManager.play_click_sfx()
				if WindowController != null:
					WindowController.snap_to_taskbar()
				_sync_label_nodes()
				accept_event()
				return

			if drawer_expanded and TOP_RIGHT_WEATHER_RECT.has_point(mpos):
				if SoundManager != null:
					SoundManager.play_click_sfx()
				GameManager.set_weather((GameManager.current_weather + 1) % 4)
				accept_event()
				return

			if (drawer_expanded and TOP_RIGHT_MUTE_RECT.has_point(mpos)) or (is_collapsed_capsule and CAPSULE_MUTE_RECT.has_point(mpos)):
				if SoundManager != null:
					SoundManager.toggle_mute()
				accept_event()
				return

			if not is_collapsed_capsule and POMODORO_RECT.has_point(mpos):
				if SoundManager != null:
					SoundManager.play_click_sfx()
				if GameManager != null:
					GameManager.toggle_pomodoro()
				_sync_label_nodes()
				accept_event()
				return

			if hovered_tab >= 0:
				if SoundManager != null:
					SoundManager.play_click_sfx()
				if hovered_tab == 4:
					_toggle_companion_mode()
				else:
					force_pin_open = false
					active_tab = hovered_tab
					drawer_expanded = true
					mouse_outside_timer = 0.0
				accept_event()
				return

			if drawer_expanded and hovered_card >= 0:
				if SoundManager != null:
					SoundManager.play_click_sfx()
				_activate_card(hovered_card)
				mouse_outside_timer = 0.0
				accept_event()
				return

func _toggle_companion_mode() -> void:
	GameManager.desktop_companion_mode = not GameManager.desktop_companion_mode
	drawer_expanded = not GameManager.desktop_companion_mode
	EventBus.toggle_desktop_mode.emit(GameManager.desktop_companion_mode)
	GameManager.save_game()

func _get_tab_button_rect(idx: int) -> Rect2:
	return Rect2(1050.0 + float(idx) * 74.0, 235.0, 68.0, 44.0)

func _get_card_rect(idx: int, total: int) -> Rect2:
	var w := 196.0 if total <= 4 else 132.0
	var gap := 12.0 if total <= 4 else 8.0
	var start_x := 548.0 if total <= 4 else 538.0
	return Rect2(start_x + float(idx) * (w + gap), 156.0, w, 58.0)

func _get_current_tab_cards() -> Array[Dictionary]:
	var list: Array[Dictionary] = []
	match active_tab:
		0:
			for b_key in ["soil_1", "mortar", "stall", "cauldron", "soil_2", "soil_3"]:
				var cfg: Dictionary = EconomyConfig.BUILDINGS[b_key]
				var req_isl := int(cfg.get("island", 1))
				var isl_on := bool(GameManager.unlocked_islands.get(req_isl, false))
				var lv := int(GameManager.building_levels.get(b_key, 1))
				var cost := EconomyConfig.calc_upgrade_cost(int(cfg["base_cost"]), lv)
				var next_ms := (int(floor(float(lv) / 25.0)) + 1) * 25
				list.append({
					"type": "building",
					"key": b_key,
					"title": String(cfg["name"]) + (" Lv." + str(lv) if isl_on else " (未解锁)"),
					"sub": ("下个2x蜕变: Lv." + str(next_ms)) if isl_on else ("需先开拓木桥解锁岛" + str(req_isl)),
					"cost": ("升级 " + str(cost) + "G") if isl_on else "待解锁岛屿"
				})
		1:
			for a_key in ["bunny", "otter", "fox", "shiba", "penguin", "toucan"]:
				var cfg: Dictionary = EconomyConfig.ANIMALS[a_key]
				var cur_rec := GameManager.get_total_recruited(a_key)
				var max_cap := GameManager.get_total_max_cap(a_key)
				var next_isl := GameManager.find_next_recruit_island(a_key)
				var lv := maxi(1, int(GameManager.animal_levels.get(a_key, 0)))
				var cost_step := cur_rec + lv
				var cost := EconomyConfig.calc_upgrade_cost(int(cfg["base_cost"]), cost_step)

				var title_str := String(cfg["name"]).split(" · ")[0]
				var trait_tag := EconomyConfig.get_animal_trait_tag(a_key, lv)
				var sub_str := ""
				var act_str := ""
				if max_cap <= 0:
					sub_str = "需先解锁新岛屿"
					act_str = "待解锁岛屿"
				elif next_isl > 0:
					sub_str = "编制 " + str(cur_rec) + "/" + str(max_cap) + " · " + trait_tag
					act_str = "招募 " + str(cost) + "G"
				else:
					sub_str = "Lv." + str(lv) + " · " + trait_tag
					act_str = "全队进修 Lv.+1 (" + str(cost) + "G)"

				list.append({
					"type": "animal",
					"key": a_key,
					"title": title_str,
					"sub": sub_str,
					"cost": act_str
				})
		2:
			var isl2_unlocked := bool(GameManager.unlocked_islands.get(2, false))
			var isl3_unlocked := bool(GameManager.unlocked_islands.get(3, false))
			list.append({
				"type": "unlock_island",
				"key": "2",
				"title": "木桥1 · 魔法密林" + (" (已通)" if isl2_unlocked else ""),
				"sub": "2x农田+12x坩埚+企鹅",
				"cost": "已解锁" if isl2_unlocked else "180G 解锁"
			})
			list.append({
				"type": "unlock_island",
				"key": "3",
				"title": "木桥2 · 天空海岸" + (" (已通)" if isl3_unlocked else ""),
				"sub": "双工坊52x+★2x富豪",
				"cost": "已解锁" if isl3_unlocked else "480G 解锁"
			})
			for lk in ["bee_hive", "wind_chime", "tiki_torch"]:
				var cfg: Dictionary = EconomyConfig.ISLAND_LANDSCAPES[lk]
				var isl := int(cfg.get("island", 1))
				var isl_open := bool(GameManager.unlocked_islands.get(isl, false))
				var is_unlocked := GameManager.is_landscape_unlocked(lk)
				var cost_amb := int(cfg.get("cost_amber", 3))
				list.append({
					"type": "landscape",
					"key": lk,
					"title": String(cfg.get("name", lk)) + (" (已点亮)" if is_unlocked else " (未建造)"),
					"sub": String(cfg.get("desc", "")) if isl_open else ("需先解锁岛屿" + str(isl)),
					"cost": "已点亮生效" if is_unlocked else (str(cost_amb) + " 琥珀 解锁" if isl_open else "待解锁岛屿")
				})
		3:
			# Direction 4: Mountain & Sea Collection Codex (4 Tiers pairing Crop + Delicacy)
			var codex_pairs := [
				{"idx": 0, "crop": "crop_0", "del": "delicacy_0", "tier_name": "一阶·晨露田园", "c_hint": "???(初始解锁)", "d_hint": "???(研钵工坊)"},
				{"idx": 1, "crop": "crop_1", "del": "delicacy_1", "tier_name": "二阶·果香烘焙", "c_hint": "???(农田Lv.25)", "d_hint": "???(研钵Lv.25)"},
				{"idx": 2, "crop": "crop_2", "del": "delicacy_2", "tier_name": "三阶·密林星露", "c_hint": "???(解锁岛2)", "d_hint": "???(岛2坩埚)"},
				{"idx": 3, "crop": "crop_3", "del": "delicacy_3", "tier_name": "四阶·云海盛宴", "c_hint": "???(解锁岛3)", "d_hint": "???(岛3双工坊)"}
			]
			for cp in codex_pairs:
				var cid := String(cp["crop"])
				var did := String(cp["del"])
				var c_name := String(EconomyConfig.CROPS[cid]["name"])
				var d_name := String(EconomyConfig.DELICACIES[did]["name"])
				var c_on := bool(GameManager.unlocked_codex.get(cid, false))
				var d_on := bool(GameManager.unlocked_codex.get(did, false))
				var bonus_pct := (10 if c_on else 0) + (10 if d_on else 0)
				list.append({
					"type": "codex",
					"key": str(cp["idx"]),
					"title": String(cp["tier_name"]) + " (+" + str(bonus_pct) + "%)",
					"sub": (c_name if c_on else String(cp["c_hint"])) + " | " + (d_name if d_on else String(cp["d_hint"])),
					"cost": "已点亮 " + str((1 if c_on else 0) + (1 if d_on else 0)) + "/2 · 永久售价+" + str(bonus_pct) + "%"
				})
	return list

func _activate_card(idx: int) -> void:
	var cards := _get_current_tab_cards()
	if idx < 0 or idx >= cards.size():
		return
	var c: Dictionary = cards[idx]
	var t := String(c["type"])
	var k := String(c["key"])
	if t == "building":
		GameManager.upgrade_building(k)
	elif t == "animal":
		if GameManager.get_total_max_cap(k) > 0:
			GameManager.upgrade_animal(k)
	elif t == "unlock_island":
		GameManager.unlock_island(int(k))
	elif t == "codex":
		var codex_pct := GameManager.get_unlocked_codex_count() * 10
		offline_banner_timer = 4.0
		offline_banner_text = "★山海图鉴总加成: 全岛商品售价永久 +" + str(codex_pct) + "% (每解锁1种农作物或工坊美食 +10%)"
	elif t == "landscape":
		if not GameManager.unlock_landscape(k):
			var req_cost := int(EconomyConfig.ISLAND_LANDSCAPES.get(k, {}).get("cost_amber", 3))
			offline_banner_timer = 3.5
			offline_banner_text = "晨露琥珀不足 (需 " + str(req_cost) + " 琥珀)，请完成飞艇皇家订单获取!"

	if drawer_expanded and tex_drawer != null:

		var fps_rect := Rect2(TOP_RIGHT_FPS_RECT.position.x, TOP_RIGHT_FPS_RECT.position.y + slide_y, TOP_RIGHT_FPS_RECT.size.x, TOP_RIGHT_FPS_RECT.size.y)
		var op_rect := Rect2(TOP_RIGHT_OPACITY_RECT.position.x, TOP_RIGHT_OPACITY_RECT.position.y + slide_y, TOP_RIGHT_OPACITY_RECT.size.x, TOP_RIGHT_OPACITY_RECT.size.y)

		var w_rect := Rect2(TOP_RIGHT_WEATHER_RECT.position.x, TOP_RIGHT_WEATHER_RECT.position.y + slide_y, TOP_RIGHT_WEATHER_RECT.size.x, TOP_RIGHT_WEATHER_RECT.size.y)

=== FILE: D:/project/maple-isle-idle/src/world/CameraController.gd ===
extends Camera2D
class_name CameraController
## Unified Cursor-Anchored Smooth Wheel Zoom & Inertia Panning Controller (`src/world/CameraController.gd`)
## Works seamlessly in BOTH Desktop Companion Mode and Windowed Mode by driving `MainWorld.world_root` transform
## while keeping `Camera2D` aligned with the `1920x300` viewport coordinate space:
## - Logarithmic Mouse Wheel zoom (`0.50x ~ 2.20x`, `ZOOM_STEP_RATIO = 1.12`) with magnetic `1.0x` native pixel snap.
## - True Zoom-to-Cursor (`以鼠标指针为中心缩放`) horizontal pivot + smart horizon vertical anchoring (`GROUND_Y = 124.0` never clips or sinks behind HUD).
## - Smooth exponential damping (`ZOOM_LERP_SPEED = 14.0`, `PAN_LERP_SPEED = 14.0`).
## - Right-Mouse-Button (`MOUSE_BUTTON_RIGHT`) drag panning with release inertia, `A/D` & `Left/Right` keyboard panning,
##   `Middle-Click` / `Space` / `Home` reset to `1.0x` panoramic view, and `1/2/3` island focus shortcuts.

const MIN_ZOOM := 0.50
const MAX_ZOOM := 2.20
const ZOOM_STEP_RATIO := 1.12
const ZOOM_LERP_SPEED := 14.0

const PAN_MAX_SPEED := 640.0
const PAN_ACCEL := 2200.0
const PAN_FRICTION := 1800.0
const PAN_LERP_SPEED := 14.0

const VIEWPORT_W := 1920.0
const VIEWPORT_H := 300.0
const WORLD_CENTER_X := 960.0
const WORLD_CENTER_Y := 150.0
const BASE_ROOT_Y := 36.0
const HORIZON_LOCAL_Y := 116.0
const ZOOM_OUT_ANCHOR_Y := 148.0

const ISLAND_FOCUS_X: Dictionary = {
	1: 860.0,
	2: 280.0,
	3: 1610.0
}

var world_ref: Node2D = null

var current_zoom: float = 1.0
var target_zoom: float = 1.0
var current_root_pos: Vector2 = Vector2(0.0, BASE_ROOT_Y)
var target_root_pos: Vector2 = Vector2(0.0, BASE_ROOT_Y)

var target_x: float = WORLD_CENTER_X
var target_y: float = WORLD_CENTER_Y
var velocity_x: float = 0.0

var is_rmb_panning: bool = false
var rmb_last_mouse: Vector2 = Vector2.ZERO
var rmb_drag_velocity: float = 0.0

func _ready() -> void:
	limit_left = -1600
	limit_right = 2800
	limit_top = -400
	limit_bottom = 700
	position = Vector2(WORLD_CENTER_X, WORLD_CENTER_Y)
	zoom = Vector2.ONE
	enabled = true

	if EventBus != null:
		EventBus.island_unlocked.connect(_on_island_unlocked)

func setup_world(p_world: Node2D) -> void:
	world_ref = p_world
	reset_camera_view(true)

func _on_island_unlocked(island_idx: int) -> void:
	focus_on_island(island_idx)

func focus_on_island(island_idx: int, desired_zoom: float = -1.0) -> void:
	var next_z := target_zoom
	if desired_zoom > 0.0:
		next_z = clampf(desired_zoom, MIN_ZOOM, MAX_ZOOM)
	target_zoom = next_z
	if ISLAND_FOCUS_X.has(island_idx):
		var focus_local_x := float(ISLAND_FOCUS_X[island_idx])
		var desired_root_x := WORLD_CENTER_X - focus_local_x * target_zoom
		target_root_pos.x = _clamp_root_x(desired_root_x, target_zoom)
		target_root_pos.y = _compute_ideal_root_y(target_zoom, 0.0)
		velocity_x = 0.0
		_sync_legacy_targets()

func reset_camera_view(immediate: bool = false) -> void:
	target_zoom = 1.0
	target_root_pos = Vector2(0.0, BASE_ROOT_Y)
	velocity_x = 0.0
	rmb_drag_velocity = 0.0
	is_rmb_panning = false
	if immediate:
		current_zoom = 1.0
		current_root_pos = target_root_pos
		_apply_to_world()
	_sync_legacy_targets()

func _sync_legacy_targets() -> void:
	target_x = (WORLD_CENTER_X - target_root_pos.x) / maxf(MIN_ZOOM, target_zoom)
	target_y = WORLD_CENTER_Y

func _clamp_root_x(raw_root_x: float, z: float) -> float:
	var center_root_x := WORLD_CENTER_X * (1.0 - z)
	var min_rx := minf(center_root_x - 240.0, WORLD_CENTER_X - 1760.0 * z)
	var max_rx := maxf(center_root_x + 240.0, WORLD_CENTER_X - 160.0 * z)
	return clampf(raw_root_x, min_rx, max_rx)

func _compute_ideal_root_y(z: float, cursor_bias_y: float = 0.0) -> float:
	if z < 1.0:
		# When zooming out (< 1.0x), keep the islands anchored cleanly above the bottom HUD strip
		return BASE_ROOT_Y + ZOOM_OUT_ANCHOR_Y * (1.0 - z)
	# When zooming in (>= 1.0x), anchor on the character/building horizon (`local_y = 116`) with subtle vertical cursor bias
	var max_bias := 48.0 * (z - 1.0)
	var clamped_bias := clampf(cursor_bias_y, -max_bias, max_bias)
	return BASE_ROOT_Y + HORIZON_LOCAL_Y * (1.0 - z) + clamped_bias

func _clamp_root_y(raw_root_y: float, z: float) -> float:
	var base_y := _compute_ideal_root_y(z, 0.0)
	if z <= 1.02:
		return base_y
	var max_vy := 48.0 * (z - 1.0)
	return clampf(raw_root_y, base_y - max_vy, base_y + max_vy)

func apply_wheel_zoom(wheel_dir: float, factor: float, mouse_screen_pos: Vector2) -> void:
	if WindowController != null and WindowController.boss_stealth_active:
		return
	var eff_factor := clampf(factor if factor > 0.01 else 1.0, 0.4, 2.5)
	var old_z := target_zoom
	var raw_new_z := clampf(old_z * pow(ZOOM_STEP_RATIO, wheel_dir * eff_factor), MIN_ZOOM, MAX_ZOOM)

	# Magnetic snap to 1.00x native pixel scale when crossing or landing near 1.0x
	if absf(old_z - 1.0) > 0.005:
		if (old_z < 1.0 and raw_new_z >= 0.955) or (old_z > 1.0 and raw_new_z <= 1.045) or absf(raw_new_z - 1.0) <= 0.035:
			raw_new_z = 1.0

	if absf(raw_new_z - old_z) < 0.001:
		return

	# True 2D Zoom-to-Cursor: keep the local world coordinate under `mouse_screen_pos` pinned at the cursor
	var pivot_screen_x := clampf(mouse_screen_pos.x, 80.0, VIEWPORT_W - 80.0)
	var local_under_cursor_x := clampf((pivot_screen_x - target_root_pos.x) / maxf(MIN_ZOOM, old_z), 60.0, 1860.0)
	var new_root_x := pivot_screen_x - local_under_cursor_x * raw_new_z

	# When snapping back to 1.0x or zooming out toward 1.0x, gently blend back toward panoramic center if near center
	if absf(raw_new_z - 1.0) < 0.005 and absf(new_root_x) < 140.0:
		new_root_x = 0.0

	var pivot_screen_y := clampf(mouse_screen_pos.y, 32.0, 232.0)
	var local_under_cursor_y := clampf((pivot_screen_y - target_root_pos.y) / maxf(MIN_ZOOM, old_z), 24.0, 196.0)
	var new_root_y := pivot_screen_y - local_under_cursor_y * raw_new_z

	target_zoom = raw_new_z
	target_root_pos.x = _clamp_root_x(new_root_x, target_zoom)
	target_root_pos.y = _clamp_root_y(new_root_y, target_zoom)
	velocity_x = 0.0
	_sync_legacy_targets()

func _apply_zoom_step(delta_zoom: float, mouse_screen_pos: Vector2) -> void:
	var dir := 1.0 if delta_zoom >= 0.0 else -1.0
	apply_wheel_zoom(dir, absf(delta_zoom) / 0.10, mouse_screen_pos)

func _input(event: InputEvent) -> void:
	if event is InputEventMouseButton:
		var mb := event as InputEventMouseButton
		if mb.pressed and mb.button_index == MOUSE_BUTTON_WHEEL_UP:
			apply_wheel_zoom(1.0, mb.factor, get_viewport().get_mouse_position())
			get_viewport().set_input_as_handled()
			return
		elif mb.pressed and mb.button_index == MOUSE_BUTTON_WHEEL_DOWN:
			apply_wheel_zoom(-1.0, mb.factor, get_viewport().get_mouse_position())
			get_viewport().set_input_as_handled()
			return
		elif mb.button_index == MOUSE_BUTTON_RIGHT:
			is_rmb_panning = mb.pressed
			rmb_last_mouse = get_viewport().get_mouse_position()
			if mb.pressed:
				velocity_x = 0.0
				rmb_drag_velocity = 0.0
			else:
				velocity_x = clampf(rmb_drag_velocity, -PAN_MAX_SPEED * 1.5, PAN_MAX_SPEED * 1.5)
			return
		elif mb.pressed and mb.button_index == MOUSE_BUTTON_MIDDLE:
			reset_camera_view(false)
			get_viewport().set_input_as_handled()
			return

	elif event is InputEventMouseMotion and is_rmb_panning:
		var cur_m := get_viewport().get_mouse_position()
		var dx := cur_m.x - rmb_last_mouse.x
		var dy := cur_m.y - rmb_last_mouse.y
		target_root_pos.x = _clamp_root_x(target_root_pos.x + dx, target_zoom)
		target_root_pos.y = _clamp_root_y(target_root_pos.y + dy * 0.85, target_zoom)
		rmb_drag_velocity = dx * 26.0
		rmb_last_mouse = cur_m
		_sync_legacy_targets()

	elif event is InputEventMagnifyGesture:
		var mg := event as InputEventMagnifyGesture
		var dir := 1.0 if mg.factor >= 1.0 else -1.0
		apply_wheel_zoom(dir, absf(mg.factor - 1.0) * 6.0, get_viewport().get_mouse_position())

	elif event is InputEventKey and event.pressed and not event.echo:
		var ke := event as InputEventKey
		if ke.keycode == KEY_HOME or ke.keycode == KEY_SPACE:
			reset_camera_view(false)
		elif ke.keycode == KEY_EQUAL or ke.keycode == KEY_KP_ADD:
			apply_wheel_zoom(1.0, 1.0, get_viewport().get_mouse_position())
		elif ke.keycode == KEY_MINUS or ke.keycode == KEY_KP_SUBTRACT:
			apply_wheel_zoom(-1.0, 1.0, get_viewport().get_mouse_position())
		elif ke.keycode == KEY_1:
			focus_on_island(1)
		elif ke.keycode == KEY_2:
			focus_on_island(2)
		elif ke.keycode == KEY_3:
			focus_on_island(3)

func _process(delta: float) -> void:
	var input_dir_x := 0.0
	if Input.is_key_pressed(KEY_A) or Input.is_key_pressed(KEY_LEFT):
		input_dir_x += 1.0 # Pressing Left moves world_root right so view pans left
	if Input.is_key_pressed(KEY_D) or Input.is_key_pressed(KEY_RIGHT):
		input_dir_x -= 1.0 # Pressing Right moves world_root left so view pans right

	var input_dir_y := 0.0
	if Input.is_key_pressed(KEY_W) or Input.is_key_pressed(KEY_UP):
		input_dir_y += 1.0 # Pressing Up moves world_root down so view pans up
	if Input.is_key_pressed(KEY_S) or Input.is_key_pressed(KEY_DOWN):
		input_dir_y -= 1.0 # Pressing Down moves world_root up so view pans down

	if not is_rmb_panning:
		if absf(input_dir_x) > 0.01:
			velocity_x = move_toward(velocity_x, input_dir_x * PAN_MAX_SPEED * current_zoom, PAN_ACCEL * delta)
		else:
			velocity_x = move_toward(velocity_x, 0.0, PAN_FRICTION * delta)

		if absf(velocity_x) > 0.5:
			target_root_pos.x = _clamp_root_x(target_root_pos.x + velocity_x * delta, target_zoom)
			_sync_legacy_targets()

		if absf(input_dir_y) > 0.01 and target_zoom > 1.02:
			target_root_pos.y = _clamp_root_y(target_root_pos.y + input_dir_y * (PAN_MAX_SPEED * 0.45) * delta, target_zoom)

	var z_weight := 1.0 - exp(-ZOOM_LERP_SPEED * delta)
	var p_weight := 1.0 - exp(-PAN_LERP_SPEED * delta)
	current_zoom = lerpf(current_zoom, target_zoom, z_weight)
	if absf(current_zoom - target_zoom) < 0.001:
		current_zoom = target_zoom
	current_root_pos = current_root_pos.lerp(target_root_pos, p_weight)
	if current_root_pos.distance_to(target_root_pos) < 0.15:
		current_root_pos = target_root_pos

	position = Vector2(WORLD_CENTER_X, WORLD_CENTER_Y)
	zoom = Vector2.ONE
	_apply_to_world()

func _apply_to_world() -> void:
	if world_ref == null:
		world_ref = get_parent() as Node2D
	if world_ref != null and "world_root" in world_ref and world_ref.world_root != null:
		var prev_z := float(world_ref.companion_zoom)
		var prev_pos: Vector2 = world_ref.world_root.position
		world_ref.companion_zoom = current_zoom
		world_ref.world_root.scale = Vector2(current_zoom, current_zoom)
		world_ref.world_root.position = current_root_pos
		if absf(prev_z - current_zoom) > 0.0005 or prev_pos.distance_to(current_root_pos) > 0.1:
			world_ref.queue_redraw()

=== FILE: D:/project/maple-isle-idle/src/world/MainWorld.gd ===
extends Node2D
class_name MainWorld
## Panoramic 3-Island World (`src/world/MainWorld.gd`)
## Strictly enforces GEMINI.md Laws 1~6:
## - Unified `GROUND_Y = 124.0` (`EconomyConfig.GROUND_Y`) across all islands.
## - Bottom-center grounding pivot (`centered = true`, `offset = Vector2(0.0, -float(frame_h) * 0.5)`) on all buildings & props.
## - Strict `z_index` layering (`Z_SKY = -100`, `Z_TERRAIN = -20`, `Z_SHADOW = -5`, `Z_SOIL_BASE = -2`, `Z_BUILDING = 0`, `Z_CROP = 1`, `Z_UNIT = 10`, `Z_CARRY_EMOTE = 20`, `Z_FLOATING_UI = 50`).
## - 1D Slot Allocator & Anti-Overlap Assertion (`|X_A - X_B| >= (W_A + W_B)/2 + 28px`, 0% horizontal overlap).

const GROUND_Y := 124.0

## GEMINI.md Law 3.1 & 3.2: 1D Slot Definitions (every adjacent pair satisfies `|X_A - X_B| >= (W_A + W_B)/2 + 28.0`)
const ISLAND_SLOTS: Dictionary = {
	1: [
		{"id": "apple_tree_1", "x": 612.4, "tex_w": 112.0, "tex_h": 120.0, "scale": 0.65},
		{"id": "soil_plot_1",  "x": 710.8, "tex_w": 64.0,  "tex_h": 26.0,  "scale": 1.00},
		{"id": "cottage_1",    "x": 811.2, "tex_w": 118.0, "tex_h": 114.0, "scale": 0.65},
		{"id": "mortar_1",     "x": 908.4, "tex_w": 68.0,  "tex_h": 58.0,  "scale": 0.85},
		{"id": "stall_1",      "x": 1013.4,"tex_w": 128.0, "tex_h": 104.0, "scale": 0.72}
	],
	2: [
		{"id": "left_hut_2",   "x": 144.0, "tex_w": 112.0, "tex_h": 108.0, "scale": 0.68},
		{"id": "soil_plot_2",  "x": 272.0, "tex_w": 64.0,  "tex_h": 26.0,  "scale": 1.00},
		{"id": "cauldron_2",   "x": 397.0, "tex_w": 68.0,  "tex_h": 62.0,  "scale": 0.85}
	],
	3: [
		{"id": "apple_tree_3", "x": 1462.0,"tex_w": 112.0, "tex_h": 120.0, "scale": 0.65},
		{"id": "soil_plot_3",  "x": 1562.4,"tex_w": 64.0,  "tex_h": 26.0,  "scale": 1.00},
		{"id": "orbis_work_3", "x": 1655.3,"tex_w": 68.0,  "tex_h": 58.0,  "scale": 0.85},
		{"id": "right_tiki_3", "x": 1755.6,"tex_w": 116.0, "tex_h": 108.0, "scale": 0.68}
	]
}

## v2.3 Direction 2: Floating Cloud Gift Encounter (`云端奇遇礼盒`)
var tex_delicacies: Texture2D
var cloud_gift_sprite: Sprite2D
var cloud_gift_label: Label
var cloud_gift_active: bool = false
var cloud_gift_timer: float = 22.0

func _ready() -> void:
	z_index = EconomyConfig.Z_TERRAIN
	z_as_relative = false

	tex_henesys = load("res://assets/pixel_art/islands/island_henesys.png")
	tex_ellinia = load("res://assets/pixel_art/islands/island_ellinia.png")
	tex_orbis = load("res://assets/pixel_art/islands/island_orbis_coast.png")
	tex_bridge = load("res://assets/pixel_art/islands/bridge_wood.png")
	tex_tree = load("res://assets/pixel_art/buildings/apple_tree.png")
	tex_cottage = load("res://assets/pixel_art/buildings/cottage_mushroom.png")
	tex_left_hut = load("res://assets/pixel_art/buildings/landmark_left_hut.png")
	tex_right_tiki = load("res://assets/pixel_art/buildings/landmark_right_tiki.png")
	tex_delicacies = load("res://assets/pixel_art/buildings/delicacies_sheet_normalized.png")

	_assert_1d_slot_layout()

	world_root.name = "WorldOffsetRoot"
	world_root.position = Vector2(0.0, 36.0)
	add_child(world_root)

	waypoint_graph = WaypointGraph.new()
	waypoint_graph.name = "WaypointGraph"
	world_root.add_child(waypoint_graph)

	ysort_layer.name = "YSortLayer"
	ysort_layer.y_sort_enabled = true
	world_root.add_child(ysort_layer)

	camera_controller = CameraController.new()
	camera_controller.name = "CameraController"
	add_child(camera_controller)
	camera_controller.setup_world(self)

	floating_text = FloatingText.new()
	floating_text.name = "FloatingText"
	world_root.add_child(floating_text)

	cloud_gift_sprite = Sprite2D.new()
	cloud_gift_sprite.name = "CloudGiftSprite"
	cloud_gift_sprite.texture = tex_delicacies
	cloud_gift_sprite.region_enabled = false
	cloud_gift_sprite.hframes = 4
	cloud_gift_sprite.vframes = 1
	cloud_gift_sprite.frame = 3
	cloud_gift_sprite.centered = true
	cloud_gift_sprite.scale = Vector2(0.85, 0.85)
	cloud_gift_sprite.visible = false
	cloud_gift_sprite.z_index = EconomyConfig.Z_CARRY_EMOTE
	cloud_gift_sprite.z_as_relative = false
	world_root.add_child(cloud_gift_sprite)

	cloud_gift_label = EconomyConfig.create_pixel_label("★奇遇礼盒", 14, Color("#FFE066"), true)
	cloud_gift_label.custom_minimum_size = Vector2(84.0, 20.0)
	cloud_gift_label.position = Vector2(-42.0, -34.0)
	cloud_gift_sprite.add_child(cloud_gift_label)

	var canvas_layer := CanvasLayer.new()
	canvas_layer.layer = 10
	add_child(canvas_layer)

	var hud_scene: PackedScene = load("res://src/ui/HUD.tscn")
	hud = hud_scene.instantiate() as HUD
	hud.name = "HUD"
	canvas_layer.add_child(hud)

	if WindowController != null:
		WindowController.setup(self, hud)

	_build_landmarks_and_buildings()
	_build_soil_plots()
	_spawn_all_roster_slots()

	EventBus.toggle_desktop_mode.connect(_on_toggle_desktop_mode)
	_apply_desktop_mode(GameManager.desktop_companion_mode)

func _assert_1d_slot_layout() -> void:
	for isl_idx in ISLAND_SLOTS.keys():
		var slots: Array = ISLAND_SLOTS[isl_idx]
		for i in range(slots.size() - 1):
			var a: Dictionary = slots[i]
			var b: Dictionary = slots[i + 1]
			var wa := float(a["tex_w"]) * float(a["scale"])
			var wb := float(b["tex_w"]) * float(b["scale"])
			var dist := absf(float(b["x"]) - float(a["x"]))
			var min_req := (wa + wb) * 0.5 + EconomyConfig.MIN_BUILDING_PADDING
			assert(dist >= min_req - 0.5, "Building Overlap Detected on Island %d between %s and %s!" % [isl_idx, str(a["id"]), str(b["id"])])

func _make_grounded_prop(tex: Texture2D, slot_x: float, frame_h: int, sc: float) -> Sprite2D:
	var sp := Sprite2D.new()
	sp.texture = tex
	# GEMINI.md Law 2.2: Bottom-Center Pivot (`centered = true`, `offset = Vector2(0.0, -float(frame_h) * 0.5)`)
	sp.centered = true
	sp.offset = Vector2(0.0, -float(frame_h) * 0.5)
	sp.scale = Vector2(sc, sc)
	sp.position = Vector2(slot_x, GROUND_Y)
	sp.z_index = EconomyConfig.Z_BUILDING
	sp.z_as_relative = false
	return sp

func _on_toggle_desktop_mode(enabled: bool) -> void:
	_apply_desktop_mode(enabled)

func _apply_desktop_mode(enabled: bool) -> void:
	if enabled:
		get_tree().root.transparent_bg = true
		DisplayServer.window_set_flag(DisplayServer.WINDOW_FLAG_TRANSPARENT, true)
		DisplayServer.window_set_flag(DisplayServer.WINDOW_FLAG_ALWAYS_ON_TOP, true)
		DisplayServer.window_set_flag(DisplayServer.WINDOW_FLAG_BORDERLESS, true)
		if SoundManager != null:
			SoundManager.set_muted(true)
	else:
		get_tree().root.transparent_bg = false
	queue_redraw()

func get_active_island_bounds() -> Rect2:
	var z := maxf(0.1, companion_zoom)
	var rx := clampf(world_root.position.x + 40.0 * z, 0.0, 1880.0)
	var ry := clampf(world_root.position.y + 8.0 * z, 0.0, 260.0)
	var rw := clampf(1840.0 * z, 200.0, 1920.0 - rx)
	var rh := clampf(230.0 * z, 100.0, 300.0 - ry)
	return Rect2(rx, ry, rw, rh)

func _build_landmarks_and_buildings() -> void:
	# Island 1 (Henesys): AppleTree (x=612.4), SoilPlot (x=710.8), Cottage (x=811.2), Mortar (x=908.4), Stall (x=1013.4)
	# GEMINI.md Law 3.3: `apple_tree.png` & `cottage_mushroom.png` have built-in grass base shadows -> NO procedural shadow!
	tree_1_node = _make_grounded_prop(tex_tree, 612.4, 120, 0.65)
	tree_1_node.name = "AppleTree_1"
	ysort_layer.add_child(tree_1_node)

	cot_1_node = _make_grounded_prop(tex_cottage, 811.2, 114, 0.65)
	cot_1_node.name = "MushroomCottage_1"
	ysort_layer.add_child(cot_1_node)

	# Island 2 (Ellinia): Left Hut (x=144.0), SoilPlot (x=272.0), Cauldron (x=397.0)
	left_hut_node = _make_grounded_prop(tex_left_hut, 144.0, 108, 0.68)
	left_hut_node.name = "LeftHut_2"
	ysort_layer.add_child(left_hut_node)
	island2_props.append(left_hut_node)

	# Island 3 (Orbis Coast): AppleTree (x=1462.0), SoilPlot (x=1562.4), Dual Workshop (x=1655.3), Right Tiki (x=1755.6)
	tree_3_node = _make_grounded_prop(tex_tree, 1462.0, 120, 0.65)
	tree_3_node.name = "AppleTree_3"
	ysort_layer.add_child(tree_3_node)
	island3_props.append(tree_3_node)

	right_tiki_node = _make_grounded_prop(tex_right_tiki, 1755.6, 108, 0.68)
	right_tiki_node.name = "RightTiki_3"
	ysort_layer.add_child(right_tiki_node)
	island3_props.append(right_tiki_node)

	mortar_workshop = Workshop.new()
	mortar_workshop.name = "MortarWorkshop_1"
	mortar_workshop.setup_workshop("mortar", 1, Vector2(908.4, GROUND_Y))
	ysort_layer.add_child(mortar_workshop)

	cauldron_workshop = Workshop.new()
	cauldron_workshop.name = "CauldronWorkshop_2"
	cauldron_workshop.setup_workshop("cauldron", 2, Vector2(397.0, GROUND_Y))
	ysort_layer.add_child(cauldron_workshop)

	orbis_linked_workshop = Workshop.new()
	orbis_linked_workshop.name = "OrbisWorkshop_3"
	orbis_linked_workshop.setup_workshop("orbis_linked", 3, Vector2(1655.3, GROUND_Y))
	ysort_layer.add_child(orbis_linked_workshop)

	market_stall = MarketStall.new()
	market_stall.name = "MarketStall_1"
	market_stall.position = Vector2(1013.4, GROUND_Y)
	ysort_layer.add_child(market_stall)

	# Functional Miniature Landscapes (v2.5-2)
	# Island 1: Bee Hive (x=662.0)
	var tex_beehive: Texture2D = load("res://assets/pixel_art/buildings/decor_beehive.png") if ResourceLoader.exists("res://assets/pixel_art/buildings/decor_beehive.png") else tex_tree
	var node_beehive := _make_grounded_prop(tex_beehive, 662.0, 52, 0.50)
	node_beehive.name = "Landscape_BeeHive_1"
	node_beehive.visible = false
	ysort_layer.add_child(node_beehive)
	landscape_nodes["bee_hive"] = node_beehive

	# Island 2: Wind Chime (x=210.0)
	var tex_windchime: Texture2D = load("res://assets/pixel_art/buildings/decor_windchime.png") if ResourceLoader.exists("res://assets/pixel_art/buildings/decor_windchime.png") else tex_left_hut
	var node_windchime := _make_grounded_prop(tex_windchime, 210.0, 58, 0.58)
	node_windchime.name = "Landscape_WindChime_2"
	node_windchime.visible = false
	ysort_layer.add_child(node_windchime)
	island2_props.append(node_windchime)
	landscape_nodes["wind_chime"] = node_windchime

	# Island 3: Tiki Torch (x=1705.0)
	var tex_torch: Texture2D = load("res://assets/pixel_art/buildings/decor_lamp.png")
	var node_torch := _make_grounded_prop(tex_torch, 1705.0, 66, 0.58)
	node_torch.name = "Landscape_TikiTorch_3"
	node_torch.visible = false
	ysort_layer.add_child(node_torch)
	island3_props.append(node_torch)
	landscape_nodes["tiki_torch"] = node_torch

func get_island_main_building_rects() -> Dictionary:
	var oy := world_root.position.y
	var p1_rect := soil_plots[0].get_screen_rect() if soil_plots.size() > 0 else Rect2(710.8 - 32.0, GROUND_Y - 26.0, 64.0, 26.0)
	var p2_rect := soil_plots[1].get_screen_rect() if soil_plots.size() > 1 else Rect2(272.0 - 32.0, GROUND_Y - 26.0, 64.0, 26.0)
	var p3_rect := soil_plots[2].get_screen_rect() if soil_plots.size() > 2 else Rect2(1562.4 - 32.0, GROUND_Y - 26.0, 64.0, 26.0)
	return {
		1: [
			{"name": "AppleTree_1",       "rect": _sprite_global_rect(tree_1_node, oy)},
			{"name": "SoilPlot_1",        "rect": Rect2(p1_rect.position + Vector2(0, oy), p1_rect.size)},
			{"name": "MushroomCottage_1", "rect": _sprite_global_rect(cot_1_node, oy)},
			{"name": "MortarWorkshop_1",  "rect": Rect2(mortar_workshop.get_screen_rect().position + Vector2(0, oy), mortar_workshop.get_screen_rect().size)},
			{"name": "MarketStall_1",     "rect": Rect2(market_stall.get_screen_rect().position + Vector2(0, oy), market_stall.get_screen_rect().size)}
		],
		2: [
			{"name": "LeftHut_2",         "rect": _sprite_global_rect(left_hut_node, oy)},
			{"name": "SoilPlot_2",        "rect": Rect2(p2_rect.position + Vector2(0, oy), p2_rect.size)},
			{"name": "CauldronWorkshop_2","rect": Rect2(cauldron_workshop.get_screen_rect().position + Vector2(0, oy), cauldron_workshop.get_screen_rect().size)}
		],
		3: [
			{"name": "AppleTree_3",       "rect": _sprite_global_rect(tree_3_node, oy)},
			{"name": "SoilPlot_3",        "rect": Rect2(p3_rect.position + Vector2(0, oy), p3_rect.size)},
			{"name": "OrbisWorkshop_3",   "rect": Rect2(orbis_linked_workshop.get_screen_rect().position + Vector2(0, oy), orbis_linked_workshop.get_screen_rect().size)},
			{"name": "RightTiki_3",       "rect": _sprite_global_rect(right_tiki_node, oy)}
		]
	}

func _sprite_global_rect(sp: Sprite2D, oy: float) -> Rect2:
	if sp == null or sp.texture == null:
		return Rect2()
	var w := float(sp.texture.get_width()) * absf(sp.scale.x)
	var h := float(sp.texture.get_height()) * absf(sp.scale.y)
	return Rect2(sp.position.x - w * 0.5, sp.position.y + oy - h, w, h)

func _build_soil_plots() -> void:
	var p1 := SoilPlot.new()
	p1.setup_plot(1, 0, "crop_0", Vector2(710.8, GROUND_Y))
	ysort_layer.add_child(p1)
	soil_plots.append(p1)

	var p2 := SoilPlot.new()
	p2.setup_plot(2, 0, "crop_2", Vector2(272.0, GROUND_Y))
	ysort_layer.add_child(p2)
	soil_plots.append(p2)

	var p3 := SoilPlot.new()
	p3.setup_plot(3, 0, "crop_3", Vector2(1562.4, GROUND_Y))
	ysort_layer.add_child(p3)
	soil_plots.append(p3)

func _spawn_all_roster_slots() -> void:
	var roster_defs := [
		# Island 1 (Henesys): Bunny Max 2, Otter Max 2, Fox Max 1, Shiba Max 1
		[AnimalWorker.RoleType.BUNNY_FARMER, 1, 0, Vector2(698.0, GROUND_Y)],
		[AnimalWorker.RoleType.BUNNY_FARMER, 1, 1, Vector2(724.0, GROUND_Y)],
		[AnimalWorker.RoleType.OTTER_LOGISTICS, 1, 0, Vector2(852.0, GROUND_Y)],
		[AnimalWorker.RoleType.OTTER_LOGISTICS, 1, 1, Vector2(868.0, GROUND_Y)],
		[AnimalWorker.RoleType.FOX_MORTAR_CHEF, 1, 0, Vector2(890.0, GROUND_Y)],
		[AnimalWorker.RoleType.SHIBA_MANAGER, 1, 0, Vector2(1036.0, GROUND_Y)],

		# Island 2 (Ellinia): Bunny Max 2, Otter Max 1, Penguin Max 1, Toucan Max 2
		[AnimalWorker.RoleType.BUNNY_FARMER, 2, 0, Vector2(262.0, GROUND_Y)],
		[AnimalWorker.RoleType.BUNNY_FARMER, 2, 1, Vector2(282.0, GROUND_Y)],
		[AnimalWorker.RoleType.OTTER_LOGISTICS, 2, 0, Vector2(335.0, GROUND_Y)],
		[AnimalWorker.RoleType.PENGUIN_ALCHEMIST, 2, 0, Vector2(378.0, GROUND_Y)],
		[AnimalWorker.RoleType.TOUCAN_TRADER, 2, 0, Vector2(426.0, GROUND_Y)],
		[AnimalWorker.RoleType.TOUCAN_TRADER, 2, 1, Vector2(448.0, GROUND_Y)],

		# Island 3 (Orbis Coast): Bunny Max 2, Otter Max 2, Fox Max 1, Penguin Max 1, Toucan Max 2
		[AnimalWorker.RoleType.BUNNY_FARMER, 3, 0, Vector2(1552.0, GROUND_Y)],
		[AnimalWorker.RoleType.BUNNY_FARMER, 3, 1, Vector2(1572.0, GROUND_Y)],
		[AnimalWorker.RoleType.OTTER_LOGISTICS, 3, 0, Vector2(1608.0, GROUND_Y)],
		[AnimalWorker.RoleType.OTTER_LOGISTICS, 3, 1, Vector2(1624.0, GROUND_Y)],
		[AnimalWorker.RoleType.FOX_MORTAR_CHEF, 3, 0, Vector2(1638.0, GROUND_Y)],
		[AnimalWorker.RoleType.PENGUIN_ALCHEMIST, 3, 0, Vector2(1674.0, GROUND_Y)],
		[AnimalWorker.RoleType.TOUCAN_TRADER, 3, 0, Vector2(1702.0, GROUND_Y)],
		[AnimalWorker.RoleType.TOUCAN_TRADER, 3, 1, Vector2(1722.0, GROUND_Y)]
	]
	for rd in roster_defs:
		var w := AnimalWorker.new()
		w.setup_worker(rd[0], rd[1], rd[2], rd[3], waypoint_graph, self)
		ysort_layer.add_child(w)
		workers.append(w)

func find_best_plot_for_island(isl_idx: int) -> SoilPlot:
	for p in soil_plots:
		if p.island_idx == isl_idx and p.is_plot_active():
			return p
	return null

func _unhandled_input(event: InputEvent) -> void:
	if event is InputEventMouseButton:
		if event.button_index == MOUSE_BUTTON_LEFT:
			if event.pressed and Input.is_key_pressed(KEY_ALT) and WindowController != null:
				WindowController.begin_window_drag()
				return
			elif not event.pressed and WindowController != null and WindowController.is_dragging:
				WindowController.end_window_drag()
				return
			elif event.pressed:
				var local_pos: Vector2 = (event.position - world_root.position) / maxf(0.1, companion_zoom)
				if cloud_gift_active and cloud_gift_sprite != null and local_pos.distance_to(cloud_gift_sprite.position) <= 36.0:
					_claim_cloud_gift()
					return
				for w in workers:
					if is_instance_valid(w) and w.visible and w.get_pet_hit_rect().has_point(local_pos):
						w.trigger_pet_boost()
						return

func _claim_cloud_gift() -> void:
	cloud_gift_active = false
	if cloud_gift_sprite != null:
		cloud_gift_sprite.visible = false
	cloud_gift_timer = randf_range(55.0, 85.0)
	var bonus_gold := maxi(60, int(round(GameManager.estimate_idle_gold_per_sec() * 25.0)))
	GameManager.add_gold(bonus_gold)
	GameManager.trigger_carnival_frenzy(20.0)
	if SoundManager != null:
		SoundManager.play_milestone_sfx()
	EventBus.spawn_floating_text.emit(cloud_gift_pos, "★奇遇礼盒+" + str(bonus_gold) + "G · 嘉年华20s!", Color("#FFE066"))

func _apply_companion_zoom() -> void:
	if camera_controller != null:
		camera_controller.target_zoom = clampf(companion_zoom, CameraController.MIN_ZOOM, CameraController.MAX_ZOOM)
	world_root.scale = Vector2(companion_zoom, companion_zoom)
	queue_redraw()

func _sync_landscape_nodes() -> void:
	if GameManager == null:
		return
	if landscape_nodes.has("bee_hive") and is_instance_valid(landscape_nodes["bee_hive"]):
		landscape_nodes["bee_hive"].visible = GameManager.is_landscape_unlocked("bee_hive")
	if landscape_nodes.has("wind_chime") and is_instance_valid(landscape_nodes["wind_chime"]):
		var isl2_open: bool = bool(GameManager.unlocked_islands.get(2, false))
		landscape_nodes["wind_chime"].visible = isl2_open and GameManager.is_landscape_unlocked("wind_chime")
	if landscape_nodes.has("tiki_torch") and is_instance_valid(landscape_nodes["tiki_torch"]):
		var isl3_open: bool = bool(GameManager.unlocked_islands.get(3, false))
		landscape_nodes["tiki_torch"].visible = isl3_open and GameManager.is_landscape_unlocked("tiki_torch")

func _process(delta: float) -> void:
	cloud_time += delta
	_sync_landscape_nodes()

	# v2.3 Direction 1: Weather & Time-of-Day ambient lighting interpolation
	var target_tint := Color.WHITE
	if GameManager != null:
		match GameManager.current_weather:
			1:
				target_tint = Color(1.0, 0.94, 0.86, 1.0)
			2:
				target_tint = Color(0.88, 0.92, 1.0, 1.0)
			3:
				target_tint = Color(0.92, 0.96, 1.0, 1.0)
	var win_op: float = WindowController.get_window_opacity() if WindowController != null else 1.0
	var target_alpha := 0.0 if (WindowController != null and WindowController.boss_stealth_active) else win_op
	target_tint.a = target_alpha
	world_root.modulate = world_root.modulate.lerp(target_tint, minf(1.0, delta * 3.5))
	world_root.modulate.a = target_alpha

	# v2.3 Direction 2: Floating Cloud Gift hovering over the open Lotus Pond (`x: 1120..1250`)
	if cloud_gift_active:
		cloud_gift_pos.x = 1188.0 + sin(cloud_time * 0.85) * 48.0
		cloud_gift_pos.y = 42.0 + sin(cloud_time * 2.6) * 4.0
		if cloud_gift_sprite != null:
			cloud_gift_sprite.visible = true
			cloud_gift_sprite.position = cloud_gift_pos
	else:
		cloud_gift_timer -= delta
		if cloud_gift_timer <= 0.0:
			cloud_gift_active = true
			cloud_gift_pos = Vector2(1188.0, 42.0)
			if cloud_gift_sprite != null:
				cloud_gift_sprite.visible = true
				cloud_gift_sprite.position = cloud_gift_pos

	var isl2_on := bool(GameManager.unlocked_islands.get(2, false))
	var isl3_on := bool(GameManager.unlocked_islands.get(3, false))
	for n in island2_props:
		n.visible = isl2_on
	for n in island3_props:
		n.visible = isl3_on

	chimney_timer += delta
	if chimney_timer >= 1.4:
		chimney_timer = 0.0
		chimney_wisps.append({"x": 796.0, "y": 54.0, "r": 3.5, "alpha": 0.55})
	for i in range(chimney_wisps.size() - 1, -1, -1):
		chimney_wisps[i]["y"] = float(chimney_wisps[i]["y"]) - 7.5 * delta
		chimney_wisps[i]["x"] = float(chimney_wisps[i]["x"]) + sin(cloud_time + float(i)) * 4.0 * delta
		chimney_wisps[i]["r"] = float(chimney_wisps[i]["r"]) + 1.8 * delta
		chimney_wisps[i]["alpha"] = float(chimney_wisps[i]["alpha"]) - 0.16 * delta
		if float(chimney_wisps[i]["alpha"]) <= 0.02:
			chimney_wisps.remove_at(i)

	for i in range(customers.size() - 1, -1, -1):
		if not is_instance_valid(customers[i]):
			customers.remove_at(i)

	customer_spawn_timer -= delta
	if customer_spawn_timer <= 0.0:
		customer_spawn_timer = randf_range(18.0, 30.0)
		if customers.size() < 4:
			var roll_vip := randf() < GameManager.get_weather_vip_chance()
			_spawn_customer(roll_vip)

	if isl3_on:
		vip_customer_timer -= delta
		if vip_customer_timer <= 0.0:
			vip_customer_timer = randf_range(35.0, 50.0)
			if customers.size() < 4:
				_spawn_customer(true)

	if WindowController != null and hud != null:
		var active_drawer_rect := HUD.DRAWER_RECT if hud.drawer_expanded else Rect2()
		WindowController.update_passthrough_polygon(get_active_island_bounds(), hud.get_hud_Hit_rect(), active_drawer_rect)

	queue_redraw()

func _spawn_customer(as_vip: bool) -> void:
	var c := CustomerNPC.new()
	var sp := Vector2(1075.0, GROUND_Y)
	if bool(GameManager.unlocked_islands.get(3, false)):
		sp = waypoint_graph.get_named_pos("spawn_right")
	c.setup_customer(sp, waypoint_graph, market_stall, as_vip)
	ysort_layer.add_child(c)
	customers.append(c)

func _get_bridge_weight_sag(min_x: float, max_x: float) -> float:
	var mid_x := (min_x + max_x) * 0.5
	var half_span := maxf(20.0, (max_x - min_x) * 0.5)
	var max_sag := 0.0
	for w in workers:
		if is_instance_valid(w) and w.visible and w.position.x >= min_x and w.position.x <= max_x:
			max_sag = maxf(max_sag, (1.0 - absf(w.position.x - mid_x) / half_span) * 1.6)
	for c in customers:
		if is_instance_valid(c) and c.position.x >= min_x and c.position.x <= max_x:
			max_sag = maxf(max_sag, (1.0 - absf(c.position.x - mid_x) / half_span) * 1.8)
	return max_sag

func _draw() -> void:
	var oy := 36.0
	var bob := roundf(sin(cloud_time * 0.55) * 1.0)

	if not GameManager.desktop_companion_mode:

	draw_set_transform(world_root.position - Vector2(0.0, oy * companion_zoom), 0.0, world_root.scale)

	var left_sag := _get_bridge_weight_sag(486.0, 594.0)
	var pond_sag := _get_bridge_weight_sag(1082.0, 1302.0)
	var right_sag := _get_bridge_weight_sag(1302.0, 1416.0)

	var isl2_tint := Color.WHITE if bool(GameManager.unlocked_islands.get(2, false)) else Color(0.55, 0.62, 0.72, 0.42)
	if tex_ellinia != null:
		draw_texture(tex_ellinia, Vector2(64.0, oy + 74.0 + bob), isl2_tint)
	if tex_bridge != null:
		draw_texture(tex_bridge, Vector2(492.0, oy + 124.0 + bob + roundf(left_sag)), isl2_tint)

	var isl3_tint := Color.WHITE if bool(GameManager.unlocked_islands.get(3, false)) else Color(0.55, 0.62, 0.72, 0.42)
	if tex_bridge != null:
		draw_texture(tex_bridge, Vector2(1312.0, oy + 118.0 + bob + roundf(right_sag)), isl3_tint)
	if tex_orbis != null:
		draw_texture(tex_orbis, Vector2(1396.0, oy + 74.0 + bob), isl3_tint)

	if tex_henesys != null:
		draw_texture(tex_henesys, Vector2(560.0, oy + 70.0))

	var ripple_alpha := 0.30 + 0.18 * sin(cloud_time * 1.6)
	for px in [1096.0, 1188.0, 1284.0]:
		var ring_r := 6.0 + fmod(cloud_time * 4.5 + px * 0.1, 5.0) + pond_sag * 1.5
		draw_arc(Vector2(px, oy + 146.0), ring_r, 0.0, PI * 2.0, 16, Color(0.88, 0.99, 1.0, ripple_alpha * 0.85), 1.0)
		draw_rect(Rect2(px - 3.0, oy + 134.0 + roundf(pond_sag), 6.0, 13.0), Color(0.36, 0.21, 0.11, 0.95), true)
	if tex_bridge != null:
		draw_texture_rect(tex_bridge, Rect2(1082.0, oy + 118.0 + roundf(pond_sag), 114.0, 38.0), false)
		draw_texture_rect(tex_bridge, Rect2(1188.0, oy + 118.0 + roundf(pond_sag), 114.0, 38.0), false)

	for wisp in chimney_wisps:
		draw_circle(Vector2(float(wisp["x"]), float(wisp["y"])), float(wisp["r"]), Color(0.98, 0.96, 0.92, float(wisp["alpha"])))

	# v2.3 Direction 1: Dusk/Night lotus pond pixel fireflies; Rain diagonal droplets (no sky circles behind roofs)
	if GameManager != null and not (WindowController != null and WindowController.boss_stealth_active):
		var w_state := GameManager.current_weather
		if w_state == 1 or w_state == 2:
			for fi in range(6):
				var fx := 1104.0 + float(fi) * 34.0 + sin(cloud_time * 1.8 + float(fi) * 1.3) * 8.0
				var fy := oy + 126.0 + cos(cloud_time * 2.3 + float(fi) * 0.9) * 6.0
				var fa := 0.55 + 0.40 * sin(cloud_time * 4.0 + float(fi))
				draw_rect(Rect2(fx, fy, 3.0, 3.0), Color(1.0, 0.94, 0.42, fa), true)
		elif w_state == 3:
			for ri in range(14):
				var rx := fmod(580.0 + float(ri) * 52.0 + cloud_time * 95.0, 720.0) + 580.0
				var ry := oy + fmod(float(ri) * 23.0 + cloud_time * 160.0, 75.0) + 72.0
				draw_line(Vector2(rx, ry), Vector2(rx - 3.0, ry + 8.0), Color(0.78, 0.92, 1.0, 0.42), 1.0)

	draw_set_transform(Vector2.ZERO, 0.0, Vector2.ONE)

=== FILE: D:/project/maple-isle-idle/src/world/WaypointGraph.gd ===
extends Node2D
class_name WaypointGraph
## Complete 3-Island Road, Dual Wooden Bridge & Building Dock Waypoint Graph (`src/world/WaypointGraph.gd`)
## Strictly enforces GEMINI.md Law 2.1 (`GROUND_Y = 124.0`) & Law 3.1 (1D Slot Coordinates):
## - Models all 3 islands' main roads (`island_2`, `island_1`, `island_3`),
## - Models both wooden bridges' (`bridge_wood`) west/arch/east endpoints and the Lotus Pond twin bridges,
## - Models dedicated loading/unloading dock nodes for every building (`soil_*_load`, `basket_*`, `mortar_in/out`, `cauldron_in/out`, `orbis_in/out`, `stall_drop`, `stall_queue_*`),
## - Provides weighted adjacency list & Dijkstra shortest-path queries for cross-island and intra-island navigation.

const GROUND_Y := 124.0

## Master Waypoint Coordinates along unified `GROUND_Y = 124.0`
const WAYPOINTS: Array[Vector2] = [
	Vector2(144.0, GROUND_Y),   # 0: Island 2 Left Hut (Ellinia West Spawn)
	Vector2(272.0, GROUND_Y),   # 1: Island 2 SoilPlot & Basket Dock
	Vector2(397.0, GROUND_Y),   # 2: Island 2 Cauldron Workshop Dock
	Vector2(492.0, GROUND_Y),   # 3: Bridge 1 West Endpoint (Ellinia -> Henesys)
	Vector2(542.0, GROUND_Y),   # 4: Bridge 1 Center Arch
	Vector2(586.0, GROUND_Y),   # 5: Bridge 1 East Endpoint
	Vector2(612.4, GROUND_Y),   # 6: Island 1 Apple Tree
	Vector2(710.8, GROUND_Y),   # 7: Island 1 SoilPlot & Basket Dock
	Vector2(811.2, GROUND_Y),   # 8: Island 1 Mushroom Cottage
	Vector2(908.4, GROUND_Y),   # 9: Island 1 Mortar Workshop Dock
	Vector2(1013.4, GROUND_Y),  # 10: Island 1 Market Stall Main Counter
	Vector2(1082.0, GROUND_Y),  # 11: Lotus Pond Bridge West
	Vector2(1192.0, GROUND_Y),  # 12: Lotus Pond Bridge Center Pier
	Vector2(1302.0, GROUND_Y),  # 13: Lotus Pond Bridge East
	Vector2(1358.0, GROUND_Y),  # 14: Bridge 2 Center Arch (Henesys -> Orbis Coast)
	Vector2(1414.0, GROUND_Y),  # 15: Bridge 2 East Endpoint (Orbis West Landing)
	Vector2(1462.0, GROUND_Y),  # 16: Island 3 Apple Tree

## Independent Building Loading / Unloading Dock Offsets (`x` offset relative to building waypoint)
const BUILDING_DOCKS: Dictionary = {

## Weighted Graph Adjacency List (`node_idx -> Array[{to: int, cost: float, bridge_id: int}]`)
var _adjacency: Dictionary = {}

func _ready() -> void:
	_build_graph_topology()

func _init() -> void:
	_build_graph_topology()

func _build_graph_topology() -> void:
	_adjacency.clear()
	for i in range(WAYPOINTS.size()):
		_adjacency[i] = []

	for i in range(WAYPOINTS.size() - 1):
		var a := WAYPOINTS[i]
		var b := WAYPOINTS[i + 1]
		var dist := absf(b.x - a.x)
		var bridge_id := 0
		if i >= 2 and i <= 5:
			bridge_id = 1 # Wooden Bridge 1 (Island 2 <-> Island 1)
		elif i >= 13 and i <= 15:
			bridge_id = 2 # Wooden Bridge 2 (Island 1 <-> Island 3)
		_adjacency[i].append({"to": i + 1, "cost": dist, "bridge_id": bridge_id})
		_adjacency[i + 1].append({"to": i, "cost": dist, "bridge_id": bridge_id})

func get_named_pos(key: String) -> Vector2:
	var idx: int = int(NAMED_WAYPOINTS.get(key, 10))
	return WAYPOINTS[clampi(idx, 0, WAYPOINTS.size() - 1)]

func get_dock_pos(dock_key: String) -> Vector2:
	if BUILDING_DOCKS.has(dock_key):
		var d: Dictionary = BUILDING_DOCKS[dock_key]
		var wp_idx := clampi(int(d.get("wp", 10)), 0, WAYPOINTS.size() - 1)
		var dx := float(d.get("dx", 0.0))
		return Vector2(WAYPOINTS[wp_idx].x + dx, GROUND_Y)
	return get_named_pos(dock_key)

func get_island_for_x(x_coord: float) -> int:
	if x_coord < 538.0:
		return 2
	elif x_coord > 1358.0:
		return 3
	return 1

func is_on_wooden_bridge(x_coord: float) -> bool:
	return (x_coord >= 486.0 and x_coord <= 594.0) or (x_coord >= 1082.0 and x_coord <= 1416.0)

func get_nearest_waypoint_index(pos: Vector2) -> int:
	var best_idx := 0
	var best_d := 1e9
	for i in range(WAYPOINTS.size()):
		var d := absf(pos.x - WAYPOINTS[i].x)
		if d < best_d:
			best_d = d
			best_idx = i
	return best_idx

## Dijkstra Shortest-Path Solver across the 3-Island & Dual-Bridge Graph
func find_shortest_node_path(start_idx: int, end_idx: int) -> Array[int]:
	var n := WAYPOINTS.size()
	var s := clampi(start_idx, 0, n - 1)
	var e := clampi(end_idx, 0, n - 1)
	if s == e:
		return [s]

	if _adjacency.is_empty():
		_build_graph_topology()

	var dist: Array[float] = []
	var prev: Array[int] = []
	var visited: Array[bool] = []
	for _i in range(n):
		dist.append(1e12)
		prev.append(-1)
		visited.append(false)
	dist[s] = 0.0

	for _step in range(n):
		var u := -1
		var best := 1e12
		for i in range(n):
			if not visited[i] and dist[i] < best:
				best = dist[i]
				u = i
		if u == -1 or u == e:
			break
		visited[u] = true
		var edges: Array = _adjacency.get(u, [])
		for edge in edges:
			var v := int(edge["to"])
			var w := float(edge["cost"])
			if not visited[v] and dist[u] + w < dist[v]:
				dist[v] = dist[u] + w
				prev[v] = u

	var node_seq: Array[int] = []
	var cur := e
	while cur != -1:
		node_seq.push_front(cur)
		if cur == s:
			break
		cur = prev[cur]
	if node_seq.is_empty():
		node_seq.append(e)
	return node_seq

func compute_path(from_pos: Vector2, to_pos: Vector2) -> Array[Vector2]:
	var start_pt := constrain_to_walkable_surface(from_pos)
	var final_pt := constrain_to_walkable_surface(to_pos)
	if absf(final_pt.x - start_pt.x) <= 1.5:
		return [final_pt]
	var min_x := minf(start_pt.x, final_pt.x) + 1.0
	var max_x := maxf(start_pt.x, final_pt.x) - 1.0
	var s_idx := get_nearest_waypoint_index(start_pt)
	var e_idx := get_nearest_waypoint_index(final_pt)
	var node_indices := find_shortest_node_path(s_idx, e_idx)
	var path: Array[Vector2] = []
	for idx in node_indices:
		var wp := WAYPOINTS[idx]
		# Strictly filter to intermediate waypoints between start_pt.x and final_pt.x to eliminate dock overshoot/backtracking
		if wp.x >= min_x and wp.x <= max_x:
			if path.is_empty() or absf(path[path.size() - 1].x - wp.x) > 1.0:
				path.append(wp)
	if path.is_empty() or absf(path[path.size() - 1].x - final_pt.x) > 1.0:
		path.append(final_pt)
	else:
		path[path.size() - 1] = final_pt
	return path

func compute_dock_to_dock_path(from_pos: Vector2, target_dock_key: String) -> Array[Vector2]:
	return compute_path(from_pos, get_dock_pos(target_dock_key))

func get_path_total_length(path: Array[Vector2]) -> float:
	if path.size() <= 1:
		return 0.0
	var total := 0.0
	for i in range(path.size() - 1):
		total += path[i].distance_to(path[i + 1])
	return total

func constrain_to_walkable_surface(pos: Vector2) -> Vector2:
	return Vector2(clampf(pos.x, 120.0, 1790.0), GROUND_Y)
